[package]
name = "unpack"
version = "2.0.0"

description = "Executable packer detection and unpacking, with a compatible Go SDK"
license = "MIT"
repository = "https://github.com/orca-zhang/unpack"

[lib]
path = "rust/lib.rs"
crate-type = ["rlib", "staticlib"]

[[bin]]
name = "unpack"
path = "rust/main.rs"

[dependencies]
base64 = "0.22"
memchr = "2"
crc32fast = "1"
flate2 = "1"

tempfile = "3"

[dev-dependencies]

[profile.release]
lto = "thin"
strip = true

`Makefile`:

```
.PHONY: build sdk test check fixtures benchmark charts

build:
	cargo build --release --locked --bin unpack

sdk:
	python3 scripts/build_go_sdk.py

test: build sdk
	cargo test --locked --all-targets
	go test -race ./...

check: sdk
	cargo fmt --all -- --check
	cargo clippy --locked --all-targets -- -D warnings
	go vet ./...
	python3 scripts/generate_charts.py --check
	python3 -m unittest discover -s scripts -p 'test_*.py'

fixtures:
	python3 scripts/generate_fixtures.py
	python3 scripts/generate_aplib_fixtures.py
	python3 scripts/generate_payload_fixtures.py
	python3 scripts/generate_version_fixtures.py

benchmark: sdk
	python3 scripts/benchmark.py

charts:
	cargo build --release --locked --bin unpack
	python3 scripts/generate_charts.py --engine target/release/unpack

```

`README.md`:

```md
![Unpack — see through every layer](docs/assets/hero.svg)

<p align="center">
  <strong>Executable packer analysis · Rust core · Go SDK</strong><br/>
  <a href="README.md">English</a> · <a href="README_zh.md">中文</a>
</p>

<p align="center">
  <a href="#features">Features</a> · <a href="#quick-start">Quick start</a> ·
  <a href="#formats">Formats</a> · <a href="#performance">Performance</a> ·
  <a href="#go-sdk">Go SDK</a> · <a href="docs/REFERENCE.md">Documentation</a>
</p>

Unpack detects executable packers, decodes compressed payloads and records each layer.
Use it through the CLI, Rust library or compatible Go SDK.

<a id="features"></a>

## Features

| Inspect | Decode | Trace | Integrate |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 22 built-in formats | 5 native decoders + UPX | Recursive layers and detection evidence | Rust / Go / CLI |

![Feature flow](docs/charts/workflow.svg)

<a id="quick-start"></a>

## Quick start

Requires Rust 1.85+. Run from the repository directory:

```sh
cargo install --path . --locked
unpack detect --json testdata/upx/pe32.packed
unpack unpack -o output.exe testdata/upx/pe32.packed
```

UPX unpacking also requires [official UPX](https://upx.github.io/). Analyzed executables are never run.

See the [CLI reference](docs/REFERENCE.md#cli) for more options and configuration.

### Demo

Detect the format → decode GZIP / XZ / UPX layers → verify the result.

![Real CLI demo: detection, three-layer unpacking and SHA-256 verification](docs/assets/demo.gif)

<a id="formats"></a>

## Supported formats

![Format support distribution](docs/charts/support.svg)

**Native decode** restores compressed content. **Static recovery** repairs PE entry points
and structure without guaranteeing runnable output. **Detect only** does not unpack.

| Format | Capability | Versions / main variants |
| --- | --- | --- |
| GZIP | Native decode | Single / concatenated members |
| ZLIB | Native decode | DEFLATE streams; no preset dictionaries |
| XZ | Native decode | Single / concatenated streams, x86 BCJ, delta |
| LZMA-Alone | Native decode | Known / unknown size; requires `--format LZMA` |
| AP32 | Native aPLib decode | AP32 containers, nested containers |
| UPX | Official backend | Verified **5.1.0**: PE32/64, ELF32/64/ARM64, x86-64 Mach-O, LZMA, x86 filter |
| ASPack | Static recovery | **2.1, 2.12, 2.42, OTHER** layouts |
| FSG | Static recovery | `FSG!` / `FSG2` signatures |
| WinUpack | Static recovery | `WinUpack` / `UPack` signatures |
| Petite | Static recovery | — |
| PESpin | Static recovery | — |
| Armadillo | Static recovery | — |
| Themida | Static recovery | — |
| PECompact | Static recovery | `PECompact` / `PEC2` / `PEC1` signatures |
| NSPack | Static recovery | — |
| MPRESS | Static recovery | PE32/64; `.MPRESS1` / `.MPRESS2` section layouts |
| VMProtect | Detect only | — |
| Enigma | Detect only | — |
| MEW | Detect only | `MEW11` / `MEW 11` signatures |
| PELock | Detect only | — |
| PEBundle | Detect only | — |
| Yoda | Detect only | Protector / Crypter |

“—” means the exact vendor release is unverified. Signatures and section names are
not vendor versions. ASPack 2.42/OTHER have synthetic layout coverage; real legacy
samples also verify conservative rejection of unsupported recovery. [Full version details →](docs/REFERENCE.md#versions)

<a id="performance"></a>

## Performance

![Detection latency, AP32 decoding and Go SDK allocations](docs/charts/overview.svg)

Local Apple M4 Pro microbenchmarks; excludes file I/O, process startup and end-to-end unpacking.
The allocation panel records the former RPC SDK; the current cgo SDK copies Rust output into Go once.
[Methodology, detailed charts and raw data →](docs/REPORT.md)

## Go SDK

Requires Go 1.21.5+, cgo, a C compiler and Rust at build time. Build the embedded Rust
static library once before compiling the SDK:

```sh
python3 scripts/build_go_sdk.py  # or: make sdk / go generate .
go build ./examples/go
```

The Go application contains the Rust core; no `unpack` executable is needed at runtime.
UPX unpacking still requires official UPX. [Build and platform details →](docs/REFERENCE.md#go-sdk)

```go
package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack"
    _ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers"
)

func main() {
    result, err := unpack.UnpackAll("packed.exe")
    if err != nil { panic(err) }
    for _, layer := range result.History {
        fmt.Println(layer.PackerName, layer.Support)
    }
    for _, warning := range result.Warnings { fmt.Println(warning) }
}
```

Legacy Go calls allow static recovery by default; the CLI does not. See the
[SDK reference](docs/REFERENCE.md#go-sdk) for strict mode, registration and other options.

For more examples, see the [Rust example](examples/basic.rs) and [Go example](examples/go/main.go).

## Documentation

- [Version details, CLI flags and SDK options](docs/REFERENCE.md)
- [Benchmark report](docs/REPORT.md)
- [Test fixtures and regeneration](testdata/README.md)

## Repository layout

| Path | Contents |
| --- | --- |
| [rust/](rust/) | Rust core and CLI |
| [drivers/](drivers/) | All 22 Go format drivers and their registration entry point |
| [native/](native/) | C header and generated Rust libraries for the Go SDK |
| [internal/](internal/) | Shared Go test helpers |
| [examples/](examples/) | Rust and Go usage examples |
| [tests/](tests/) | Rust integration tests; Go tests live beside their packages |
| [testdata/](testdata/) | Shared fixtures, manifests and provenance |
| [scripts/](scripts/) | Build, fixture, benchmark and chart tools |
| [docs/](docs/) | Reference docs, benchmark reports and images |

Go SDK sources and Cargo/Go manifests stay at the root for standard build commands.
Individual Go drivers use paths such as `github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/upx`.

## License

[MIT](LICENSE). Official UPX is a separate executable with its own license.

```

`README_zh.md`:

```md
![Unpack — 看清每一层](docs/assets/hero-zh.svg)

<p align="center">
  <strong>可执行文件壳分析 · Rust 核心 · Go SDK</strong><br/>
  <a href="README.md">English</a> · <a href="README_zh.md">中文</a>
</p>

<p align="center">
  <a href="#features">功能</a> · <a href="#quick-start">快速开始</a> ·
  <a href="#formats">支持格式</a> · <a href="#performance">性能</a> ·
  <a href="#go-sdk">Go SDK</a> · <a href="docs/REFERENCE_zh.md">详细文档</a>
</p>

Unpack 用于识别可执行文件的壳、解码压缩载荷，并逐层记录处理结果。提供命令行、Rust 库和兼容原接口的 Go SDK。

<a id="features"></a>

## 功能

| 识别 | 解码 | 追踪 | 集成 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 22 种内置格式 | 5 个原生解码器 + UPX | 多层处理与识别依据 | Rust / Go / CLI |

![功能流程](docs/charts/workflow-zh.svg)

<a id="quick-start"></a>

## 快速开始

需要 Rust 1.85+，在仓库目录内执行：

```sh
cargo install --path . --locked
unpack detect --json testdata/upx/pe32.packed
unpack unpack -o output.exe testdata/upx/pe32.packed
```

解包 UPX 文件还需安装[官方 UPX](https://upx.github.io/)。处理过程中不会执行待分析的程序。

更多参数和环境配置见[命令行说明](docs/REFERENCE_zh.md#cli)。

### 演示

识别格式 → 解开 GZIP / XZ / UPX 三层 → 校验结果。

![真实样本的识别、三层解包与 SHA-256 校验演示](docs/assets/demo-zh.gif)

<a id="formats"></a>

## 支持格式

![格式支持分布](docs/charts/support-zh.svg)

**原生解码**直接还原压缩内容；**静态恢复**仅修补 PE 入口和结构，不保证程序可运行；**仅识别**不提供解包。

| 格式 | 能力 | 版本 / 主要变体 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| GZIP | 原生解码 | 单成员、多成员 |
| ZLIB | 原生解码 | DEFLATE 流；不支持预置字典 |
| XZ | 原生解码 | 单流、多流、x86 BCJ、delta |
| LZMA-Alone | 原生解码 | 已知 / 未知长度；需 `--format LZMA` |
| AP32 | 原生 aPLib 解码 | AP32 容器、嵌套容器 |
| UPX | 官方后端解包 | 已验证 **5.1.0**：PE32/64、ELF32/64/ARM64、x86-64 Mach-O、LZMA、x86 filter |
| ASPack | 静态恢复 | **2.1、2.12、2.42、OTHER** 布局 |
| FSG | 静态恢复 | `FSG!` / `FSG2` 签名 |
| WinUpack | 静态恢复 | `WinUpack` / `UPack` 签名 |
| Petite | 静态恢复 | — |
| PESpin | 静态恢复 | — |
| Armadillo | 静态恢复 | — |
| Themida | 静态恢复 | — |
| PECompact | 静态恢复 | `PECompact` / `PEC2` / `PEC1` 签名 |
| NSPack | 静态恢复 | — |
| MPRESS | 静态恢复 | PE32/64；`.MPRESS1` / `.MPRESS2` 节布局 |
| VMProtect | 仅识别 | — |
| Enigma | 仅识别 | — |
| MEW | 仅识别 | `MEW11` / `MEW 11` 签名 |
| PELock | 仅识别 | — |
| PEBundle | 仅识别 | — |
| Yoda | 仅识别 | Protector / Crypter |

“—”表示未验证具体发行版；签名和节名不代表厂商版本。[完整版本明细 →](docs/REFERENCE_zh.md#versions)

ASPack 2.42/OTHER 目前仅有合成布局测试；真实旧壳样本还验证无法可靠恢复时会明确报错。

<a id="performance"></a>

## 性能

![识别耗时、AP32 解码与 Go SDK 分配量](docs/charts/overview-zh.svg)

数据来自 Apple M4 Pro 上的本机微基准，不含文件 I/O、进程启动和完整解包。
[测量方法、详细图表与原始数据 →](docs/REPORT.md)

分配量面板保留的是旧 RPC SDK 的历史数据；当前 cgo SDK 会将 Rust 输出复制到 Go 一次。

## Go SDK

构建需要 Go 1.21.5+、cgo、C 编译器和 Rust。先构建嵌入 SDK 的 Rust 静态库：

```sh
python3 scripts/build_go_sdk.py  # 或 make sdk / go generate .
go build ./examples/go
```

Rust 核心会编译进 Go 应用，运行时无需 `unpack` 程序。UPX 解包仍需官方 UPX。
[构建与平台说明 →](docs/REFERENCE_zh.md#go-sdk)

```go
package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack"
    _ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers"
)

func main() {
    result, err := unpack.UnpackAll("packed.exe")
    if err != nil { panic(err) }
    for _, layer := range result.History {
        fmt.Println(layer.PackerName, layer.Support)
    }
    for _, warning := range result.Warnings { fmt.Println(warning) }
}
```

旧 Go 接口默认允许静态恢复，CLI 默认关闭。严格模式、驱动注册和其他选项见 [SDK 文档](docs/REFERENCE_zh.md#go-sdk)。

Rust 库用法见 [Rust 示例](examples/basic.rs)，完整 Go 示例见 [examples/go](examples/go/main.go)。

## 更多文档

- [版本明细、命令行参数与 SDK 选项](docs/REFERENCE_zh.md)
- [性能报告](docs/REPORT.md)
- [测试样本与生成方法](testdata/README.md)

## 目录结构

| 路径 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |
| [rust/](rust/) | Rust 核心与命令行 |
| [drivers/](drivers/) | 全部 22 种 Go 格式驱动及统一注册入口 |
| [native/](native/) | Go SDK 使用的 C 头文件与生成的 Rust 静态库 |
| [internal/](internal/) | Go 测试共用工具 |
| [examples/](examples/) | Rust、Go 使用示例 |
| [tests/](tests/) | Rust 集成测试；Go 测试与对应包放在一起 |
| [testdata/](testdata/) | 统一存放测试样本、清单与来源说明 |
| [scripts/](scripts/) | 构建、样本生成、基准测试和图表工具 |
| [docs/](docs/) | 参考文档、性能报告与图片 |

Go SDK 源码及 Cargo、Go 配置保留在根目录，方便使用标准构建命令。
单独导入 Go 驱动时使用 `github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/upx` 这样的路径。

## 许可证

[MIT](LICENSE)。官方 UPX 作为独立程序使用，适用其自己的许可证。

```

`docs/REFERENCE.md`:

```md
# Technical reference

[English](REFERENCE.md) · [中文](REFERENCE_zh.md) · [README](../README.md)

<a id="versions"></a>

## Version and layout details

### ASPack layout versions

Offsets are relative to the PE entry-point stub. These labels describe recognized
layouts; they do not certify complete unpacking of every executable from that release.

| Layout label | Compatibility ID | Marker offset | Original-entry-point field | Legacy block table | Capability |
| --- | ---: | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2.1 | 1 | `0x4fd` | `0x4f4` | None | Entry-point / PE repair; overlapping RET bytes are not a block count |
| 2.12 | 2 | `0x3b9` | `0x748` | `0x57c` | PE repair + limited legacy block layout |
| OTHER (`0x41f`) | 3 | `0x41f` | `0x7a4` | `0x5d8` | PE repair + limited legacy block layout; vendor version unknown |
| 2.42 | 4 | `0x42b` | `0x7b0` | `0x5e4` | PE repair + limited legacy block layout |

Source: [Rust layout definitions](../rust/aspack.rs), [recovery implementation](../rust/recover.rs)
and [synthetic layout manifest](../testdata/pe-versions/manifest.json).
The legacy block decoder is not a complete native ASPack codec.

### UPX versions, architectures and methods

The checked-in fixtures use **official UPX 5.1.0**. Other UPX releases depend on the
installed backend and input; this repository does not establish a 1.x–5.x compatibility range.

| Fixture | Executable variant | Packing selection | Actual method / filter | Validation |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `pe32` | PE32 / x86 | `--best` | `2` / `0x16` | Official-decoder bytes + SHA-256 |
| `pe32-lzma` | PE32 / x86 | `--lzma` | `14` / `0x16` | Official-decoder bytes + SHA-256 |
| `pe32-filter` | PE32 / x86 | `--filter=0x16` | `2` / `0x16` | Official-decoder bytes + SHA-256 |
| `pe64` | PE32+ / x86-64 | `--best` | `2` / `0x00` | Official-decoder bytes + SHA-256 |
| `elf32` | ELF32 / x86 | `--best` | `2` / `0x00` | Official-decoder bytes + SHA-256 |
| `elf64` | ELF64 / x86-64 | `--best` | `2` / `0x00` | Official-decoder bytes + SHA-256 |
| `elf-arm64` | ELF64 / ARM64 | `--best` | `2` / `0x00` | Official-decoder bytes + SHA-256 |
| `macho64` | Thin Mach-O 64 / x86-64 | `--best --force-macos` | `2` / `0x49` | Official-decoder bytes + structure; experimental packing |

Source: [UPX fixture manifest](../testdata/upx/manifest.json).
Mach-O execution compatibility is unverified; ARM64 Mach-O is not a verified round trip.

### Compatibility IDs are not vendor versions

The detector checks section evidence before string evidence. The mappings below
apply when the listed evidence wins; a different earlier match can take priority.

| Family | Evidence → current `version` ID | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| UPX | `UPX0` / `UPX1` / `UPX!` → 1 | Legacy Go constants 1–4 do not prove four supported UPX releases |
| FSG | `FSG!` → 1; `FSG2` → 2 | Signature variants, not a verified 1.x / 2.x release range |
| WinUpack | `.upx0` / `.upx1` → 1; `WinUpack` → 1; `UPack` → 2 | `WinUpack!` also matches the earlier `WinUpack` signature |
| PECompact | Section → 1; `PECompact` → 1; `PEC2` → 2; `PEC1` → 3 | Signature ordering, not vendor-version ordering |
| MPRESS | `.MPRESS1` / `.MPRESS2` → 1; `MPRESS` string → 1 | Section suffixes 1 / 2 do not distinguish product releases |

### Executable container variants

| Container | Structural handling | Real UPX round-trip fixtures |
| --- | --- | --- |
| PE | PE32 (`0x10b`), PE32+ (`0x20b`) | x86 / x86-64 |
| ELF | ELF32 / ELF64, little / big endian header validation | Little-endian x86 / x86-64 / ARM64 |
| Mach-O | Thin 32 / 64-bit, universal FAT32 / FAT64, both byte orders | Thin x86-64 only |

Structural validation covers more variants than the real packed fixture corpus.
It is not proof of unpacking every CPU, container version or commercial protector.

## Detection and recovery

Detection uses PE sections and packer-specific signatures in a stable order.
Generic instructions such as `PUSHAD; CALL` are not sufficient evidence. In
particular, `.vmp*` identifies VMProtect rather than Themida. Detection is still
heuristic: renamed sections, modified stubs and unrelated strings can affect it.
`version` is a compatibility ID matching the Go constants, not a vendor version.

Static recovery restores a plausible entry point and repairs selected PE fields.
It does **not** implement general decompression, devirtualization, anti-debugging
bypass, or guarantee a runnable file. The existing ASPack sample is covered by
structure/entry-point tests, not a successful execution test. Rust and the CLI
require explicit recovery opt-in; the old Go methods keep recovery enabled for
compatibility and return `Warnings` on the aggregate result.

## Build and install

Requires Rust 1.85+; Go 1.21.5+ is needed only for the Go SDK. Install official
[UPX](https://upx.github.io/) for UPX unpacking (5.1.0 was used for the fixtures).
Detection, AP32, all four payload codecs and static recovery require no external decompressor. No analyzed executable is run.

```sh
cargo build --release --locked
# Or install the Rust executable on PATH:
cargo install --path . --locked
```

The Go SDK statically links the Rust library through cgo. It needs Rust, Python 3,
and a C compiler when building; deployment does not need the Rust CLI or a Rust
shared library. `UNPACK_UPX` optionally selects the official UPX executable.

<a id="cli"></a>

## Rust CLI

```sh
unpack detect testdata/upx/pe32.packed
unpack detect --json testdata/upx/elf64.packed
unpack unpack -o output.exe testdata/upx/pe32.packed
unpack unpack -o decoded.exe testdata/aplib/upx.ap32
unpack unpack -o decoded.exe testdata/payloads/gzip-xz-upx.packed
unpack unpack --format LZMA -o payload.bin testdata/payloads/text-lzma.packed
unpack unpack --depth 5 --max-size 268435456 input.exe
unpack unpack --recover -o recovered.exe testdata/aspack/aspack_test.exe
unpack formats
```

The default output is `<stem>_unpacked.<extension>`. Existing files are preserved
unless `--force` is provided; the input itself cannot be overwritten. Output is
written to a temporary file in the destination directory before publication.
Default limits are 10 layers, 256 MiB per input/output and 30 seconds per UPX
process. `--depth 0` removes the configured depth limit, with an absolute cap of
1024 layers and cycle detection. Intermediate layers remain in memory; UPX alone
needs private temporary input/output files, removed on normal completion/error.
UPX's own process memory is not constrained by the output file limit.
XZ/LZMA have a separate fixed 64 MiB decoder dictionary/model memory budget.
LZMA-Alone has no reliable magic and is never guessed automatically. Explicit
format selection decodes one layer; recursive mode validates recognized inner
containers/executables. ZLIB preset-dictionary streams are reported as unsupported.
LZMA-Alone has no checksum; strict completion and declared output size are checked.

The x86-64 Mach-O fixture uses upstream UPX's experimental `--force-macos` packing
option. Tests verify decoded bytes/structure, not packed-program execution on macOS.
ARM64 Mach-O is not listed as a verified round-trip case.

## Go SDK

Existing built-in imports and calls are retained. Build the static library before
compiling Go, from a writable clone:

```sh
python3 scripts/build_go_sdk.py  # or go generate .
go build ./examples/go
```

For another Go application, use the clone containing the generated archive:

```sh
go mod edit -replace github.com/orca-zhang/unpack=/absolute/path/to/unpack
```

Configured SDK build targets: macOS amd64/arm64, Linux GNU amd64/arm64, Windows amd64
with MinGW GCC and Rust target `x86_64-pc-windows-gnu`. The script reads `GOOS` and
`GOARCH`; cross compilation also needs the matching `rustup target add` and C
cross compiler. Set `CGO_ENABLED=1`. Native archives are generated and ignored by Git;
`go get` alone cannot build the SDK. Rebuild the archive after changing Rust; the
script clears Go's build cache so the linker uses the new archive. The Go CLI and
custom Go unpacker implementations have been removed.

```go
package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack"
    _ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers"
)

func main() {
    result, err := unpack.UnpackAll("packed.exe")
    if err != nil { panic(err) }
    for _, layer := range result.History {
        fmt.Println(layer.PackerName, layer.Version, layer.Support)
    }
    for _, warning := range result.Warnings { fmt.Println(warning) }
    // result.ReaderAt owns an in-memory result; it needs no Close.
}
```

Import individual drivers (for example `_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/upx"`) to
register only those formats. Import `drivers` to register all 22, or import
`github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/ap32` for native AP32 support.
Individual driver packages now live under `drivers/`; update earlier imports by
adding `/drivers` before the format name. The root SDK and aggregate `drivers`
import paths are unchanged. `Inspect(path)`
reports detection evidence and errors for all built-ins regardless of registration;
legacy `DetectFormat` keeps its no-match-on-error signature and uses registered
formats. `RegisterFormat` selects Rust-backed adapters implementing `RustFormat()`;
legacy custom Go detector/unpacker callbacks return `unsupported` and are never executed.

For explicit limits, cancellation and strict unpacking:

```go
result, err := unpack.UnpackWithOptions(ctx, path, unpack.Options{
    MaxDepth: 10,
    MaxSize: 256 * 1024 * 1024,
    AllowRecovery: false,
})
```

Rust errors are exposed as `*unpack.Error` with codes `invalid_input`, `unsupported`,
`limit`, `backend`, `io`, `cancelled` or `internal`. The C ABI owns metadata/output
buffers until Go copies the output once and frees the Rust result. It catches Rust
panics at the C boundary. There is no CLI discovery, subprocess or base64 transport
in the SDK. The CLI retains RPC protocols 1/2 for existing external clients.
Go adds a two-minute request timeout. Context cancellation returns promptly and
signals Rust; UPX is killed and reaped. Native codec cancellation is cooperative
at layer boundaries, so a running codec can finish after the Go call returns.

For explicit one-layer LZMA decoding without registration:

```go
result, err := unpack.UnpackWithOptions(ctx, path, unpack.Options{Format: "LZMA"})
```

Named `gzip`, `zlib`, `xz` and `lzma` Go packages also expose the standard driver API.

## Rust library

```rust
use unpack::{Options, unpack_path};

fn main() -> unpack::Result<()> {
    let result = unpack_path("packed.exe", &Options::default())?;
    for layer in result.history { println!("{}", layer.packer_name); }
    Ok(())
}
```

`detect(&[u8])`, `detect_format`, `unpack(&[u8], &Options)`, `unpack_path` and
`unpack_format` accept data or paths without requiring the Go SDK. Named-format
unpacking performs exactly one layer, matching individual Go driver methods.
Recovery stops after one repair because packer section names may remain in the
result; that is not evidence of another layer.

`unpack_owned(Vec<u8>, &Options)` and `unpack_format_owned` consume an existing
buffer without the initial whole-file clone. The CLI and `unpack_path` use this
path. `aplib::decompress(raw, max_size)` decodes raw aPLib data; automatic detection
requires an AP32 header because a raw stream has no reliable magic. AP32 size and
packed/unpacked CRCs are checked; malformed offsets, missing end markers and
over-limit output are errors. AP32 support does not imply full support for PE
packers that happen to use aPLib internally.
`compression::unpack(data, format, max_size)` directly decodes one GZIP/ZLIB/XZ/LZMA
payload, including arbitrary data. Mach-O validation covers thin/universal 32/64-bit
structures in both byte orders; real UPX samples currently cover x86-64 thin Mach-O.

Runnable SDK examples:

```sh
cargo run --example basic -- testdata/upx/pe32.packed
go run ./examples/go testdata/upx/elf64.packed
```

## Tests and fixtures

```sh
cargo build --locked
cargo test --locked --all-targets
cargo clippy --locked --all-targets -- -D warnings
python3 scripts/build_go_sdk.py
go test -race ./...
go vet ./...
```

Eight real UPX variants cover PE32, PE64, ELF32/x86-64/ARM64, x86-64 Mach-O,
LZMA and an x86 filter. Twenty independent payload fixtures cover empty streams,
concatenation, XZ BCJ/delta/SHA-256, known-size LZMA and GZIP -> XZ -> UPX chains.
Tests check official-decoder output and SHA-256 manifests, not just file length or
a changed entry point. Five additional AP32/raw aPLib vectors cover token types,
offset boundaries, nested containers and a real AP32 -> UPX chain. They were
checked using an independent decoder; see the fixture provenance. Synthetic PE
fixtures cover all 17 PE packer detectors, legacy static
recovery, unsupported formats, malformed headers, limits, SDK registration,
embedded native calls, cancellation and rejected Go plugin callbacks. Synthetic stubs are not evidence that a real
commercial protector can be unpacked. These tests do not skip missing fixtures.
See [testdata/README.md](../testdata/README.md) for provenance and regeneration.

The version matrix adds **105 synthetic cases**, including **81 ASPack cases** for
2.1/2.12/OTHER/2.42 layouts. They cover shifted entry points, reordered sections,
imports, truncated/ambiguous markers, file-backed bounds and legacy block streams.
**16 real RetDec samples** add ASPack 2.12, FSG 1.33 and MPRESS 1.05/1.27/2.05/2.19
(code before/after/between data; LZMA/LZMAT). These must be recognized and reject
unverifiable recovery. A reference original proves a jump into compressed MPRESS
storage is not the original entry point. ASPack 2.42/OTHER currently have synthetic
layout coverage only. See the fixture manifest for source commits, licenses and hashes.

CI builds and tests native SDK linkage on Linux, macOS and Windows. `CGO_ENABLED=0`
can compile the SDK's error stub, but all operations require cgo and the archive.

## Rebuild the charts

Regenerate the terminal demo with `python3 scripts/generate_demo.py` after building
the release CLI. It requires Pillow, official UPX and `shasum` or `sha256sum`.
The script captures real output and verifies the decoded fixture byte for byte;
playback timing is adjusted for readability.

All seven charts have English and Chinese versions, share a vector style and are generated from checked-in measurements,
fixture sizes and live format metadata. [The report](REPORT.md) includes the
detailed detection, CRC/AP32, SDK allocation and fixture-size charts. RPC allocation measurements are historical.

```sh
make benchmark  # Re-measure the current implementation; preserves historical baseline
make charts     # Regenerate SVGs from measurements and live format metadata
python3 scripts/generate_charts.py --check
```

CI checks chart freshness without rerunning machine-dependent benchmarks.
The [logo](assets/logo.svg) and [header artwork](assets/hero.svg) are standalone,
editable SVGs; chart generation uses only the Python standard library.

```

`docs/REFERENCE_zh.md`:

```md
# 技术参考

[English](REFERENCE.md) · [中文](REFERENCE_zh.md) · [README](../README_zh.md)

<a id="versions"></a>

## 版本与布局明细

### ASPack 布局版本

以下偏移均相对 PE 入口桩。版本标签表示可识别的布局，不表示该发行版产生的所有程序都能完整脱壳。

| 布局标签 | 兼容 ID | 标记偏移 | 原始入口点字段 | 旧式块表 | 能力 |
| --- | ---: | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2.1 | 1 | `0x4fd` | `0x4f4` | 无 | 入口点 / PE 修补；重叠的 RET 字节不作为块计数 |
| 2.12 | 2 | `0x3b9` | `0x748` | `0x57c` | PE 修补 + 有限旧式块布局 |
| OTHER（`0x41f`） | 3 | `0x41f` | `0x7a4` | `0x5d8` | PE 修补 + 有限旧式块布局；厂商版本未知 |
| 2.42 | 4 | `0x42b` | `0x7b0` | `0x5e4` | PE 修补 + 有限旧式块布局 |

依据：[Rust 布局定义](../rust/aspack.rs)、[恢复实现](../rust/recover.rs)、
[合成布局清单](../testdata/pe-versions/manifest.json)。旧式块解码器不是完整原生 ASPack 编解码器。

### UPX 版本、架构与方法

仓库样本使用**官方 UPX 5.1.0**。其他版本的兼容性取决于安装的后端和输入；
当前证据不足以声称完整覆盖 UPX 1.x～5.x。

| 样本 | 可执行文件变体 | 加壳选项 | 实际方法 / 过滤器 | 验证方式 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `pe32` | PE32 / x86 | `--best` | `2` / `0x16` | 官方解码结果逐字节比较 + SHA-256 |
| `pe32-lzma` | PE32 / x86 | `--lzma` | `14` / `0x16` | 官方解码结果逐字节比较 + SHA-256 |
| `pe32-filter` | PE32 / x86 | `--filter=0x16` | `2` / `0x16` | 官方解码结果逐字节比较 + SHA-256 |
| `pe64` | PE32+ / x86-64 | `--best` | `2` / `0x00` | 官方解码结果逐字节比较 + SHA-256 |
| `elf32` | ELF32 / x86 | `--best` | `2` / `0x00` | 官方解码结果逐字节比较 + SHA-256 |
| `elf64` | ELF64 / x86-64 | `--best` | `2` / `0x00` | 官方解码结果逐字节比较 + SHA-256 |
| `elf-arm64` | ELF64 / ARM64 | `--best` | `2` / `0x00` | 官方解码结果逐字节比较 + SHA-256 |
| `macho64` | 薄 Mach-O 64 / x86-64 | `--best --force-macos` | `2` / `0x49` | 官方解码字节 + 结构；实验性加壳 |

依据：[UPX 样本清单](../testdata/upx/manifest.json)。Mach-O 未验证执行兼容性；ARM64 Mach-O 未列为已验证往返支持。

### 兼容编号不等于厂商版本

识别时先检查节名，再检查字符串。以下映射适用于对应证据胜出的情况；更早命中的证据可能优先。

| 格式 | 识别依据 → 当前 `version` ID | 解读 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| UPX | `UPX0` / `UPX1` / `UPX!` → 1 | 旧 Go 常量 1～4 不代表验证过四个 UPX 发行版 |
| FSG | `FSG!` → 1；`FSG2` → 2 | 签名变体，不是已验证的 1.x / 2.x 发行版范围 |
| WinUpack | `.upx0` / `.upx1` → 1；`WinUpack` → 1；`UPack` → 2 | `WinUpack!` 也会先命中 `WinUpack` |
| PECompact | 节名 → 1；`PECompact` → 1；`PEC2` → 2；`PEC1` → 3 | 按签名顺序编号，不按厂商版本排序 |
| MPRESS | `.MPRESS1` / `.MPRESS2` → 1；`MPRESS` 字符串 → 1 | 节名中的 1 / 2 不能用来区分产品发行版 |

### 可执行文件容器变体

| 容器 | 结构处理范围 | 真实 UPX 往返样本 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| PE | PE32（`0x10b`）、PE32+（`0x20b`） | x86 / x86-64 |
| ELF | ELF32 / ELF64，大端 / 小端头部校验 | 小端 x86 / x86-64 / ARM64 |
| Mach-O | 薄文件 32 / 64 位、通用 FAT32 / FAT64、两种字节序 | 仅薄文件 x86-64 |

结构校验覆盖范围大于真实加壳样本范围，不能据此推断所有 CPU、容器版本或商业保护壳都能解包。

## 识别与恢复说明

静态恢复会尝试还原入口点、修补部分 PE 字段，不等于通用解压、去虚拟化或保证程序可运行。
旧 ASPack 样例只验证 PE 结构和入口点，没有执行成功的证明。Rust API 和 CLI 默认关闭
静态恢复，需显式启用；原 Go 解包方法为兼容保留恢复行为，并在聚合结果的 `Warnings`
中说明。单驱动 `Unpack` 的返回类型不支持提示，应结合 `Inspect` 的能力字段判断。

识别使用节名和格式签名，并采用稳定顺序；不再只凭 `PUSHAD; CALL` 等通用指令判定壳。
`.vmp*` 归为 VMProtect。重命名节、修改桩代码等仍可能影响识别。
`version` 是兼容旧 Go 常量的编号，不表示厂商的实际版本。

<a id="build-and-install"></a>

## 安装与构建

需要 Rust 1.85+；使用 Go SDK 时需要 Go 1.21.5+。UPX 解包还需要安装
[官方 UPX](https://upx.github.io/)，样例使用 5.1.0 生成。识别、AP32 解码和静态恢复不依赖 UPX。
GZIP、ZLIB、XZ、LZMA 载荷解码也不需要外部解压程序。工具不会执行待分析的程序。

```sh
cargo build --release --locked
# 或将 Rust 程序安装到 PATH：
cargo install --path . --locked
```

Go SDK 通过 cgo 静态链接 Rust 核心。构建时需要 Rust、Python 3 和 C 编译器，部署时无需
Rust CLI 或 Rust 动态库。`UNPACK_UPX` 可指定官方 UPX 程序路径。

<a id="cli"></a>

## 命令行

```sh
unpack detect --json testdata/upx/pe32.packed
unpack unpack -o output.exe testdata/upx/pe32.packed
unpack unpack -o decoded.exe testdata/aplib/upx.ap32
unpack unpack -o decoded.exe testdata/payloads/gzip-xz-upx.packed
unpack unpack --format LZMA -o payload.bin testdata/payloads/text-lzma.packed
unpack unpack --depth 5 --max-size 268435456 input.exe
unpack unpack --recover -o recovered.exe testdata/aspack/aspack_test.exe
unpack formats
```

默认输出为 `<文件名>_unpacked.<扩展名>`。默认保留已有文件，只有 `--force` 才会替换输出，
即使指定 `--force` 也不能覆盖输入。输出完成后再发布到目标路径。

默认最多 10 层，单层输入/输出最大 256 MiB，每次 UPX 调用最多 30 秒。
`--depth 0` 取消配置的层数限制，仍有 1024 层硬限制和循环检查。
内置多层处理在内存中传递数据，官方 UPX 调用使用临时目录并在正常结束或报错时清理。
输出大小限制不等于对 UPX 进程本身内存的限制。
XZ/LZMA 的字典与模型另有固定 64 MiB 内存上限。LZMA-Alone 没有可靠魔数，默认不猜测识别。
指定格式时只处理一层；自动模式会继续校验已识别的内层可执行文件或容器。
需要外部预置字典的 ZLIB 流会返回不支持。LZMA-Alone 本身没有校验和，工具会检查严格结束条件和声明长度。

Mach-O 样本生成使用上游 UPX 的实验选项 `--force-macos`；测试验证解包字节和结构，
不证明加壳程序在 macOS 上可运行。ARM64 Mach-O 暂不列为已验证往返支持。

## Go SDK

内置驱动保留原调用方式。

先在可写的仓库副本中构建静态库，再编译 Go：

```sh
python3 scripts/build_go_sdk.py  # 或 go generate .
go build ./examples/go
```

在其他 Go 项目中，使用已生成静态库的仓库副本：

```sh
go mod edit -replace github.com/orca-zhang/unpack=/absolute/path/to/unpack
```

构建目标包括 macOS amd64/arm64、Linux GNU amd64/arm64、Windows amd64。
Windows 使用 MinGW GCC 和 `x86_64-pc-windows-gnu` Rust 目标。脚本读取 `GOOS`/`GOARCH`；
交叉编译还需安装相应 Rust target 和 C 交叉编译器，并启用 `CGO_ENABLED=1`。
静态库由脚本生成且不提交 Git，单独 `go get` 不足以完成构建。Rust 修改后需重建静态库；
脚本会清理 Go 编译缓存，确保链接新库。Go 命令行与自定义 Go 解包实现已移除。

```go
package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack"
    _ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers"
)

func main() {
    result, err := unpack.UnpackAll("packed.exe")
    if err != nil { panic(err) }
    for _, layer := range result.History {
        fmt.Println(layer.PackerName, layer.Version, layer.Support)
    }
    for _, warning := range result.Warnings { fmt.Println(warning) }
    // result.ReaderAt 为内存结果，不需要 Close。
}
```

导入 `drivers` 注册全部 22 种格式，也可以只导入 `_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/upx"`，
或导入 `github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/ap32` 单独注册原生 AP32 解码。
各格式驱动现统一放在 `drivers/` 下，旧导入路径需在格式名前加上 `/drivers`。
SDK 主包及聚合 `drivers` 的导入路径保持不变。
`DetectFormat` 仅检查已注册格式，保留出错时返回未识别的旧签名。
新增 `Inspect(path)` 能返回错误、识别依据和能力字段，不受驱动注册范围限制。
`RegisterFormat` 只选择实现 `RustFormat()` 的 Rust SDK 适配器；旧 Go 检测/解包回调会返回 `unsupported`，不会执行。

需要上下文取消、明确限制和严格模式时：

```go
result, err := unpack.UnpackWithOptions(ctx, path, unpack.Options{
    MaxDepth: 10,
    MaxSize: 256 * 1024 * 1024,
    AllowRecovery: false,
})
```

Rust 业务错误为 `*unpack.Error`，错误码包括 `invalid_input`、`unsupported`、`limit`、
`backend`、`io`、`cancelled`、`internal`。C ABI 持有元数据和结果，Go 复制结果一次后释放 Rust
内存，Rust panic 在 ABI 边界捕获。SDK 不再查找 CLI、启动 Rust 子进程或通过 base64 传输。
CLI 仍保留 RPC 协议 1/2，供其他现有客户端使用。
Go 请求有两分钟超时；取消上下文会及时返回，并通知 Rust 终止、回收 UPX 进程。
原生编解码器在层边界检查取消，已开始的单层解码可能在 Go 返回后继续完成。

无需注册驱动即可显式解码一层 LZMA-Alone：

```go
result, err := unpack.UnpackWithOptions(ctx, path, unpack.Options{Format: "LZMA"})
```

也可分别导入 `gzip`、`zlib`、`xz`、`lzma` 包，使用与原驱动相同的接口。

## Rust 库

```rust
use unpack::{Options, unpack_path};

fn main() -> unpack::Result<()> {
    let result = unpack_path("packed.exe", &Options::default())?;
    for layer in result.history { println!("{}", layer.packer_name); }
    Ok(())
}
```

也可使用 `detect(&[u8])`、`detect_format`、`unpack(&[u8], &Options)`、`unpack_format`。
指定单个格式时只处理一层，对应原 Go 单驱动接口。
静态恢复后可能仍保留壳节名，因此恢复后停止继续识别，避免把相同标记当成新的壳层。

新增 `unpack_owned(Vec<u8>, &Options)`、`unpack_format_owned`，直接消费已有缓冲区，
避免初始整文件复制，CLI 和 `unpack_path` 已采用此路径。
`aplib::decompress(raw, max_size)` 可解码原始 aPLib 数据；自动识别只接受有明确头部的 AP32。
AP32 会校验声明长度、压缩数据 CRC 和输出 CRC，非法回溯、缺失结束标记、超限输出会报错。
支持 AP32 不等于已经完整实现内部使用 aPLib 的各种 PE 壳。
`compression::unpack(data, format, max_size)` 可直接解码单个 GZIP/ZLIB/XZ/LZMA 载荷。
Mach-O 校验覆盖薄文件、通用容器、32/64 位及大小端；真实 UPX 往返样本目前覆盖 x86-64 薄文件。

可直接运行 SDK 示例：

```sh
cargo run --example basic -- testdata/upx/pe32.packed
go run ./examples/go testdata/upx/elf64.packed
```

## 验证与样例

```sh
cargo build --locked
cargo test --locked --all-targets
cargo clippy --locked --all-targets -- -D warnings
python3 scripts/build_go_sdk.py
go test -race ./...
go vet ./...
```

仓库包含由无害 C 源码生成的真实 UPX PE32、PE64、ELF64、LZMA、x86 filter 样本，
保存官方解包结果和 SHA-256 清单；测试逐字节比较，不只检查长度或入口点变化。
UPX 样本已增至 8 个变体，新增 ELF32、ARM64 ELF、x86-64 Mach-O。
另有 20 个独立载荷案例，覆盖空流、多成员、XZ BCJ/delta/SHA-256、已知长度 LZMA，
以及 GZIP → XZ → UPX 等真实数据链。
新增 5 组经独立解码器核对的 AP32/raw aPLib 向量，覆盖各类编码、偏移边界、嵌套容器，
以及真实的 AP32 → UPX 数据链。
合成 PE 桩覆盖全部格式识别、旧驱动静态恢复、畸形输入、资源限制、Go 注册机制、
静态库调用、上下文取消，以及拒绝执行旧 Go 回调。合成样例不能证明真实商业壳能够完整脱壳。
测试不再因为这些样本缺失而跳过。来源与重建方法见 [testdata/README.md](../testdata/README.md)。

版本矩阵增加 **105 个合成用例**，其中 ASPack 四种布局共 **81 例**，覆盖入口偏移、节顺序、
导入表、截断/歧义标记、文件映射边界和旧块流。另有 **16 个真实 RetDec 样本**：
ASPack 2.12、FSG 1.33、MPRESS 1.05/1.27/2.05/2.19，覆盖代码/数据排列与 LZMA/LZMAT 差异。
真实样本要求正确识别并拒绝无法验证的恢复；上游原始文件用于证明跳到压缩节不是原始入口。
ASPack 2.42/OTHER 目前只有合成布局覆盖。来源提交、许可和哈希见样本清单。

CI 配置覆盖 Linux、macOS、Windows 的静态库链接与测试。`CGO_ENABLED=0` 可编译错误占位实现，
实际 SDK 操作需要 cgo 和 Rust 静态库。

## 重建图表

构建 release CLI 后，用 `python3 scripts/generate_demo.py` 重建终端演示。
需要 Pillow、官方 UPX，以及 `shasum` 或 `sha256sum`。
脚本采集真实输出，并逐字节核对解包结果；播放节奏为便于阅读作了调整。

7 张图表均提供中英文版本，采用统一的矢量样式，依据仓库测量数据、样本大小和实时格式元数据生成。
[实现与性能报告](REPORT.md) 包含识别、CRC/AP32、SDK 分配和样本大小的详细图表。

```sh
make benchmark  # 重测当前实现，保留历史基线
make charts     # 根据测量数据和实际格式列表重建 SVG
python3 scripts/generate_charts.py --check
```

CI 只检查图表与数据是否一致，不重复运行依赖机器性能的基准。
[Logo](assets/logo.svg) 和[头图](assets/hero.svg) 均为独立、可编辑的 SVG；
图表生成只使用 Python 标准库。

```

`docs/REPORT.md`:

```md
<p><img src="assets/logo.svg" width="64" height="64" alt="Unpack Logo"/></p>

# 实现与性能图表

[项目首页](../README_zh.md) · [完整格式与版本表](../README_zh.md#formats) · [English](../README.md)

当前实现新增 GZIP、ZLIB、XZ、LZMA-Alone 载荷解码，扩展 UPX 的 ELF32、ARM64 ELF、
x86-64 Mach-O 样本，并优化 AP32 校验和。Go 仅保留 SDK，通过 cgo 静态链接 Rust 核心。
图表来自实际格式表、样本文件和本机测量，原始数据保存在本目录。
性能对比的柱长采用对数缩放，数值与倍率保留实测值；格式分布和样本大小仍按比例绘制。

![关键指标总览](charts/overview-zh.svg)

| 图表 | 读图重点 | 数据来源 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [功能流程](charts/workflow-zh.svg) | 原生解码、外部 UPX、静态恢复、仅识别的区别 | [实际格式列表](formats.json)与 Rust 处理路径 |
| [支持分布](charts/support-zh.svg) | 6 条解码路径、10 种恢复、6 种仅识别 | [formats.json](formats.json) |
| [样本大小](charts/upx-fixtures-zh.svg) | 8 个真实 UPX 变体；不作为通用压缩率结论 | [UPX 清单](../testdata/upx/manifest.json)与样本文件 |
| [AP32 / CRC 耗时](charts/ap32-decode-zh.svg) | 对数柱长，标注为真实数值，越低越好 | [优化前](benchmarks/codecs-before.json) / [优化后](benchmarks/codecs-after.json) |
| [识别耗时](charts/native-detection-zh.svg) | 同时展示未命中路径改善和短路径的轻微回退 | [基线](benchmarks/baseline.json) / [优化后](benchmarks/optimized.json) |
| [SDK 分配量](charts/sdk-allocations-zh.svg) | 旧 RPC 架构的历史分配数据，不代表当前 SDK | [rpc.txt](benchmarks/rpc.txt) |

## 实际能力

![功能与处理路径](charts/workflow-zh.svg)

![格式支持范围](charts/support-zh.svg)

22 种内置格式中，AP32、GZIP、ZLIB、XZ、LZMA-Alone 由 Rust 原生解码，UPX 使用官方外部程序。
10 种旧壳仅做有限静态 PE 恢复，另有 6 种只识别。载荷编解码能力与完整 PE 壳恢复是不同的能力：
新增 LZMA 或 aPLib 解码，不等于已经完整实现所有使用这些算法的保护壳。

ASPack 按 2.1、2.12、2.42、OTHER（`0x41f`）四种布局区分恢复字段。
FSG、WinUpack、PECompact 的 `version` 是兼容签名编号，MPRESS 的节名后缀也不是厂商版本。
详细布局偏移、UPX 5.1.0 的 8 个变体，以及 PE / ELF / Mach-O 结构范围见[版本明细](REFERENCE_zh.md#versions)。

LZMA-Alone 没有可靠魔数，需 `--format LZMA`、Go `Options{Format: "LZMA"}` 或命名驱动；
显式选择只处理一层。XZ/LZMA 字典与模型有独立的 64 MiB 内存上限，输出还受 `max_size` 限制。
GZIP、ZLIB、XZ 校验其格式中的完整性字段；LZMA-Alone 本身没有校验和，检查声明长度与严格结束状态。
需要外部预置字典的 ZLIB 暂不支持。

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant App as Go 应用
    participant SDK as Go SDK
    participant Core as Rust 核心
    participant UPX as 官方 UPX
    App->>SDK: UnpackAll(gzip-xz-upx.packed)
    SDK->>Core: cgo 调用静态链接的 Rust 核心
    Core->>Core: 原生 GZIP 解码与校验
    Core->>Core: 原生 XZ 解码与校验
    Core->>UPX: 私有临时文件中的 UPX 数据
    UPX-->>Core: 解包后的 PE
    Core-->>SDK: 元数据与 Rust 持有的输出
    SDK->>SDK: 复制结果到 Go，释放 Rust 缓冲区
    SDK-->>App: ReaderAt 与层级历史
```

SDK 的文件读取、检测、递归、解码和恢复都在 Rust 中完成，结果复制到 Go 一次。
Go 自定义解包实现和 Go CLI 已移除，内置驱动保留为 SDK 适配器。
运行时无需 Rust 可执行程序；UPX 解包仍使用官方外部程序。

## 样本与实际覆盖

![UPX 样本大小](charts/upx-fixtures-zh.svg)

8 个 UPX 变体来自无害的 `testdata/src/tiny.c`，覆盖 PE32/64、ELF32/x86-64/ARM64、
x86-64 Mach-O、LZMA 方法与 x86 filter `0x16`。生成器检查 UPX 实际选择的方法/过滤器，
并保留官方解码结果。UPX 可能规范化文件头，完整文件未必与加壳前字节相同。

Mach-O 生成使用上游实验选项 `--force-macos`，只验证解包字节和结构，不证明加壳程序能在 macOS 运行。
ARM64 Mach-O 候选被上游解码器拒绝，因此未列为已验证往返支持。通用 Mach-O、大小端及 32/64 位
结构由合成测试覆盖，不作为真实通用程序脱壳的证明。

另有 5 组经独立解码器核对的 AP32/raw aPLib 向量，以及 20 个 Python 标准编解码器生成并往返校验的
载荷案例，包含空流、多成员、多流、XZ BCJ/delta/SHA-256、已知长度 LZMA、容器到 UPX 的真实数据链。
测试会拒绝畸形输入、截断、尾随数据、错误校验和、非法字典大小和过量输出。所有测试只解码，不运行样本。

PE 版本矩阵新增 105 个合成用例（ASPack 四种布局 81 例），覆盖偏移、节顺序、导入表、
截断、歧义和块边界；另有来自 MIT 许可 RetDec 语料的 16 个真实样本，覆盖 ASPack 2.12、
FSG 1.33、MPRESS 1.05/1.27/2.05/2.19 与 LZMA/LZMAT。真实样本用于验证识别及拒绝
不可靠恢复：MPRESS 桩内跳转和压缩节内目标不得作为原始入口点。
ASPack 2.42/OTHER 目前只有合成布局覆盖。静态恢复不等于完整解压。

样本较小且数据重复度高，压缩比不能外推到真实软件。AP32 测试编码器主要发出 literal，
不参与压缩比比较。来源与重建方法见 [样本说明](../testdata/README.md)。

## AP32 解码优化

![AP32 校验与解码耗时](charts/ap32-decode-zh.svg)

用 `crc32fast` 替代逐位循环，校验多项式与结果不变。以下数据来自 Apple M4 Pro、release 构建，
同一 65 KiB 输入重复 5 批，每批 200 次；先计算每批平均耗时再取中位数。

| 路径 | 优化前 | 优化后 | 本机微基准比值 |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| CRC32 校验 | 393.21 μs | 0.95 μs | 约 412.7 倍 |
| AP32：两个 CRC + 解码 | 872.39 μs | 50.43 μs | 约 17.3 倍 |

原始记录：[codecs-before.json](benchmarks/codecs-before.json)、[codecs-after.json](benchmarks/codecs-after.json)。
计时包含相应函数内的校验、分配与解码，不包含文件读取、管道、进程启动和其他格式。
热缓存固定样本的结果不是全库、所有输入或端到端加速保证。

## 检测优化

![原生识别耗时](charts/native-detection-zh.svg)

每个结果取 5 批、每批 1,000 次的平均耗时中位数，计时不包含编译和读取文件。

| 路径 | 历史基线 | 当前记录 | 说明 |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| 普通 PE，所有壳签名均未命中 | 4.32 ms | 51.00 μs | 快速字符串搜索减少无匹配扫描开销 |
| UPX PE，节名直接命中 | 159.54 ns | 168.75 ns | 短路径的绝对差异很小，新增格式判断带来少量开销 |

原始数据：[baseline.json](benchmarks/baseline.json)、[optimized.json](benchmarks/optimized.json)。
机器、命令和输入哈希见 [environment.json](benchmarks/environment.json)。历史基线是在替换签名搜索前
采集的快照，普通重测不覆盖它，不应拿另一台机器的当前测量直接比较。柱长使用对数缩放，数值与比值仍按原始测量值计算。

## Go SDK 静态链接与历史测量

SDK 通过 cgo 链接 `libunpack.a`。Rust 请求分发代码与 CLI 共用，结果由 Rust 持有，
Go 复制一次后释放；运行时不查找 Rust CLI，也不启动 Rust 子进程。
构建需要 Rust、C 编译器、cgo 和 `python3 scripts/build_go_sdk.py`，平台说明见
[SDK 文档](REFERENCE_zh.md#go-sdk)。上下文取消会通知 Rust；UPX 被终止、回收，
原生编解码器在层边界检查取消。

![旧 RPC SDK 历史分配](charts/sdk-allocations-zh.svg)

下表与图表保留旧 RPC 协议 1/2 的历史比较；**不代表当前静态链接 SDK 的内存或速度**。
它只测量“已有响应缓冲区 → 解码结果”，不含接收缓冲区、文件 I/O、启动、管道或 Rust 解包。

| Payload | 旧 base64 解码分配 | 旧二进制帧解码分配 |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| 64 KiB | 74,056 B/op | 336 B/op |
| 4 MiB | 约 4.01 MiB/op | 336 B/op |

历史记录：[rpc.txt](benchmarks/rpc.txt)。当前结果见 [native-sdk.txt](benchmarks/native-sdk.txt)。
当前 SDK 基准使用 `BenchmarkNativeSDK`，
使用 64 KiB / 4 MiB 未加壳零填充文件，包含热缓存文件读取、Rust 调用和输出复制；
不测压缩解码。Go 的 B/op 不包含 Rust 分配器内存，无法直接与旧解码微基准比较。
重测写入 `native-sdk.txt`，历史 RPC 记录保留。

Rust 的 `unpack_owned` / `unpack_format_owned` 继续消费原输入 allocation；
未加壳输入的指针比较测试验证没有初始整文件复制。

<title id="title">How Unpack processes each layer</title>

`drivers/all.go`:

```go
// Package drivers provides a convenient way to import all supported packers at once.
// Importing this package will automatically register all packers with the unpack library.
//
// Usage:
//
//	import _ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers"
//
// This will register all supported packers:
//   - UPX
//   - AP32 (native aPLib container decompression)
//   - GZIP, ZLIB, XZ, LZMA (native payload decoding; LZMA requires explicit selection)
//   - ASPack
//   - FSG
//   - WinUpack
//   - Petite
//   - PESpin
//   - Armadillo
//   - Themida
//   - PECompact
//   - NSPack
//   - MPRESS
//   - VMProtect, Enigma, MEW, PELock, PEBundle, Yoda (detection only)
package drivers

import (
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/ap32"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/armadillo"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/aspack"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/enigma"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/fsg"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/gzip"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/lzma"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/mew"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/mpress"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/nspack"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/pebundle"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/pecompact"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/pelock"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/pespin"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/petite"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/themida"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/upx"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/vmprotect"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/winupack"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/xz"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/yoda"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers/zlib"
)

```

`drivers/ap32/ap32.go`:

```go
// Package ap32 provides native Rust decompression of aPLib AP32 containers.
package ap32

import (
	"github.com/orca-zhang/unpack"
	"io"
)

const (
	AP32_VER_NONE = iota
	AP32_VER_1
)

type AP32 struct{}

func init()                         { unpack.RegisterFormat("AP32", AP32{}) }
func (AP32) Name() string           { return "AP32" }
func (AP32) RustFormat() string     { return "AP32" }
func (AP32) Detect(path string) int { return (unpack.RustUnpacker{Format: "AP32"}).Detect(path) }
func (AP32) Unpack(path string) (io.ReaderAt, error) {
	return (unpack.RustUnpacker{Format: "AP32"}).Unpack(path)
}

```

import (
	"github.com/orca-zhang/unpack"

const (
	ARMADILLO_VER_NONE = 0 + iota
	ARMADILLO_VER_1
	ARMADILLO_VER_2
	ARMADILLO_VER_3
)

// Armadillo delegates detection and unpacking to the Rust core.
type Armadillo struct{}

func (Armadillo) Name() string       { return "Armadillo" }
func (Armadillo) RustFormat() string { return "Armadillo" }
func (Armadillo) Detect(path string) int {
	return (unpack.RustUnpacker{Format: "Armadillo"}).Detect(path)
}
func (Armadillo) Unpack(path string) (io.ReaderAt, error) {
	return (unpack.RustUnpacker{Format: "Armadillo"}).Unpack(path)
}

`drivers/aspack/aspack.go`:

```go
package aspack

import (
	"github.com/orca-zhang/unpack"
	"io"
)

func init() { unpack.RegisterFormat("ASPack", ASPack{}) }

const (
	ASPACK_EP_OFFSET_212   = (58 + 0x70e)
	ASPACK_EP_OFFSET_OTHER = (58 + 0x76a)
	ASPACK_EP_OFFSET_242   = (58 + 0x776)

	ASPACK_EPBUFF_OFFSET_21    = (0x4fd)
	ASPACK_EPBUFF_OFFSET_212   = (0x3b9)
	ASPACK_EPBUFF_OFFSET_OTHER = (0x41f)
	ASPACK_EPBUFF_OFFSET_242   = (0x42B)

	ASPACK_BLOCKS_OFFSET_212   = 0x57c
	ASPACK_BLOCKS_OFFSET_OTHER = 0x5d8
	ASPACK_BLOCKS_OFFSET_242   = 0x5e4

	ASPACK_STR_INIT_MLT_OFFSET_212   = 0x70e
	ASPACK_STR_INIT_MLT_OFFSET_OTHER = 0x76a
	ASPACK_STR_INIT_MLT_OFFSET_242   = 0x776

	ASPACK_COMP_BLOCK_OFFSET_212   = 0x6d6
	ASPACK_COMP_BLOCK_OFFSET_OTHER = 0x732
	ASPACK_COMP_BLOCK_OFFSET_242   = 0x73e

	ASPACK_WRKBUF_OFFSET_212   = 0x148
	ASPACK_WRKBUF_OFFSET_OTHER = 0x13a
	ASPACK_WRKBUF_OFFSET_242   = 0x148
)

const (
	ASPACK_VER_NONE = 0 + iota
	ASPACK_VER_21
	ASPACK_VER_212
	ASPACK_VER_OTHER
	ASPACK_VER_242
)

// ASPack delegates detection and unpacking to the Rust core.
type ASPack struct{}

func (ASPack) Name() string           { return "ASPack" }
func (ASPack) RustFormat() string     { return "ASPack" }
func (ASPack) Detect(path string) int { return (unpack.RustUnpacker{Format: "ASPack"}).Detect(path) }
func (ASPack) Unpack(path string) (io.ReaderAt, error) {
	return (unpack.RustUnpacker{Format: "ASPack"}).Unpack(path)
}

type CompressedBlock struct {
	SourceRVA        uint32 // Source RVA in compressed data
	DestRVA          uint32 // Destination RVA in decompressed data
	CompressedSize   uint32 // Compressed size
	DecompressedSize uint32 // Decompressed size
}

```

`drivers/aspack/aspack_test.go`:

```go
package aspack

import (
	"bytes"
	"io"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"

	"github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/internal/testfixture"
)

func TestASPackLayoutAdapters(t *testing.T) {
	packer := ASPack{}
	for _, test := range []struct {
		profile string
		version int
	}{
		{"2.1", ASPACK_VER_21}, {"2.12", ASPACK_VER_212}, {"other-0x41f", ASPACK_VER_OTHER}, {"2.42", ASPACK_VER_242},
	} {
		t.Run(test.profile, func(t *testing.T) {
			for _, variant := range []string{"basic", "shifted-entry", "reordered-sections", "preserve-imports", "no-imports"} {
				base := filepath.Join(testfixture.Root(), "testdata/pe-versions", "aspack-"+test.profile+"-"+variant)
				if got := packer.Detect(base + ".bin"); got != test.version {
					t.Fatalf("%s version: got %d, want %d", variant, got, test.version)
				}
				reader, err := packer.Unpack(base + ".bin")
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
				got, err := io.ReadAll(io.NewSectionReader(reader, 0, 1<<20))
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
				want, err := os.ReadFile(base + ".recovered")
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
				if !bytes.Equal(got, want) {
					t.Fatalf("%s SDK result differs from golden", variant)
				}
			}
		})
	}
	if packer.Detect(filepath.Join(testfixture.Root(), "testdata/aspack/aspack_test.exe")) != ASPACK_VER_21 {
		t.Fatal("existing 2.1 fixture regressed")
	}
}

```

`drivers/fsg/fsg_test.go`:

```go
package fsg

import (
	"bytes"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"

	"github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/internal/testfixture"
)

func TestVersionAdapters(t *testing.T) {
	packer := FSG{}
	for _, version := range []int{1, 2} {
		for _, shift := range []int{0, 29} {
			name := fmt.Sprintf("fsg-id%d-entry%d", version, shift)
			t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
				base := filepath.Join(testfixture.Root(), "testdata/pe-versions", name)
				if packer.Name() != "FSG" || packer.Detect(base+".bin") != version {
					t.Fatal("version detection failed")
				}
				reader, err := packer.Unpack(base + ".bin")
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
				got, err := io.ReadAll(io.NewSectionReader(reader, 0, 1<<20))
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
				want, err := os.ReadFile(base + ".recovered")
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
				if !bytes.Equal(got, want) {
					t.Fatal("SDK output differs from golden")
				}
			})
		}
	}
	if packer.Detect("nonexistent.exe") != 0 {
		t.Fatal("missing input detected")
	}
	if _, err := packer.Unpack("nonexistent.exe"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("missing input accepted")
	}
}

```

package mpress

import (
	"github.com/orca-zhang/unpack"
	"io"
)

func init() { unpack.RegisterFormat("MPRESS", MPRESS{}) }

const (
	MPRESS_VER_NONE = 0 + iota
	MPRESS_VER_1
	MPRESS_VER_2
)

// MPRESS delegates detection and unpacking to the Rust core.
type MPRESS struct{}

`drivers/upx/README.md`:

```md
# UPX Go driver

This package preserves `UPX`, `UPX_VER_*`, `UPXHeader`, `Name`, `Detect` and `Unpack`.
Its implementation now delegates to the Rust core, which uses official UPX for
actual decompression rather than the old approximate NRV/filter implementation.

See the [main README](../../README.md) for engine installation, runtime requirements
and the Go SDK. Real PE32, PE64, ELF64, LZMA and filter fixtures are in
[testdata/upx](../../testdata/upx); their provenance is documented in
[testdata/README.md](../../testdata/README.md).

```

`drivers/upx/testdata/README.md`:

```md
# UPX fixtures moved

The checked-in real UPX samples and SHA-256 manifest are now in
[testdata/upx](../../../testdata/upx). See
[testdata/README.md](../../../testdata/README.md) for generation instructions.
Tests no longer silently skip when sample files are missing.

```

`drivers/upx/upx.go`:

```go
package upx

import (
	"github.com/orca-zhang/unpack"
	"io"
)

func init() { unpack.RegisterFormat("UPX", UPX{}) }

const (
	UPX_VER_NONE = 0 + iota
	UPX_VER_1
	UPX_VER_2
	UPX_VER_3
	UPX_VER_4
)

// UPX delegates detection and unpacking to the Rust core.
type UPX struct{}

func (UPX) Name() string           { return "UPX" }
func (UPX) RustFormat() string     { return "UPX" }
func (UPX) Detect(path string) int { return (unpack.RustUnpacker{Format: "UPX"}).Detect(path) }
func (UPX) Unpack(path string) (io.ReaderAt, error) {
	return (unpack.RustUnpacker{Format: "UPX"}).Unpack(path)
}

type UPXHeader struct {
	Signature       [4]byte // "UPX!"
	VersionMajor    uint8
	VersionMinor    uint8
	VersionRevision uint8
	Format          uint8
	Method          uint32
	OriginalSize    uint32
	CompressedSize  uint32
	Filter          uint32
	FilterCTO       uint32
	HeaderChecksum  uint32
	FileChecksum    uint32
}

```

`drivers/upx/upx_test.go`:

```go
package upx

import (
	"bytes"
	"github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/internal/testfixture"
	"io"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func TestUPXRealSamples(t *testing.T) {
	for _, name := range []string{"pe32", "pe32-lzma", "pe32-filter", "pe64", "elf64", "elf32", "elf-arm64", "macho64"} {
		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
			dir := filepath.Join(testfixture.Root(), "testdata/upx")
			packer := UPX{}
			if packer.Name() != "UPX" {
				t.Fatal(packer.Name())
			}
			if packer.Detect(filepath.Join(dir, name+".packed")) != UPX_VER_1 {
				t.Fatal("UPX not detected")
			}
			originalName := name
			if strings.HasPrefix(name, "pe32-") {
				originalName = "pe32"
			}
			if packer.Detect(filepath.Join(dir, originalName+".original")) != UPX_VER_NONE {
				t.Fatal("false positive on original")
			}
			reader, err := packer.Unpack(filepath.Join(dir, name+".packed"))
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			got, err := io.ReadAll(io.NewSectionReader(reader, 0, 1<<20))
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			want, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(dir, name+".unpacked"))
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if !bytes.Equal(got, want) {
				t.Fatal("output differs from official UPX decoder")
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestUPXInvalidInputs(t *testing.T) {
	if (UPX{}).Detect("nonexistent.exe") != UPX_VER_NONE {
		t.Fatal("missing file detected")
	}
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "fake.exe")
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte("UPX!"), 0600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if (UPX{}).Detect(path) != UPX_VER_NONE {
		t.Fatal("non-executable signature detected")
	}
	if _, err := (UPX{}).Unpack(path); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("accepted invalid executable")
	}
}

func BenchmarkUPXDetect(b *testing.B) {
	path := filepath.Join(testfixture.Root(), "testdata/upx/pe32.packed")
	for i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {
		(UPX{}).Detect(path)
	}
}

```

`drivers/winupack/winupack_test.go`:

```go
package winupack

import (
	"bytes"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"

	"github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/internal/testfixture"
)

func TestVersionAdapters(t *testing.T) {
	packer := WinUpack{}
	for _, version := range []int{1, 2} {
		for _, shift := range []int{0, 29} {
			name := fmt.Sprintf("winupack-id%d-entry%d", version, shift)
			t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
				base := filepath.Join(testfixture.Root(), "testdata/pe-versions", name)
				if packer.Name() != "WinUpack" || packer.Detect(base+".bin") != version {
					t.Fatal("version detection failed")
				}
				reader, err := packer.Unpack(base + ".bin")
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
				got, err := io.ReadAll(io.NewSectionReader(reader, 0, 1<<20))
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
				want, err := os.ReadFile(base + ".recovered")
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
				if !bytes.Equal(got, want) {
					t.Fatal("SDK output differs from golden")
				}
			})
		}
	}
	if packer.Detect("nonexistent.exe") != 0 {
		t.Fatal("missing input detected")
	}
	if _, err := packer.Unpack("nonexistent.exe"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("missing input accepted")
	}
}

`examples/basic.rs`:

```rs
use unpack::{Options, unpack_path};

fn main() -> unpack::Result<()> {
    let path = std::env::args_os()
        .nth(1)
        .ok_or_else(|| unpack::Error::invalid("usage: cargo run --example basic -- FILE"))?;
    let result = unpack_path(path, &Options::default())?;
    for layer in result.history {
        println!(
            "Layer {}: {} ({:?})",
            layer.layer, layer.packer_name, layer.support
        );
    }
    for warning in result.warnings {
        eprintln!("Warning: {warning}");
    }
    println!("Output: {} bytes", result.data.len());
    Ok(())
}

```

`examples/benchmark.rs`:

```rs
//! Small reproducible benchmarks; no shell startup, filesystem reads or compilation in timed loops.
use std::{fs, hint::black_box, path::PathBuf, time::Instant};

fn main() {
    let iterations: usize = std::env::args()
        .nth(1)
        .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1000);
    assert!(iterations > 0);
    let root = PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"));
    let mut measurements = Vec::new();
    for (name, file) in [
        ("detect_plain_pe", "pe32.original"),
        ("detect_upx_pe", "pe32.packed"),
    ] {
        let data = fs::read(root.join("testdata/upx").join(file)).unwrap();
        let mut samples = Vec::new();
        for _ in 0..5 {
            let started = Instant::now();
            for _ in 0..iterations {
                black_box(unpack::detect(black_box(&data)).unwrap());
            }
            samples.push(started.elapsed().as_nanos() as f64 / iterations as f64);
        }
        samples.sort_by(f64::total_cmp);
        measurements.push(serde_json::json!({"name":name,"iterations":iterations,"median_ns":samples[2],"samples_ns":samples}));
    }
    println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&measurements).unwrap());
}

```

`examples/codecs_benchmark.rs`:

```rs
//! Measures CRC and AP32 decoding with an unchanged fixture, outside process and filesystem costs.
use std::{fs, hint::black_box, path::PathBuf, time::Instant};

fn measure<T>(
    name: &str,
    iterations: usize,
    mut operation: impl FnMut() -> T,
) -> serde_json::Value {
    let mut samples = Vec::new();
    for _ in 0..5 {
        let started = Instant::now();
        for _ in 0..iterations {
            black_box(operation());
        }
        samples.push(started.elapsed().as_nanos() as f64 / iterations as f64);
    }
    samples.sort_by(f64::total_cmp);
    serde_json::json!({"name":name,"iterations":iterations,"median_ns":samples[2],"samples_ns":samples})
}

fn main() {
    let root = PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"));
    let data = fs::read(root.join("testdata/upx/pe32.original")).unwrap();
    let ap32 = fs::read(root.join("testdata/aplib/pe32.ap32")).unwrap();
    let measurements = [
        measure("crc32_65kib", 200, || {
            unpack::aplib::crc32(black_box(&data))
        }),
        measure("ap32_65kib", 200, || {
            unpack::aplib::unpack(black_box(&ap32), data.len()).unwrap()
        }),
    ];
    println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&measurements).unwrap());
}

```

`examples/go/main.go`:

```go
package main

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"github.com/orca-zhang/unpack"
	_ "github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/drivers"
	"os"
)

func main() {
	if len(os.Args) != 2 {
		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "usage: go run ./examples/go FILE")
		os.Exit(1)
	}
	result, err := unpack.UnpackWithOptions(context.Background(), os.Args[1], unpack.Options{MaxDepth: 10})
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}
	for _, layer := range result.History {
		fmt.Printf("Layer %d: %s (%s)\n", layer.Layer, layer.PackerName, layer.Support)
	}
	for _, warning := range result.Warnings {
		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Warning:", warning)
	}
	if reader, ok := result.ReaderAt.(interface{ Size() int64 }); ok {
		fmt.Printf("Output: %d bytes\n", reader.Size())
	}
}

func Root() string {

`native/unpack.h`:

```h
#ifndef UNPACK_CORE_H
#define UNPACK_CORE_H
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>

/* Buffers belong to handle and remain valid until unpack_core_result_free. */
typedef struct {
    const uint8_t *metadata;
    size_t metadata_len;
    const uint8_t *data;
    size_t data_len;
    void *handle;
} unpack_result;

void *unpack_core_context_new(void);
/* May run concurrently with unpack_core_call; free only after the call returns. */
void unpack_core_context_cancel(void *context);
void unpack_core_context_free(void *context);
unpack_result unpack_core_call(const uint8_t *request, size_t length, const void *context);
void unpack_core_result_free(void *handle);
#endif

```

`rust/api.rs`:

```rs
//! Shared request dispatcher for the CLI protocol and the statically linked SDK.
use crate::{Detection, Error, LayerInfo, Options, read_limited, unpack_format_owned};
use base64::{Engine, engine::general_purpose::STANDARD};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::{
    path::PathBuf,
    sync::{Arc, atomic::AtomicBool},
    time::Duration,
};

#[derive(Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct Request {
    protocol: u32,
    action: String,
    path: Option<PathBuf>,
    format: Option<String>,
    max_depth: Option<usize>,
    max_size: Option<usize>,
    allow_recovery: Option<bool>,
    timeout_ms: Option<u64>,
    formats: Option<Vec<String>>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Default)]
pub struct Response {
    pub protocol: u32,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub detection: Option<Detection>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub data: Option<String>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub data_size: Option<usize>,
    pub history: Vec<LayerInfo>,
    pub warnings: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub error: Option<Error>,
}

pub fn dispatch(
    input: &[u8],
    cancellation: Option<Arc<AtomicBool>>,
) -> (Response, Option<Vec<u8>>) {
    let mut protocol = 1;
    let mut payload = None;
    let result = (|| {
        if input.len() > 65536 {
            return Err(Error::limit("RPC request exceeds 64 KiB"));
        }
        let request: Request =
            serde_json::from_slice(input).map_err(|e| Error::invalid(e.to_string()))?;
        if request.protocol != 1 && request.protocol != 2 {
            return Err(Error::invalid("unsupported RPC protocol"));
        }
        protocol = request.protocol;
        let mut options = Options::default();
        if let Some(v) = request.max_depth {
            options.max_depth = v;
        }
        if let Some(v) = request.max_size {
            if v == 0 || v > 1024 * 1024 * 1024 {
                return Err(Error::limit("max_size must be between 1 byte and 1 GiB"));
            }
            options.max_size = v;
        }
        if let Some(v) = request.allow_recovery {
            options.allow_recovery = v;
        }
        options.formats = request.formats;
        if let Some(v) = request.timeout_ms {
            options.backend_timeout = Duration::from_millis(v.clamp(1, 300_000));
        }
        options.cancellation = cancellation;
        options.check_cancelled()?;
        let path = request
            .path
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("path is required"))?;
        let data = read_limited(&path, options.max_size)?;
        options.check_cancelled()?;
        let mut response = Response {
            protocol,
            ..Response::default()
        };
        match request.action.as_str() {
            "detect" => {
                response.detection = crate::detect_filtered(
                    &data,
                    request.format.as_deref(),
                    options.formats.as_deref(),
                )?
            }
            "unpack" => {
                let result = unpack_format_owned(data, &options, request.format.as_deref())?;
                if protocol == 1 {
                    response.data = Some(STANDARD.encode(&result.data));
                } else {
                    response.data_size = Some(result.data.len());
                    payload = Some(result.data);
                }
                response.history = result.history;
                response.warnings = result.warnings;
            }
            _ => return Err(Error::invalid("unknown RPC action")),
        }
        Ok(response)
    })();
    let response = result.unwrap_or_else(|error| Response {
        protocol,
        error: Some(error),
        ..Response::default()
    });
    (response, payload)
}

```

`rust/aplib.rs`:

```rs
//! Bounds-checked aPLib bitstream decoding and AP32 size/CRC validation.
//! Raw streams have no signature; only AP32 containers participate in automatic detection.
use crate::{Error, Result, pe::u32le};

const HEADER_SIZE: usize = 24;

pub struct Header {
    pub header_size: usize,
    pub packed_size: usize,
    pub unpacked_size: usize,
    packed_crc: u32,
    unpacked_crc: u32,
}

impl Header {
    pub fn parse(data: &[u8]) -> Result<Self> {
        if data.get(..4) != Some(b"AP32") {
            return Err(Error::invalid("missing AP32 signature"));
        }
        let header = Self {
            header_size: u32le(data, 4)? as usize,
            packed_size: u32le(data, 8)? as usize,
            packed_crc: u32le(data, 12)?,
            unpacked_size: u32le(data, 16)? as usize,
            unpacked_crc: u32le(data, 20)?,
        };
        if header.header_size < HEADER_SIZE || header.packed_size == 0 || header.unpacked_size == 0
        {
            return Err(Error::invalid("invalid AP32 sizes"));
        }
        let end = header
            .header_size
            .checked_add(header.packed_size)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("AP32 size overflow"))?;
        if end != data.len() {
            return Err(Error::invalid("AP32 packed size does not match container"));
        }
        Ok(header)
    }
}

pub fn unpack(data: &[u8], max_size: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let header = Header::parse(data)?;
    if header.unpacked_size > max_size {
        return Err(Error::limit("AP32 output exceeds size limit"));
    }
    let packed = &data[header.header_size..];
    if crc32(packed) != header.packed_crc {
        return Err(Error::invalid("AP32 packed CRC mismatch"));
    }
    let output = decompress(packed, header.unpacked_size)?;
    if output.len() != header.unpacked_size {
        return Err(Error::invalid("AP32 unpacked size mismatch"));
    }
    if crc32(&output) != header.unpacked_crc {
        return Err(Error::invalid("AP32 unpacked CRC mismatch"));
    }
    Ok(output)
}

struct Bits<'a> {
    data: &'a [u8],
    pos: usize,
    tag: u8,
    left: u8,
}
impl Bits<'_> {
    fn byte(&mut self) -> Result<u8> {
        let value = *self
            .data
            .get(self.pos)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("truncated aPLib stream"))?;
        self.pos += 1;
        Ok(value)
    }
    fn bit(&mut self) -> Result<u8> {
        if self.left == 0 {
            self.tag = self.byte()?;
            self.left = 8;
        }
        self.left -= 1;
        Ok((self.tag >> self.left) & 1)
    }
    fn gamma(&mut self) -> Result<usize> {
        let mut value = 1usize;
        loop {
            value = value
                .checked_mul(2)
                .and_then(|v| v.checked_add(self.bit().ok()? as usize))
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("invalid aPLib gamma code"))?;
            if self.bit()? == 0 {
                return Ok(value);
            }
        }
    }
}

fn copy_match(output: &mut Vec<u8>, offset: usize, length: usize, limit: usize) -> Result<()> {
    if offset == 0 || offset > output.len() {
        return Err(Error::invalid("invalid aPLib match offset"));
    }
    if length > limit.saturating_sub(output.len()) {
        return Err(Error::limit("aPLib output exceeds size limit"));
    }
    for _ in 0..length {
        output.push(output[output.len() - offset]);
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Decode a raw aPLib stream, requiring an explicit end marker and rejecting trailing bytes.
/// `max_size` bounds every literal and overlapping match before writing to the output.
pub fn decompress(data: &[u8], max_size: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    if max_size == 0 || max_size > 1024 * 1024 * 1024 {
        return Err(Error::limit("invalid aPLib output limit"));
    }
    let mut bits = Bits {
        data,
        pos: 0,
        tag: 0,
        left: 0,
    };
    let mut output = Vec::with_capacity(max_size.min(65536));
    output.push(bits.byte()?);
    let (mut last_offset, mut was_match) = (0usize, false);
    loop {
        if bits.bit()? == 0 {
            if output.len() >= max_size {
                return Err(Error::limit("aPLib output exceeds size limit"));
            }
            output.push(bits.byte()?);
            was_match = false;
        } else if bits.bit()? == 0 {
            let high = bits.gamma()?;
            let (offset, length) = if !was_match && high == 2 {
                (last_offset, bits.gamma()?)
            } else {
                let high = high
                    .checked_sub(if was_match { 2 } else { 3 })
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("invalid aPLib match code"))?;
                let offset = high
                    .checked_mul(256)
                    .and_then(|v| v.checked_add(bits.byte().ok()? as usize))
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("aPLib offset overflow"))?;
                let adjustment = usize::from(offset >= 32000)
                    + usize::from(offset >= 1280)
                    + if offset < 128 { 2 } else { 0 };
                let length = bits
                    .gamma()?
                    .checked_add(adjustment)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("aPLib match length overflow"))?;
                last_offset = offset;
                (offset, length)
            };
            copy_match(&mut output, offset, length, max_size)?;
            was_match = true;
        } else if bits.bit()? == 0 {
            let code = bits.byte()?;
            let offset = (code >> 1) as usize;
            if offset == 0 {
                if bits.pos != data.len() {
                    return Err(Error::invalid("trailing aPLib data"));
                }
                return Ok(output);
            }
            copy_match(&mut output, offset, 2 + (code & 1) as usize, max_size)?;
            last_offset = offset;
            was_match = true;
        } else {
            let mut offset = 0;
            for _ in 0..4 {
                offset = offset * 2 + bits.bit()? as usize;
            }
            if offset == 0 {
                if output.len() >= max_size {
                    return Err(Error::limit("aPLib output exceeds size limit"));
                }
                output.push(0);
            } else {
                copy_match(&mut output, offset, 1, max_size)?;
            }
            was_match = false;
        }
    }
}

pub fn crc32(data: &[u8]) -> u32 {
    crc32fast::hash(data)
}

`rust/aspack.rs`:

```rs
//! ASPack layouts used by the legacy static recovery path, not a complete ASPack codec.
use crate::{Error, Result};

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct Layout {
    pub id: u32,
    pub name: &'static str,
    pub signature: usize,
    pub oep: usize,
    pub blocks: Option<usize>,
}

pub const LAYOUTS: &[Layout] = &[
    // In 2.1 the RET at 0x502 overlaps the old guessed 0x500 table. It is not a block count.
    Layout {
        id: 1,
        name: "2.1 layout",
        signature: 0x4fd,
        oep: 0x4f4,
        blocks: None,
    },
    Layout {
        id: 2,
        name: "2.12 layout",
        signature: 0x3b9,
        oep: 0x748,
        blocks: Some(0x57c),
    },
    Layout {
        id: 3,
        name: "OTHER (0x41f) layout",
        signature: 0x41f,
        oep: 0x7a4,
        blocks: Some(0x5d8),
    },
    Layout {
        id: 4,
        name: "2.42 layout",
        signature: 0x42b,
        oep: 0x7b0,
        blocks: Some(0x5e4),
    },
];

pub fn identify(code: &[u8]) -> Result<Option<&'static Layout>> {
    let mut candidates = LAYOUTS.iter().filter(|layout| {
        code.get(layout.signature..layout.signature + 6) == Some(b"\x68\0\0\0\0\xc3")
    });
    let found = candidates.next();
    if candidates.next().is_some() {
        return Err(Error::invalid("ambiguous ASPack version markers"));
    }
    Ok(found)
}

pub fn layout(id: u32) -> Result<&'static Layout> {
    LAYOUTS
        .iter()
        .find(|layout| layout.id == id)
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::unsupported("unknown ASPack recovery layout"))
}

`rust/backend.rs`:

```rs
use crate::{Error, Options, Result, read_limited};
use std::{
    fs::{self, File},
    io::Read,
    process::{Command, Stdio},
    thread,
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};

pub fn upx(data: &[u8], options: &Options) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    options.check_cancelled()?;
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir()?;
    let input = dir.path().join("packed.bin");
    let output = dir.path().join("unpacked.bin");
    let diagnostics = dir.path().join("stderr.txt");
    fs::write(&input, data)?;
    let executable = std::env::var_os("UNPACK_UPX").unwrap_or_else(|| "upx".into());
    let mut child = Command::new(executable)
        .args(["-d", "-q", "-o"])
        .arg(&output)
        .arg(&input)
        .env_remove("UPX")
        .stdin(Stdio::null())
        .stdout(Stdio::null())
        .stderr(File::create(&diagnostics)?)
        .spawn()
        .map_err(|e| {
            Error::new(
                "backend",
                format!("cannot start UPX (install upx or set UNPACK_UPX): {e}"),
            )
        })?;
    let started = Instant::now();
    let status = loop {
        if let Err(error) = options.check_cancelled() {
            let _ = child.kill();
            let _ = child.wait();
            return Err(error);
        }
        match child.try_wait() {
            Ok(Some(status)) => break status,
            Ok(None) => {}
            Err(e) => {
                let _ = child.kill();
                let _ = child.wait();
                return Err(e.into());
            }
        }
        if started.elapsed() > options.backend_timeout
            || fs::metadata(&output).is_ok_and(|m| m.len() > options.max_size as u64)
            || fs::metadata(&diagnostics).is_ok_and(|m| m.len() > 65536)
        {
            let _ = child.kill();
            let _ = child.wait();
            return Err(Error::limit("UPX exceeded time or output size limit"));
        }
        thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10));
    };
    if !status.success() {
        let mut message = String::new();
        File::open(diagnostics)?
            .take(4096)
            .read_to_string(&mut message)?;
        return Err(Error::new(
            "backend",
            format!("UPX failed: {}", message.trim()),
        ));
    }
    read_limited(&output, options.max_size)
}

`rust/compression.rs`:

```rs
//! Native payload decoding. Unframed DEFLATE and LZMA streams are never guessed.
use crate::{Error, Result};
use flate2::bufread::{MultiGzDecoder, ZlibDecoder};
use lzma_rust2::{LzmaReader, XzReader};
use std::io::{self, Read};

const MEMORY_LIMIT_KIB: u32 = 64 * 1024;
pub const NAMES: &[&str] = &["GZIP", "ZLIB", "XZ", "LZMA"];

pub fn identify(data: &[u8], requested: Option<&str>) -> Result<Option<&'static str>> {
    let name = if data.starts_with(b"\x1f\x8b") {
        if data.len() < 10 || data[2] != 8 || data[3] & 0xe0 != 0 {
            return Err(Error::invalid("invalid GZIP header"));
        }
        Some("GZIP")
    } else if data.starts_with(b"\xfd7zXZ\0") {
        if data.len() < 12
            || data[6] != 0
            || data[7] & 0xf0 != 0
            || crc32fast::hash(&data[6..8]) != u32::from_le_bytes(data[8..12].try_into().unwrap())
        {
            return Err(Error::invalid("invalid XZ stream header"));
        }
        Some("XZ")
    } else if data.len() >= 2
        && data[0] & 15 == 8
        && data[0] >> 4 <= 7
        && u16::from_be_bytes([data[0], data[1]]) % 31 == 0
    {
        Some("ZLIB")
    } else if requested == Some("LZMA") {
        validate_lzma_header(data)?;
        Some("LZMA")
    } else {
        None
    };
    Ok(name)
}

fn validate_lzma_header(data: &[u8]) -> Result<()> {
    if data.len() < 14 || data[0] >= 225 || data[13] != 0 {
        return Err(Error::invalid("invalid LZMA-Alone header"));
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn codec_error(error: io::Error) -> Error {
    if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::OutOfMemory {
        Error::limit("LZMA/XZ decoder exceeds 64 MiB memory limit")
    } else if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::Unsupported {
        Error::unsupported(format!("unsupported compressed payload: {error}"))
    } else {
        Error::invalid(format!("invalid compressed payload: {error}"))
    }
}

fn read_bounded(reader: &mut impl Read, max_size: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut output = Vec::new();
    reader
        .take(max_size as u64 + 1)
        .read_to_end(&mut output)
        .map_err(codec_error)?;
    if output.len() > max_size {
        return Err(Error::limit("decoded payload exceeds size limit"));
    }
    Ok(output)
}

pub fn unpack(data: &[u8], format: &str, max_size: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    if max_size == 0 || max_size > 1024 * 1024 * 1024 {
        return Err(Error::limit("invalid payload size limit"));
    }
    let (output, trailing) = match format {
        "GZIP" => {
            let mut decoder = MultiGzDecoder::new(data);
            let output = read_bounded(&mut decoder, max_size)?;
            (output, !decoder.into_inner().is_empty())
        }
        "ZLIB" => {
            if data.get(1).is_some_and(|flags| flags & 0x20 != 0) {
                return Err(Error::unsupported(
                    "preset-dictionary ZLIB requires an external dictionary",
                ));
            }
            let mut decoder = ZlibDecoder::new(data);
            let output = read_bounded(&mut decoder, max_size)?;
            (output, !decoder.into_inner().is_empty())
        }
        "XZ" => {
            let mut decoder = XzReader::new_mem_limit(data, true, MEMORY_LIMIT_KIB);
            let output = read_bounded(&mut decoder, max_size)?;
            (output, !decoder.into_inner().is_empty())
        }
        "LZMA" => {
            validate_lzma_header(data)?;
            let size = u64::from_le_bytes(data[5..13].try_into().unwrap());
            if size != u64::MAX && size > max_size as u64 {
                return Err(Error::limit("LZMA declared size exceeds output limit"));
            }
            let mut decoder =
                LzmaReader::new_mem_limit(data, MEMORY_LIMIT_KIB, None).map_err(codec_error)?;
            decoder.set_relaxed_end_condition(false);
            let output = read_bounded(&mut decoder, max_size)?;
            let (rest, buffered) = decoder.into_parts();
            (output, !rest.is_empty() || !buffered.is_empty())
        }
        _ => {
            return Err(Error::unsupported(format!(
                "unknown compression codec: {format}"
            )));
        }
    };
    if trailing {
        return Err(Error::invalid("trailing data after compressed payload"));
    }
    Ok(output)
}

`rust/detect.rs`:

```rs
use crate::{Error, Result, pe::Pe};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Support {
    Unpack,
    Recovery,
    DetectOnly,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Detection {
    pub name: String,
    // Compatibility IDs match the original Go constants; these are not vendor version numbers.
    pub version: u32,
    pub support: Support,
    pub evidence: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Format {
    pub name: &'static str,
    pub support: Support,
    pub sections: &'static [&'static str],
    pub signatures: &'static [&'static str],
}

pub const FORMATS: &[Format] = &[
    Format {
        name: "GZIP",
        support: Support::Unpack,
        sections: &[],
        signatures: &[],
    },
    Format {
        name: "ZLIB",
        support: Support::Unpack,
        sections: &[],
        signatures: &[],
    },
    Format {
        name: "XZ",
        support: Support::Unpack,
        sections: &[],
        signatures: &[],
    },
    Format {
        name: "LZMA",
        support: Support::Unpack,

    },
    Format {

        support: Support::Recovery,

    },

    },

        sections: &[".yP", ".yC"],
        signatures: &["yoda's Protector", "yoda's Crypter"],
    },
