```rs
fn contains(data: &[u8], needle: &[u8]) -> bool {
    !needle.is_empty() && memchr::memmem::find(data, needle).is_some()
}

pub fn detect(data: &[u8]) -> Result<Option<Detection>> {
    detect_format(data, None)
}

pub fn detect_format(data: &[u8], format: Option<&str>) -> Result<Option<Detection>> {
    detect_filtered(data, format, None)
}

pub fn detect_filtered(
    data: &[u8],
    format: Option<&str>,
    allowed: Option<&[String]>,
) -> Result<Option<Detection>> {
    if let Some(name) = format {
        find_format(name)?;
    }
    if let Some(names) = allowed {
        for name in names {
            find_format(name)?;
        }
    }
    if let Some(name) = crate::compression::identify(data, format)? {
        if format.is_none_or(|requested| requested == name)
            && allowed.is_none_or(|names| names.iter().any(|n| n == name))
        {
            return Ok(Some(Detection {
                name: name.into(),
                version: 1,
                support: Support::Unpack,
                evidence: format!("{name} payload header"),
            }));
        }
        return Ok(None);
    }
    if data.starts_with(b"AP32") {
        crate::aplib::Header::parse(data)?;
        if format.is_none_or(|name| name == "AP32")
            && allowed.is_none_or(|names| names.iter().any(|n| n == "AP32"))
        {
            return Ok(Some(Detection {
                name: "AP32".into(),
                version: 1,
                support: Support::Unpack,
                evidence: "AP32 container header".into(),
            }));
        }
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let pe = if data.starts_with(b"MZ") {
        Some(Pe::parse(data)?)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let other_executable = if data.starts_with(b"\x7fELF") {
        validate_elf(data)?;
        true
    } else if crate::macho::has_magic(data) {
        crate::macho::validate(data)?;
        true
    } else {
        false
    };
    if pe.is_none() && !other_executable {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let head = &data[..data.len().min(65536)];
    let tail = &data[data.len().saturating_sub(1024)..];
    // Strong section evidence is checked before string evidence, in stable priority order.
    for sections in [true, false] {
        for f in FORMATS {
            if f.name == "AP32"
                || crate::compression::NAMES.contains(&f.name)
                || format.is_some_and(|name| name != f.name)
                || allowed.is_some_and(|names| !names.iter().any(|n| n == f.name))
                || (pe.is_none() && f.name != "UPX")
            {
                continue;
            }
            if sections {
                if let Some(pe) = &pe {
                    if let Some(s) = pe
                        .sections
                        .iter()
                        .find(|s| f.sections.contains(&s.name.as_str()))
                    {
                        let version = if f.name == "ASPack" {
                            crate::aspack::identify(pe.entry_code(data).unwrap_or(&[]))?
                                .map_or(0, |layout| layout.id)
                        } else {
                            1
                        };
                        if version != 0 {
                            return Ok(Some(Detection {
                                name: f.name.into(),
                                version,
                                support: f.support,
                                evidence: format!("section {}", s.name),
                            }));
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                for (i, sig) in f.signatures.iter().enumerate() {
                    if contains(head, sig.as_bytes()) || contains(tail, sig.as_bytes()) {
                        return Ok(Some(Detection {
                            name: f.name.into(),
                            version: (i + 1) as u32,
                            support: f.support,
                            evidence: format!("signature {sig}"),
                        }));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

pub fn find_format(name: &str) -> Result<&'static Format> {
    FORMATS
        .iter()
        .find(|f| f.name == name)
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid(format!("unknown packer: {name}")))
}

fn validate_elf(data: &[u8]) -> Result<()> {
    use crate::pe::bytes;
    let identity = bytes(data, 0, 16)?;
    let is64 = match identity[4] {
        1 => false,
        2 => true,
        _ => return Err(Error::invalid("invalid ELF class")),
    };
    let little = match identity[5] {
        1 => true,
        2 => false,
        _ => return Err(Error::invalid("invalid ELF encoding")),
    };
    if identity[6] != 1 {
        return Err(Error::invalid("invalid ELF version"));
    }
    let header = if is64 { 64 } else { 52 };
    bytes(data, 0, header)?;
    let read16 = |offset| -> Result<u16> {
        let value = bytes(data, offset, 2)?.try_into().unwrap();
        Ok(if little {
            u16::from_le_bytes(value)
        } else {
            u16::from_be_bytes(value)
        })
    };
    let size_offset = if is64 { 52 } else { 40 };
    if read16(size_offset)? as usize != header {
        return Err(Error::invalid("invalid ELF header size"));
    }
    let offset = if is64 {
        let value = bytes(data, 32, 8)?.try_into().unwrap();
        if little {
            u64::from_le_bytes(value)
        } else {
            u64::from_be_bytes(value)
        }
    } else {
        let value = bytes(data, 28, 4)?.try_into().unwrap();
        u64::from(if little {
            u32::from_le_bytes(value)
        } else {
            u32::from_be_bytes(value)
        })
    };
    let offset = usize::try_from(offset).map_err(|_| Error::invalid("ELF offset overflow"))?;
    let count = read16(size_offset + 4)? as usize;
    let entry_size = read16(size_offset + 2)? as usize;
    if count == 0 || count > 4096 || entry_size != if is64 { 56 } else { 32 } {
        return Err(Error::invalid("invalid ELF program headers"));
    }
    bytes(data, offset, count * entry_size)?;
    Ok(())
}
```

`rust/ffi.rs`:

```rs
//! Ownership boundary for the cgo SDK. No Rust panic may unwind across C.
use crate::{
    Error,
    api::{self, Response},
};
use std::{
    ffi::c_void,
    panic::{AssertUnwindSafe, catch_unwind},
    ptr, slice,
    sync::{
        Arc,
        atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering},
    },
};

#[repr(C)]
pub struct NativeResult {
    metadata: *const u8,
    metadata_len: usize,
    data: *const u8,
    data_len: usize,
    handle: *mut c_void,
}

struct OwnedResult {
    metadata: Vec<u8>,
    data: Vec<u8>,
}

fn error_response(error: Error) -> (Response, Option<Vec<u8>>) {
    (
        Response {
            protocol: 2,
            error: Some(error),
            ..Response::default()
        },
        None,
    )
}

#[unsafe(no_mangle)]
pub extern "C" fn unpack_core_context_new() -> *mut c_void {
    Box::into_raw(Box::new(Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false)))).cast()
}

#[unsafe(no_mangle)]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn unpack_core_context_cancel(context: *mut c_void) {
    if !context.is_null() {
        unsafe { &*context.cast::<Arc<AtomicBool>>() }.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }
}

#[unsafe(no_mangle)]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn unpack_core_context_free(context: *mut c_void) {
    if !context.is_null() {
        drop(unsafe { Box::from_raw(context.cast::<Arc<AtomicBool>>()) });
    }
}

#[unsafe(no_mangle)]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn unpack_core_call(
    input: *const u8,
    length: usize,
    context: *const c_void,
) -> NativeResult {
    let result = catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
        if length > 65536 {
            return error_response(Error::limit("SDK request exceeds 64 KiB"));
        }
        if input.is_null() {
            return error_response(Error::invalid("null SDK request"));
        }
        let cancellation = if context.is_null() {
            None
        } else {
            Some(unsafe { &*context.cast::<Arc<AtomicBool>>() }.clone())
        };
        api::dispatch(
            unsafe { slice::from_raw_parts(input, length) },
            cancellation,
        )
    }));
    let (response, data) =
        result.unwrap_or_else(|_| error_response(Error::new("internal", "Rust core panicked")));
    let owned = Box::new(OwnedResult {
        metadata: serde_json::to_vec(&response).expect("response serialization is infallible"),
        data: data.unwrap_or_default(),
    });
    NativeResult {
        metadata: owned.metadata.as_ptr(),
        metadata_len: owned.metadata.len(),
        data: if owned.data.is_empty() {
            ptr::null()
        } else {
            owned.data.as_ptr()
        },
        data_len: owned.data.len(),
        handle: Box::into_raw(owned).cast(),
    }
}

#[unsafe(no_mangle)]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn unpack_core_result_free(handle: *mut c_void) {
    if !handle.is_null() {
        drop(unsafe { Box::from_raw(handle.cast::<OwnedResult>()) });
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use serde_json::{Value, json};

    unsafe fn metadata(result: &NativeResult) -> Value {
        serde_json::from_slice(unsafe {
            slice::from_raw_parts(result.metadata, result.metadata_len)
        })
        .unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn abi_owns_binary_output_and_preserves_errors() {
        let request = json!({"protocol":2,"action":"unpack","path":concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/testdata/aplib/tokens.ap32")}).to_string();
        unsafe {
            let context = unpack_core_context_new();
            let result = unpack_core_call(request.as_ptr(), request.len(), context);
            assert_eq!(metadata(&result)["data_size"], result.data_len);
            assert_eq!(
                slice::from_raw_parts(result.data, result.data_len),
                include_bytes!("../testdata/aplib/tokens.unpacked")
            );
            unpack_core_result_free(result.handle);
            unpack_core_context_cancel(context);
            let result = unpack_core_call(request.as_ptr(), request.len(), context);
            assert_eq!(metadata(&result)["error"]["code"], "cancelled");
            unpack_core_result_free(result.handle);
            unpack_core_context_free(context);
            for (input, size, code) in [
                (ptr::null(), 0, "invalid_input"),
                (ptr::null(), 65537, "limit"),
            ] {
                let result = unpack_core_call(input, size, ptr::null());
                assert_eq!(metadata(&result)["error"]["code"], code);
                assert_eq!(result.data_len, 0);
                unpack_core_result_free(result.handle);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`rust/lib.rs`:

```rs
//! Rust core for executable packer detection, UPX unpacking and explicit legacy PE recovery.
pub mod api;
pub mod aplib;
mod aspack;
mod backend;
pub mod compression;
mod detect;
mod ffi;
pub mod macho;
pub mod pe;
mod recover;

pub use detect::{Detection, FORMATS, Format, Support, detect, detect_filtered, detect_format};
use serde::Serialize;
use std::{
    collections::HashSet,
    fmt,
    fs::File,
    hash::{Hash, Hasher},
    io::Read,
    path::Path,
    sync::{
        Arc,
        atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering},
    },
    time::Duration,
};

pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, Error>;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
pub struct Error {
    pub code: String,
    pub message: String,
}
impl Error {
    pub fn new(code: &str, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            code: code.into(),
            message: message.into(),
        }
    }
    pub fn invalid(message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self::new("invalid_input", message)
    }
    pub fn unsupported(message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self::new("unsupported", message)
    }
    pub fn limit(message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self::new("limit", message)
    }
}
impl fmt::Display for Error {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}", self.message)
    }
}
impl std::error::Error for Error {}
impl From<std::io::Error> for Error {
    fn from(e: std::io::Error) -> Self {
        Self::new("io", e.to_string())
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Options {
    pub max_depth: usize,
    pub max_size: usize,
    pub allow_recovery: bool,
    pub backend_timeout: Duration,
    pub formats: Option<Vec<String>>,
    /// Cooperative cancellation, checked between layers and while polling UPX.
    pub cancellation: Option<Arc<AtomicBool>>,
}
impl Default for Options {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            max_depth: 10,
            max_size: 256 * 1024 * 1024,
            allow_recovery: false,
            backend_timeout: Duration::from_secs(30),
            formats: None,
            cancellation: None,
        }
    }
}

impl Options {
    pub(crate) fn check_cancelled(&self) -> Result<()> {
        if self
            .cancellation
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|flag| flag.load(Ordering::Relaxed))
        {
            return Err(Error::new("cancelled", "operation cancelled"));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
pub struct LayerInfo {
    pub packer_name: String,
    pub version: u32,
    pub layer: usize,
    pub support: Support,
}
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct UnpackResult {
    pub data: Vec<u8>,
    pub history: Vec<LayerInfo>,
    pub warnings: Vec<String>,
}

pub fn read_limited(path: &Path, max_size: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    validate_size(max_size)?;
    let f = File::open(path)?;
    let metadata = f.metadata()?;
    if !metadata.is_file() {
        return Err(Error::invalid("input must be a regular file"));
    }
    if metadata.len() > max_size as u64 {
        return Err(Error::limit("input exceeds size limit"));
    }
    let mut data = Vec::with_capacity(metadata.len() as usize + 1);
    f.take(max_size as u64 + 1).read_to_end(&mut data)?;
    if data.len() > max_size {
        return Err(Error::limit("input exceeds size limit"));
    }
    Ok(data)
}

pub fn unpack_path(path: impl AsRef<Path>, options: &Options) -> Result<UnpackResult> {
    unpack_owned(read_limited(path.as_ref(), options.max_size)?, options)
}

/// Reuse the caller's input allocation instead of cloning the whole file.
pub fn unpack_owned(data: Vec<u8>, options: &Options) -> Result<UnpackResult> {
    unpack_format_owned(data, options, None)
}

pub fn unpack(data: &[u8], options: &Options) -> Result<UnpackResult> {
    unpack_format(data, options, None)
}

/// A named format performs exactly one layer, for the existing Go driver API.
pub fn unpack_format(data: &[u8], options: &Options, format: Option<&str>) -> Result<UnpackResult> {
    validate_size(options.max_size)?;
    if data.len() > options.max_size {
        return Err(Error::limit("input exceeds size limit"));
    }
    unpack_format_owned(data.to_vec(), options, format)
}

/// Owned counterpart of `unpack_format`, used by the CLI and Go bridge.
pub fn unpack_format_owned(
    data: Vec<u8>,
    options: &Options,
    format: Option<&str>,
) -> Result<UnpackResult> {
    unpack_using(data, options, format, backend::upx)
}

fn unpack_using(
    data: Vec<u8>,
    options: &Options,
    format: Option<&str>,
    mut upx: impl FnMut(&[u8], &Options) -> Result<Vec<u8>>,
) -> Result<UnpackResult> {
    validate_size(options.max_size)?;
    if data.len() > options.max_size {
        return Err(Error::limit("input exceeds size limit"));
    }
    if let Some(name) = format {
        detect::find_format(name)?;
    }
    let mut result = UnpackResult {
        data,
        history: Vec::new(),
        warnings: Vec::new(),
    };
    let mut seen = HashSet::new();
    loop {
        options.check_cancelled()?;
        if options.max_depth != 0 && result.history.len() >= options.max_depth {
            break;
        }
        if result.history.len() >= 1024 {
            return Err(Error::limit("hard layer limit reached"));
        }
        let detection =
            match detect::detect_filtered(&result.data, format, options.formats.as_deref())? {
                Some(d) => d,
                None if format.is_some() => {
                    return Err(Error::invalid("requested packer not detected"));
                }
                None => break,
            };
        let mut hash = std::collections::hash_map::DefaultHasher::new();
        result.data.hash(&mut hash);
        if !seen.insert(hash.finish()) {
            return Err(Error::invalid("unpacking cycle detected"));
        }
        let next = match detection.support {
            Support::Unpack if detection.name == "AP32" => {
                aplib::unpack(&result.data, options.max_size)?
            }
            Support::Unpack if compression::NAMES.contains(&detection.name.as_str()) => {
                compression::unpack(&result.data, &detection.name, options.max_size)?
            }
            Support::Unpack => upx(&result.data, options)?,
            Support::Recovery if options.allow_recovery => {
                recover::recover(&result.data, &detection, options.max_size)?
            }
            Support::Recovery => {
                return Err(Error::unsupported(format!(
                    "{} only supports static PE recovery; enable allow_recovery / --recover explicitly",
                    detection.name
                )));
            }
            Support::DetectOnly => {
                return Err(Error::unsupported(format!(
                    "{} is recognized; unpacking is not implemented",
                    detection.name
                )));
            }
        };
        options.check_cancelled()?;
        if next.len() > options.max_size {
            return Err(Error::limit("output exceeds size limit"));
        }
        if next == result.data {
            return Err(Error::invalid("unpacker made no progress"));
        }
        let executable_source = result.data.starts_with(b"MZ")
            || result.data.starts_with(b"\x7fELF")
            || macho::has_magic(&result.data);
        if (result.data.starts_with(b"MZ") && !next.starts_with(b"MZ"))
            || (result.data.starts_with(b"\x7fELF") && !next.starts_with(b"\x7fELF"))
            || (macho::has_magic(&result.data) && !macho::has_magic(&next))
            || (executable_source && detect(&next).is_err())
        {
            return Err(Error::invalid("unpacker produced a malformed executable"));
        }
        result.data = next;
        result.history.push(LayerInfo {
            packer_name: detection.name.clone(),
            version: detection.version,
            layer: result.history.len() + 1,
            support: detection.support,
        });
        if detection.support == Support::Recovery {
            result.warnings.push(format!("{}: static PE recovery only; compressed code, VM protection and runtime behavior are not verified", detection.name));
            // A repaired file keeps the packer's section names. Do not treat that as another layer.
            break;
        }
        if format.is_some() {
            break;
        }
    }
    Ok(result)
}

fn validate_size(size: usize) -> Result<()> {
    if size == 0 || size > 1024 * 1024 * 1024 {
        return Err(Error::limit("max_size must be between 1 byte and 1 GiB"));
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn legacy_codec_rejects_truncation_and_invalid_distance() {
        assert_eq!(
            recover::legacy_lz(&[0xe0, b'A', b'B', b'C'], 3).unwrap(),
            b"ABC"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            recover::legacy_lz(&[0x80, b'A', 0xff, 0xff], 3).unwrap(),
            b"AAA"
        );
        assert!(recover::legacy_lz(&[0xe0, b'A'], 3).is_err());
        assert!(recover::legacy_lz(&[0, 0xff, 0xff], 3).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn recursive_control_flow_tracks_layers_depth_and_cycles() {
        // Mock decoder outputs exercise recursion; these are not real nested UPX streams.
        let inner = include_bytes!("../testdata/upx/pe32.packed").to_vec();
        let plain = include_bytes!("../testdata/upx/pe32.unpacked").to_vec();
        let mut outer = inner.clone();
        outer.extend_from_slice(b"mock outer layer");
        for depth in [0, 1, 2, 10] {
            let mut outputs = [inner.clone(), plain.clone()].into_iter();
            let result = unpack_using(
                outer.clone(),
                &Options {
                    max_depth: depth,
                    ..Options::default()
                },
                None,
                |_, _| Ok(outputs.next().unwrap()),
            )
            .unwrap();
            assert_eq!(result.history.len(), if depth == 1 { 1 } else { 2 });
            assert_eq!(
                result.data,
                if depth == 1 {
                    inner.clone()
                } else {
                    plain.clone()
                }
            );
        }
        let result = unpack_using(outer.clone(), &Options::default(), Some("UPX"), |_, _| {
            Ok(inner.clone())
        })
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(result.history.len(), 1);
        let mut outputs = [inner.clone(), outer.clone()].into_iter();
        let error = unpack_using(
            outer.clone(),
            &Options {
                max_depth: 0,
                ..Options::default()
            },
            None,
            |_, _| Ok(outputs.next().unwrap()),
        )
        .unwrap_err();
        assert!(error.message.contains("cycle"));
        assert!(
            unpack_using(outer.clone(), &Options::default(), None, |data, _| Ok(
                data.to_vec()
            ))
            .unwrap_err()
            .message
            .contains("no progress")
        );
        assert!(
            unpack_using(outer.clone(), &Options::default(), None, |_, _| Ok(
                b"invalid".to_vec()
            ))
            .is_err()
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn unlimited_depth_retains_a_hard_limit() {
        let input = include_bytes!("../testdata/upx/pe32.packed");
        let error = unpack_using(
            input.to_vec(),
            &Options {
                max_depth: 0,
                ..Options::default()
            },
            None,
            |data, _| {
                let mut output = data.to_vec();
                output.push(0);
                Ok(output)
            },
        )
        .unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(error.code, "limit");
    }
}
```

`rust/macho.rs`:

```rs
//! Structural validation for Mach-O load commands and universal containers.
use crate::{Error, Result, pe::bytes};

pub fn has_magic(data: &[u8]) -> bool {
    // Java class files share CAFEBABE; their version field is not a plausible fat-slice count.
    if data.starts_with(b"\xca\xfe\xba\xbe")
        && data.len() >= 8
        && u32::from_be_bytes(data[4..8].try_into().unwrap()) > 32
    {
        return false;
    }
    matches!(
        data.get(..4),
        Some(
            b"\xce\xfa\xed\xfe"
                | b"\xcf\xfa\xed\xfe"
                | b"\xfe\xed\xfa\xce"
                | b"\xfe\xed\xfa\xcf"
                | b"\xca\xfe\xba\xbe"
                | b"\xbe\xba\xfe\xca"
                | b"\xca\xfe\xba\xbf"
                | b"\xbf\xba\xfe\xca"
        )
    )
}

struct Reader<'a> {
    data: &'a [u8],
    little: bool,
}
impl Reader<'_> {
    fn u32(&self, offset: usize) -> Result<u32> {
        let value = bytes(self.data, offset, 4)?.try_into().unwrap();
        Ok(if self.little {
            u32::from_le_bytes(value)
        } else {
            u32::from_be_bytes(value)
        })
    }
    fn u64(&self, offset: usize) -> Result<u64> {
        let value = bytes(self.data, offset, 8)?.try_into().unwrap();
        Ok(if self.little {
            u64::from_le_bytes(value)
        } else {
            u64::from_be_bytes(value)
        })
    }
}

pub fn validate(data: &[u8]) -> Result<()> {
    match bytes(data, 0, 4)? {
        b"\xca\xfe\xba\xbe" | b"\xbe\xba\xfe\xca" | b"\xca\xfe\xba\xbf" | b"\xbf\xba\xfe\xca" => {
            validate_fat(data)
        }
        _ => validate_thin(data).map(|_| ()),
    }
}

fn validate_thin(data: &[u8]) -> Result<u32> {
    let (little, is64) = match bytes(data, 0, 4)? {
        b"\xce\xfa\xed\xfe" => (true, false),
        b"\xcf\xfa\xed\xfe" => (true, true),
        b"\xfe\xed\xfa\xce" => (false, false),
        b"\xfe\xed\xfa\xcf" => (false, true),
        _ => return Err(Error::invalid("invalid thin Mach-O signature")),
    };
    let reader = Reader { data, little };
    let header = if is64 { 32 } else { 28 };
    bytes(data, 0, header)?;
    if !matches!(reader.u32(12)?, 2 | 6 | 8) {
        return Err(Error::invalid("Mach-O is not executable, dylib or bundle"));
    }
    let count = reader.u32(16)? as usize;
    let size = reader.u32(20)? as usize;
    if count == 0 || count > 4096 || size < count * 8 {
        return Err(Error::invalid("invalid Mach-O load command count"));
    }
    bytes(data, header, size)?;
    let end = header + size;
    let mut command = header;
    for _ in 0..count {
        let kind = reader.u32(command)?;
        let size = reader.u32(command + 4)? as usize;
        if size < 8 || size % if is64 { 8 } else { 4 } != 0 || size > end - command {
            return Err(Error::invalid("invalid Mach-O load command size"));
        }
        let segment = match kind {
            1 => Some((56, 68, 48)),
            0x19 => Some((72, 80, 64)),
            _ => None,
        };
        if (kind == 1 && is64) || (kind == 0x19 && !is64) {
            return Err(Error::invalid("Mach-O segment bitness mismatch"));
        }
        if let Some((base, section_size, sections_field)) = segment {
            if size < base {
                return Err(Error::invalid("short Mach-O segment command"));
            }
            let sections = reader.u32(command + sections_field)? as usize;
            if sections > (size - base) / section_size {
                return Err(Error::invalid("Mach-O sections exceed segment command"));
            }
            let (offset, length) = if kind == 1 {
                (
                    u64::from(reader.u32(command + 32)?),
                    u64::from(reader.u32(command + 36)?),
                )
            } else {
                (reader.u64(command + 40)?, reader.u64(command + 48)?)
            };
            if length != 0 {
                checked_slice(data, offset, length)?;
            }
        }
        command += size;
    }
    if command != end {
        return Err(Error::invalid("Mach-O load command size mismatch"));
    }
    reader.u32(4)
}

fn checked_slice(data: &[u8], offset: u64, size: u64) -> Result<&[u8]> {
    let offset = usize::try_from(offset).map_err(|_| Error::invalid("Mach-O offset overflow"))?;
    let size = usize::try_from(size).map_err(|_| Error::invalid("Mach-O size overflow"))?;
    bytes(data, offset, size)
}

fn validate_fat(data: &[u8]) -> Result<()> {
    let little = matches!(
        data.get(..4),
        Some(b"\xbe\xba\xfe\xca" | b"\xbf\xba\xfe\xca")
    );
    let is64 = matches!(
        data.get(..4),
        Some(b"\xca\xfe\xba\xbf" | b"\xbf\xba\xfe\xca")
    );
    let reader = Reader { data, little };
    let count = reader.u32(4)? as usize;
    if count == 0 || count > 32 {
        return Err(Error::invalid(
            "invalid universal Mach-O architecture count",
        ));
    }
    let entry_size = if is64 { 32 } else { 20 };
    let header_size = 8 + count * entry_size;
    bytes(data, 0, header_size)?;
    let mut ranges = Vec::with_capacity(count);
    for i in 0..count {
        let entry = 8 + i * entry_size;
        let (offset, size, align) = if is64 {
            (
                reader.u64(entry + 8)?,
                reader.u64(entry + 16)?,
                reader.u32(entry + 24)?,
            )
        } else {
            (
                u64::from(reader.u32(entry + 8)?),
                u64::from(reader.u32(entry + 12)?),
                reader.u32(entry + 16)?,
            )
        };
        let end = offset
            .checked_add(size)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("universal Mach-O size overflow"))?;
        if size == 0
            || offset < header_size as u64
            || align > 31
            || offset % (1u64 << align) != 0
            || ranges.iter().any(|&(a, b)| offset < b && a < end)
        {
            return Err(Error::invalid(
                "invalid or overlapping universal Mach-O slices",
            ));
        }
        let slice = checked_slice(data, offset, size)?;
        if validate_thin(slice)? != reader.u32(entry)? {
            return Err(Error::invalid("universal Mach-O CPU type mismatch"));
        }
        ranges.push((offset, end));
    }
    Ok(())
}

```

`rust/main.rs`:

```rs
use std::{
    env, fs,
    io::{self, Read, Write},
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    process::ExitCode,
};
use unpack::{Error, FORMATS, Options, Result, api::Response, read_limited};

fn rpc() -> Result<()> {
    let mut input = Vec::new();
    io::stdin().take(65537).read_to_end(&mut input)?;
    let (response, payload) = unpack::api::dispatch(&input, None);
    let mut stdout = io::stdout().lock();
    serde_json::to_writer(&mut stdout, &response).map_err(|e| Error::new("io", e.to_string()))?;
    if response.protocol == 2 {
        stdout.write_all(b"\n")?;
        if let Some(data) = payload {
            stdout.write_all(&data)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn save(path: &Path, input: &Path, data: &[u8], force: bool) -> Result<()> {
    // Never truncate the source, including through a symlink.
    if path.exists() && fs::canonicalize(path)? == fs::canonicalize(input)? {
        return Err(Error::invalid("output must differ from input"));
    }
    let parent = path
        .parent()
        .filter(|p| !p.as_os_str().is_empty())
        .unwrap_or(Path::new("."));
    let mut temp = tempfile::NamedTempFile::new_in(parent)?;
    temp.write_all(data)?;
    temp.as_file().sync_all()?;
    if force {
        temp.persist(path)
    } else {
        temp.persist_noclobber(path)
    }
    .map_err(|e| Error::new("io", e.error.to_string()))?;
    Ok(())
}

fn cli() -> Result<()> {
    let mut args = env::args_os().skip(1);
    let command = args.next().unwrap_or_else(|| "help".into());
    match command.to_str() {
        Some("rpc") => rpc(),
        Some("version" | "--version" | "-v") => {
            println!("unpack {}", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
            Ok(())
        }
        Some("formats") => {
            serde_json::to_writer_pretty(io::stdout().lock(), FORMATS)
                .map_err(|e| Error::new("io", e.to_string()))?;
            println!();
            Ok(())
        }
        Some("detect" | "unpack") => {
            let mut options = Options::default();
            let (mut input, mut output, mut json, mut force, mut format) =
                (None, None, false, false, None);
            while let Some(arg) = args.next() {
                match arg.to_str() {
                    Some("-o" | "--output") => {
                        output = Some(PathBuf::from(
                            args.next()
                                .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("missing output path"))?,
                        ))
                    }
                    Some("-depth" | "--depth" | "--max-depth") => {
                        options.max_depth = args
                            .next()
                            .and_then(|a| a.to_str().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()))
                            .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("depth must be a nonnegative integer"))?;
                    }
                    Some("--max-size") => {
                        options.max_size = args
                            .next()
                            .and_then(|a| a.to_str().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()))
                            .filter(|v| *v > 0 && *v <= 1024 * 1024 * 1024)
                            .ok_or_else(|| {
                                Error::invalid("max-size must be between 1 byte and 1 GiB")
                            })?;
                    }
                    Some("--recover") => options.allow_recovery = true,
                    Some("--format") => {
                        format = Some(
                            args.next()
                                .and_then(|s| s.into_string().ok())
                                .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("missing format name"))?,
                        )
                    }
                    Some("--json") => json = true,
                    Some("--force") => force = true,
                    Some("--") => {
                        if input.is_some() {
                            return Err(Error::invalid("exactly one input is required"));
                        }
                        input = args.next().map(PathBuf::from);
                        if args.next().is_some() {
                            return Err(Error::invalid("exactly one input is required"));
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    Some(value) if value.starts_with('-') => {
                        return Err(Error::invalid(format!("unknown option: {value}")));
                    }
                    _ if input.is_none() => input = Some(PathBuf::from(arg)),
                    _ => return Err(Error::invalid("exactly one input is required")),
                }
            }
            let input = input.ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("input path is required"))?;
            let data = read_limited(&input, options.max_size)?;
            if command == "detect" {
                let detection = unpack::detect_format(&data, format.as_deref())?;
                if json {
                    serde_json::to_writer(io::stdout().lock(), &detection)
                        .map_err(|e| Error::new("io", e.to_string()))?;
                    println!();
                } else if let Some(d) = detection {
                    println!(
                        "Packer detected: {} (version {}, {:?}; {})",
                        d.name, d.version, d.support, d.evidence
                    );
                } else {
                    println!("No packer detected in: {}", input.display());
                }
                return Ok(());
            }
            let result = unpack::unpack_format_owned(data, &options, format.as_deref())?;
            let output = output.unwrap_or_else(|| {
                let stem = input.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy();
                let extension = input
                    .extension()
                    .map(|s| format!(".{}", s.to_string_lossy()))
                    .unwrap_or_default();
                input.with_file_name(format!("{stem}_unpacked{extension}"))
            });
            save(&output, &input, &result.data, force)?;
            if json {
                let response = Response {
                    protocol: 1,
                    history: result.history,
                    warnings: result.warnings,
                    ..Response::default()
                };
                serde_json::to_writer(io::stdout().lock(), &response)
                    .map_err(|e| Error::new("io", e.to_string()))?;
                println!();
            } else {
                for layer in result.history {
                    println!(
                        "Layer {}: {} ({:?})",
                        layer.layer, layer.packer_name, layer.support
                    );
                }
                for warning in result.warnings {
                    eprintln!("Warning: {warning}");
                }
                println!("Saved: {}", output.display());
            }
            Ok(())
        }
        Some("help" | "--help" | "-h") => {
            println!(
                "unpack - executable packer analysis\n\nCommands:\n  detect [--json] FILE\n  unpack [-o OUTPUT] [--format NAME] [--depth N] [--recover] [--force] [--json] FILE\n  formats\n  version\n\nLimits: --max-size BYTES (default 256 MiB); depth 0 is unlimited (hard cap 1024).\nAP32/GZIP/ZLIB/XZ: native decoding. LZMA-Alone requires --format LZMA.\nUPX: install upx or set UNPACK_UPX. Recovery mode does not guarantee runnable output."
            );
            Ok(())
        }
        _ => Err(Error::invalid("unknown command; run unpack help")),
    }
}

fn main() -> ExitCode {
    match cli() {
        Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,
        Err(e) => {
            eprintln!("{}: {e}", e.code);
            ExitCode::FAILURE
        }
    }
}

```

`rust/pe.rs`:

```rs
use crate::{Error, Result};

pub fn bytes(data: &[u8], offset: usize, size: usize) -> Result<&[u8]> {
    offset
        .checked_add(size)
        .and_then(|end| data.get(offset..end))
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("truncated executable"))
}

pub fn u16le(data: &[u8], offset: usize) -> Result<u16> {
    Ok(u16::from_le_bytes(
        bytes(data, offset, 2)?.try_into().unwrap(),
    ))
}

pub fn u32le(data: &[u8], offset: usize) -> Result<u32> {
    Ok(u32::from_le_bytes(
        bytes(data, offset, 4)?.try_into().unwrap(),
    ))
}

pub fn put32(data: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u32) -> Result<()> {
    let end = offset
        .checked_add(4)
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("offset overflow"))?;
    data.get_mut(offset..end)
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("truncated PE field"))?
        .copy_from_slice(&value.to_le_bytes());
    Ok(())
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Section {
    pub name: String,
    pub virtual_size: u32,
    pub rva: u32,
    pub size: u32,
    pub offset: u32,
    pub flags: u32,
    pub header: usize,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Pe {
    pub optional: usize,
    pub entry: u32,
    pub image_base: u64,
    pub headers_size: u32,
    pub directories: usize,
    pub directory_count: usize,
    pub is_64: bool,
    pub sections: Vec<Section>,
}

impl Pe {
    pub fn parse(data: &[u8]) -> Result<Self> {
        if bytes(data, 0, 2)? != b"MZ" {
            return Err(Error::invalid("missing DOS signature"));
        }
        let pe = u32le(data, 0x3c)? as usize;
        if bytes(data, pe, 24)?.get(..4) != Some(b"PE\0\0") {
            return Err(Error::invalid("missing PE signature"));
        }
        let count = u16le(data, pe + 6)? as usize;
        if count == 0 || count > 96 {
            return Err(Error::invalid("invalid PE section count"));
        }
        let optional_size = u16le(data, pe + 20)? as usize;
        let optional = pe + 24;
        bytes(data, optional, optional_size)?;
        let is_64 = match u16le(data, optional)? {
            0x10b => false,
            0x20b => true,
            _ => return Err(Error::invalid("unsupported PE optional header")),
        };
        let base_size = if is_64 { 112 } else { 96 };
        if optional_size < base_size {
            return Err(Error::invalid("short PE optional header"));
        }
        let directory_count = u32le(data, optional + base_size - 4)? as usize;
        if directory_count > (optional_size - base_size) / 8 {
            return Err(Error::invalid("PE directories exceed optional header"));
        }
        let image_base = if is_64 {
            u64::from_le_bytes(bytes(data, optional + 24, 8)?.try_into().unwrap())
        } else {
            u32le(data, optional + 28)? as u64
        };
        let mut sections = Vec::with_capacity(count);
        let table = optional + optional_size;
        bytes(data, table, count * 40)?;
        for i in 0..count {
            let header = table + i * 40;
            let name = bytes(data, header, 8)?;
            let name =
                String::from_utf8_lossy(&name[..name.iter().position(|b| *b == 0).unwrap_or(8)])
                    .into_owned();
            let section = Section {
                name,
                virtual_size: u32le(data, header + 8)?,
                rva: u32le(data, header + 12)?,
                size: u32le(data, header + 16)?,
                offset: u32le(data, header + 20)?,
                flags: u32le(data, header + 36)?,
                header,
            };
            section
                .rva
                .checked_add(section.virtual_size.max(section.size))
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("PE section RVA overflow"))?;
            // Packed sections may have zero raw size, but present raw data must be in the file.
            if section.size != 0 {
                bytes(data, section.offset as usize, section.size as usize)?;
            }
            sections.push(section);
        }
        let headers_size = u32le(data, optional + 60)?;
        if (headers_size as usize) < table + count * 40 || headers_size as usize > data.len() {
            return Err(Error::invalid("invalid PE header size"));
        }
        Ok(Self {
            optional,
            entry: u32le(data, optional + 16)?,
            image_base,
            headers_size,
            directories: optional + base_size,
            directory_count,
            is_64,
            sections,
        })
    }

    pub fn section_at(&self, rva: u32) -> Option<&Section> {
        self.sections
            .iter()
            .find(|s| rva >= s.rva && rva - s.rva < s.virtual_size.max(s.size))
    }

    pub fn offset(&self, rva: u32) -> Result<usize> {
        if rva < self.headers_size {
            return Ok(rva as usize);
        }
        let s = self
            .section_at(rva)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("RVA outside PE sections"))?;
        let delta = rva - s.rva;
        if delta >= s.size {
            return Err(Error::invalid("RVA has no file-backed data"));
        }
        Ok(s.offset as usize + delta as usize)
    }

    pub fn entry_code<'a>(&self, data: &'a [u8]) -> Result<&'a [u8]> {
        let offset = self.offset(self.entry)?;
        let section = self
            .section_at(self.entry)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("entry point outside sections"))?;
        let end = (section.offset as usize + section.size as usize).min(offset + 8192);
        data.get(offset..end)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("invalid entry point"))
    }

    pub fn valid_oep(&self, value: u32) -> bool {
        value != self.entry
            && self.section_at(value).is_some_and(|s| {
                // Static repair cannot establish an OEP inside the stub or known packed storage.
                self.section_at(self.entry)
                    .is_none_or(|stub| stub.header != s.header)
                    && !crate::FORMATS
                        .iter()
                        .any(|format| format.sections.contains(&s.name.as_str()))
                    && (s.flags & 0x2000_0020 != 0
                        || s.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case("code")
                        || s.name == ".text")
            })
            && self.offset(value).is_ok()
    }
}
```

`rust/recover.rs`:

```rs
//! Ports the old Go SDK's static PE repair mode. This is not a VM or a general decompressor.
use crate::{
    Detection, Error, Result,
    pe::{Pe, put32, u32le},
};

pub fn recover(data: &[u8], detection: &Detection, max_size: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let pe = Pe::parse(data)?;
    let code = pe.entry_code(data)?;
    let oep = if detection.name == "ASPack" {
        let offset = crate::aspack::layout(detection.version)?.oep;
        u32le(code, offset).ok().filter(|oep| pe.valid_oep(*oep))
    } else {
        find_oep(code, &pe)
    }
    .ok_or_else(|| {
        Error::unsupported(format!(
            "{}: no verifiable original entry point",
            detection.name
        ))
    })?;
    let mut output = data.to_vec();
    if detection.name == "ASPack" {
        recover_aspack_blocks(data, &mut output, &pe, code, detection.version, max_size)?;
    }
    put32(&mut output, pe.optional + 16, oep)?;
    repair_imports(&mut output, &pe)?;
    for s in &pe.sections {
        let flags = if s.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case("code") || s.name == ".text" {
            s.flags | 0x6000_0020
        } else {
            s.flags | 0x4000_0000
        };
        put32(&mut output, s.header + 36, flags)?;
    }
    // The old checksum is invalid after any repair; a zero checksum is legal for user PE files.
    put32(&mut output, pe.optional + 64, 0)?;
    Pe::parse(&output)?;
    Ok(output)
}

fn find_oep(code: &[u8], pe: &Pe) -> Option<u32> {
    for i in 0..code.len().saturating_sub(4) {
        let immediate = u32le(code, i + 1).ok()?;
        if code[i] == 0x68 && code.get(i + 5) == Some(&0xc3) {
            let rva = u64::from(immediate)
                .checked_sub(pe.image_base)
                .and_then(|v| u32::try_from(v).ok())
                .unwrap_or(immediate);
            if pe.valid_oep(rva) {
                return Some(rva);
            }
        }
        // Follow an actual relative tail jump, rather than treating its displacement as an RVA.
        if code[i] == 0xe9 {
            let target = i64::from(pe.entry) + i as i64 + 5 + i64::from(immediate as i32);
            if let Ok(rva) = u32::try_from(target) {
                if pe.valid_oep(rva) {
                    return Some(rva);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn recover_aspack_blocks(
    data: &[u8],
    output: &mut Vec<u8>,
    pe: &Pe,
    code: &[u8],
    version: u32,
    max_size: usize,
) -> Result<()> {
    let layout = crate::aspack::layout(version)?;
    let Some(table) = layout.blocks else {
        return Ok(());
    };
    let section = pe
        .sections
        .iter()
        .find(|s| s.name == ".aspack")
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("missing ASPack section"))?;
    let compressed =
        &data[section.offset as usize..section.offset as usize + section.size as usize];
    let count = u32le(code, table)? as usize;
    // shortcut: only the legacy block table layout is recovered; other ASPack layouts need a native codec.
    if count == 0 {
        return Ok(());
    }
    if count > 1000 {
        return Err(Error::unsupported(format!(
            "ASPack {}: unsupported block table count",
            layout.name
        )));
    }
    crate::pe::bytes(code, table + 4, count * 16)?;
    let mut decoded_total = 0usize;
    for i in 0..count {
        let p = table + 4 + i * 16;
        let destination = u32le(code, p)?;
        let size = u32le(code, p + 4)? as usize;
        let unpacked_size = u32le(code, p + 8)? as usize;
        let source = u32le(code, p + 12)?;
        if unpacked_size == 0 || unpacked_size > max_size {
            return Err(Error::limit("ASPack block size exceeds limit"));
        }
        decoded_total = decoded_total
            .checked_add(unpacked_size)
            .filter(|v| *v <= max_size)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::limit("ASPack total decompression exceeds limit"))?;
        let target = pe
            .section_at(destination)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("ASPack block destination outside sections"))?;
        let delta = destination - target.rva;
        if delta >= target.size || unpacked_size > (target.size - delta) as usize {
            return Err(Error::invalid(
                "ASPack block exceeds file-backed destination section",
            ));
        }
        let source = source.checked_sub(section.rva).unwrap_or(source) as usize;
        let input = crate::pe::bytes(compressed, source, size)?;
        let unpacked = legacy_lz(input, unpacked_size)?;
        let offset = pe.offset(destination)?;
        let end = offset
            .checked_add(unpacked.len())
            .filter(|v| *v <= max_size)
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::limit("ASPack output exceeds limit"))?;
        if output.len() < end {
            output.resize(end, 0);
        }
        output[offset..end].copy_from_slice(&unpacked);
    }
    Ok(())
}

// Exact legacy literal/back-reference layout; malformed streams are errors instead of zero-filled success.
pub fn legacy_lz(input: &[u8], expected: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut output = Vec::with_capacity(expected.min(65536));
    let (mut pos, mut bits, mut remaining) = (0, 0u8, 0);
    fn bit(input: &[u8], pos: &mut usize, bits: &mut u8, remaining: &mut u8) -> Result<u8> {
        if *remaining == 0 {
            *bits = *input
                .get(*pos)
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("truncated compressed bitstream"))?;
            *pos += 1;
            *remaining = 8;
        }
        *remaining -= 1;
        Ok((*bits >> *remaining) & 1)
    }
    while output.len() < expected {
        if bit(input, &mut pos, &mut bits, &mut remaining)? == 1 {
            output.push(
                *input
                    .get(pos)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("truncated compressed literal"))?,
            );
            pos += 1;
        } else {
            let raw = crate::pe::u16le(input, pos)? as i16;
            pos += 2;
            let distance = -i32::from(raw);
            let length = (bit(input, &mut pos, &mut bits, &mut remaining)? as usize) * 2
                + bit(input, &mut pos, &mut bits, &mut remaining)? as usize
                + 2;
            if distance <= 0 || distance as usize > output.len() || length > expected - output.len()
            {
                return Err(Error::invalid("invalid compressed back-reference"));
            }
            for _ in 0..length {
                output.push(output[output.len() - distance as usize]);
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(output)
}

fn repair_imports(data: &mut [u8], pe: &Pe) -> Result<()> {
    if pe.directory_count <= 1 {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let current = u32le(data, pe.directories + 8)?;
    if current != 0 && valid_imports(data, pe, current).is_some() {
        return Ok(());
    }
    for s in &pe.sections {
        for delta in (0..(s.size as usize).saturating_sub(39)).step_by(4) {
            let rva = s.rva + delta as u32;
            if let Some(size) = valid_imports(data, pe, rva) {
                put32(data, pe.directories + 8, rva)?;
                put32(data, pe.directories + 12, size)?;
                return Ok(());
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn valid_imports(data: &[u8], pe: &Pe, rva: u32) -> Option<u32> {
    let start = pe.offset(rva).ok()?;
    for i in 0..256 {
        let p = start + i * 20;
        let descriptor = data.get(p..p + 20)?;
        if descriptor.iter().all(|b| *b == 0) {
            return (i > 0).then_some(((i + 1) * 20) as u32);
        }
        let name = pe.offset(u32le(data, p + 12).ok()?).ok()?;
        let suffix = data.get(name..)?;
        let end = suffix.iter().take(256).position(|b| *b == 0)?;
        let name = suffix.get(..end)?;
        if name.len() < 5
            || !name.is_ascii()
            || !name[name.len() - 4..].eq_ignore_ascii_case(b".dll")
        {
            return None;
        }
        pe.offset(u32le(data, p + 16).ok()?).ok()?;
    }
    None
}
```

`rust_bridge.go`:

```go
package unpack

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"
)

// Error preserves the machine-readable error code returned by Rust.
type Error struct {
	Code    string `json:"code"`
	Message string `json:"message"`
}

func (e *Error) Error() string { return e.Code + ": " + e.Message }

type request struct {
	Protocol      int       `json:"protocol"`
	Action        string    `json:"action"`
	Path          string    `json:"path"`
	Format        string    `json:"format,omitempty"`
	MaxDepth      *int      `json:"max_depth,omitempty"`
	MaxSize       int64     `json:"max_size,omitempty"`
	AllowRecovery bool      `json:"allow_recovery"`
	Formats       *[]string `json:"formats,omitempty"`
}

type response struct {
	Protocol  int         `json:"protocol"`
	Detection *Detection  `json:"detection"`
	Data      []byte      `json:"-"`
	DataSize  *int64      `json:"data_size"`
	History   []LayerInfo `json:"history"`
	Warnings  []string    `json:"warnings"`
	Error     *Error      `json:"error"`
}

func callRust(ctx context.Context, req request) (*response, error) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 2*time.Minute)
	defer cancel()
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	req.Protocol = 2
	input, err := json.Marshal(req)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if len(input) > 65536 {
		return nil, &Error{Code: "limit", Message: "SDK request exceeds 64 KiB"}
	}
	maxSize := req.MaxSize
	if maxSize == 0 {
		maxSize = 256 * 1024 * 1024
	}
	return callNative(ctx, input, req.Action, maxSize)
}

func decodeMetadata(metadata []byte, dataSize int64, action string, maxSize int64) (*response, error) {
	if len(metadata) > 2*1024*1024 {
		return nil, errors.New("Rust metadata exceeds size limit")
	}
	var result response
	if err := json.Unmarshal(metadata, &result); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid Rust metadata: %w", err)
	}
	if result.Error != nil {
		return nil, result.Error
	}
	if result.Protocol != 2 {
		return nil, errors.New("incompatible Rust library protocol")
	}
	if action == "unpack" {
		if result.DataSize == nil || *result.DataSize < 0 || *result.DataSize > maxSize || *result.DataSize != dataSize {
			return nil, errors.New("Rust library returned invalid binary data size")
		}
	} else if dataSize != 0 || result.DataSize != nil {
		return nil, errors.New("unexpected Rust response data")
	}
	return &result, nil
}

```

`rust_bridge_test.go`:

```go
package unpack

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func TestNativeMetadataValidation(t *testing.T) {
	for _, header := range []string{
		`{bad json}`, `{"protocol":1}`, `{"protocol":2}`, `{"protocol":2,"data_size":-1}`,
		`{"protocol":2,"data_size":8}`, `{"protocol":2,"data_size":2048}`,
	} {
		if _, err := decodeMetadata([]byte(header), 4, "unpack", 1024); err == nil {
			t.Fatalf("invalid metadata accepted: %s", header)
		}
	}
	if _, err := decodeMetadata([]byte(`{"protocol":2,"data_size":0}`), 0, "unpack", 1); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := decodeMetadata([]byte(`{"protocol":2}`), 1, "detect", 1024); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("detection output accepted")
	}
}

func TestConcurrentNativeCallsAndCancellation(t *testing.T) {
	path := filepath.Join("testdata", "aplib", "tokens.ap32")
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for i := 0; i < 24; i++ {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func(i int) {
			defer wg.Done()
			ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
			defer cancel()
			if i%2 == 0 {
				cancel()
			}
			result, err := callRust(ctx, request{Action: "unpack", Path: path})
			if i%2 == 0 {
				if !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
					t.Errorf("cancellation: %v", err)
				}
			} else if err != nil || len(result.Data) == 0 {
				t.Errorf("native call: %v", err)
			}
		}(i)
	}
	wg.Wait()
}

func TestNativeCancellationStopsUPX(t *testing.T) {
	// A benign blocking backend proves cancellation is forwarded into Rust.
	if os.PathSeparator != '/' {
		t.Skip("shell backend fixture requires Unix")
	}
	dir := t.TempDir()
	started := filepath.Join(dir, "started")
	backend := filepath.Join(dir, "backend")
	if err := os.WriteFile(backend, []byte("#!/bin/sh\nprintf '%s' \"$5\" > \"$UNPACK_TEST_STARTED\"\nwhile :; do :; done\n"), 0700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Setenv("UNPACK_UPX", backend)
	t.Setenv("UNPACK_TEST_STARTED", started)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { _, err := callRust(ctx, request{Action: "unpack", Path: sample("pe32.packed")}); done <- err }()
	deadline := time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second)
	for {
		if _, err := os.Stat(started); err == nil {
			break
		}
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			t.Fatal("backend did not start")
		}
		time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	cancel()
	select {
	case err := <-done:
		if !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
			t.Fatalf("cancellation: %v", err)
		}
	case <-time.After(time.Second):
		t.Fatal("SDK cancellation stalled")
	}
	// Observe Rust cleanup as well as the prompt Go context return.
	input, err := os.ReadFile(started)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	deadline = time.Now().Add(3 * time.Second)
	for {
		if _, err := os.Stat(string(input)); os.IsNotExist(err) {
			break
		}
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			t.Fatal("Rust backend was not reaped and cleaned up")
		}
		time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
	}
}

func BenchmarkNativeSDK(b *testing.B) {
	for _, size := range []int{64 * 1024, 4 * 1024 * 1024} {
		b.Run(fmt.Sprintf("%dKiB", size/1024), func(b *testing.B) {
			path := filepath.Join(b.TempDir(), "plain.bin")
			if err := os.WriteFile(path, make([]byte, size), 0600); err != nil {
				b.Fatal(err)
			}
			b.SetBytes(int64(size))
			b.ReportAllocs()
			b.ResetTimer()
			for i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {
				if _, err := callRust(context.Background(), request{Action: "unpack", Path: path}); err != nil {
					b.Fatal(err)
				}
			}
		})
	}
}
```

`rust_native.go`:

```go
//go:build cgo

package unpack

/*
#cgo darwin,arm64 LDFLAGS: ${SRCDIR}/native/darwin-arm64/libunpack.a -liconv
#cgo darwin,amd64 LDFLAGS: ${SRCDIR}/native/darwin-amd64/libunpack.a -liconv
#cgo linux,amd64 LDFLAGS: ${SRCDIR}/native/linux-amd64/libunpack.a -ldl -lpthread -lm -lrt -lutil
#cgo linux,arm64 LDFLAGS: ${SRCDIR}/native/linux-arm64/libunpack.a -ldl -lpthread -lm -lrt -lutil
#cgo windows,amd64 LDFLAGS: ${SRCDIR}/native/windows-amd64/libunpack.a -lws2_32 -luserenv -lbcrypt -lntdll -lsynchronization
#include <stdlib.h>
#include "native/unpack.h"
*/
import "C"

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"sync"
	"unsafe"
)

func callNative(ctx context.Context, input []byte, action string, maxSize int64) (*response, error) {
	nativeCtx := C.unpack_core_context_new()
	var mu sync.Mutex // Serialize cancel/free; the Rust call runs without this lock.
	type outcome struct {
		result *response
		err    error
	}
	done := make(chan outcome, 1)
	go func() {
		defer func() {
			mu.Lock()
			C.unpack_core_context_free(nativeCtx)
			nativeCtx = nil
			mu.Unlock()
		}()
		request := C.CBytes(input)
		defer C.free(request)
		result := C.unpack_core_call((*C.uint8_t)(request), C.size_t(len(input)), nativeCtx)
		defer C.unpack_core_result_free(result.handle)
		if ctx.Err() != nil {
			done <- outcome{err: ctx.Err()}
			return
		}
		if result.metadata_len > 2*1024*1024 || result.metadata == nil {
			done <- outcome{err: errors.New("invalid Rust metadata buffer")}
			return
		}
		metadata := C.GoBytes(unsafe.Pointer(result.metadata), C.int(result.metadata_len))
		decoded, err := decodeMetadata(metadata, int64(result.data_len), action, maxSize)
		if err == nil && action == "unpack" {
			if result.data_len != 0 && result.data == nil {
				err = errors.New("invalid Rust output buffer")
			} else {
				// One copy transfers ownership from Rust to the returned Go ReaderAt.
				decoded.Data = C.GoBytes(unsafe.Pointer(result.data), C.int(result.data_len))
			}
		}
		done <- outcome{decoded, err}
	}()
	select {
	case result := <-done:
		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		return result.result, result.err
	case <-ctx.Done():
		mu.Lock()
		if nativeCtx != nil {
			C.unpack_core_context_cancel(nativeCtx)
		}
		mu.Unlock()
		return nil, ctx.Err()
	}
}
```

`scripts/benchmark.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Record local measurements. The baseline is historical and is never overwritten here."""
import hashlib
import json
import pathlib
import platform
import subprocess

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DEST = ROOT / 'docs/benchmarks'


def output(*args):
    return subprocess.check_output(args, cwd=ROOT, text=True)


def main():
    DEST.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    native = output('cargo', 'run', '--release', '--locked', '--quiet', '--example', 'benchmark', '--', '1000')
    json.loads(native)
    (DEST / 'optimized.json').write_text(native)
    sdk = output('go', 'test', '-run', '^$', '-bench', 'BenchmarkNativeSDK', '-benchmem', '-benchtime=100ms', '-count=5', '.')
    (DEST / 'native-sdk.txt').write_text(sdk)
    codecs = output('cargo', 'run', '--release', '--locked', '--quiet', '--example', 'codecs_benchmark')
    json.loads(codecs)
    (DEST / 'codecs-after.json').write_text(codecs)
    environment = {
        'platform': platform.platform(), 'machine': platform.machine(),
        'rust': output('rustc', '--version').strip(), 'go': output('go', 'version').strip(),
        'native_command': 'cargo run --release --locked --quiet --example benchmark -- 1000',
        'go_command': 'go test -run ^$ -bench BenchmarkNativeSDK -benchmem -benchtime=100ms -count=5 .',
        'method': 'Rust: median of five batch-average latencies, compilation/fixture reads excluded. Go: five testing.B runs including file reads on zero-filled plain inputs.',
        'scope': 'Rust benchmarks exclude file I/O. Native Go SDK benchmark includes file reading, Rust dispatch, result copying and Go allocations; it reports no Rust allocator totals.',
        'historical_rpc': 'rpc.txt is retained from the former subprocess SDK and is not regenerated.',
        'codec_command': 'cargo run --release --locked --quiet --example codecs_benchmark',
        'codec_method': 'Median of five batches of 200 operations. codecs-before.json precedes the crc32fast change.',
        'baseline': 'Initial migrated Rust core, before memmem search; baseline.json is a historical measurement, not regenerated by this command.',
        'corpus_sha256': {name: hashlib.sha256((ROOT / 'testdata/upx' / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for name in ['pe32.original', 'pe32.packed']},
    }
    (DEST / 'environment.json').write_text(json.dumps(environment, indent=2) + '\n')
    print('Wrote optimized.json, native-sdk.txt, codecs-after.json and environment.json. Baselines retained.')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

```

`scripts/build_go_sdk.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build the Rust archive consumed by cgo for the selected Go target."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
TARGETS = {
    ('darwin', 'arm64'): 'aarch64-apple-darwin',
    ('darwin', 'amd64'): 'x86_64-apple-darwin',
    ('linux', 'arm64'): 'aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu',
    ('linux', 'amd64'): 'x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu',
    ('windows', 'amd64'): 'x86_64-pc-windows-gnu',
}


def main():
    env = json.loads(subprocess.check_output(['go', 'env', '-json', 'GOOS', 'GOARCH', 'CGO_ENABLED'], text=True))
    pair = (env['GOOS'], env['GOARCH'])
    if env['CGO_ENABLED'] != '1':
        raise SystemExit('The Go SDK requires CGO_ENABLED=1 and a C compiler for the target.')
    target = TARGETS.get(pair)
    if target is None:
        raise SystemExit(f'Unsupported Go SDK target: {pair}')
    subprocess.run(['cargo', 'build', '--locked', '--release', '--lib', '--target', target], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
    # Cargo metadata resolves CARGO_TARGET_DIR and repository/global Cargo configuration.
    metadata = json.loads(subprocess.check_output(['cargo', 'metadata', '--no-deps', '--format-version=1'], cwd=ROOT, text=True))
    source = Path(metadata['target_directory']) / target / 'release' / 'libunpack.a'
    dest = ROOT / 'native' / '-'.join(pair) / 'libunpack.a'
    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    shutil.copyfile(source, dest)
    # Replacing an archive alone does not invalidate Go's package cache.
    subprocess.run(['go', 'clean', '-cache'], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
    print(f'Built {dest.relative_to(ROOT)}; run go build ./examples/go or go test ./...')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

```

`scripts/generate_aplib_fixtures.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build independently verifiable aPLib token streams; this is a test encoder, not a compressor."""
import hashlib
import json
import pathlib
import struct
import zlib

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DEST = ROOT / 'testdata/aplib'


class Stream:
    def __init__(self, first):
        self.data = bytearray([first])
        self.plain = bytearray([first])
        self.left = 0
        self.tag = 0
        self.was_match = False
        self.last_offset = 0

    def bit(self, value):
        if not self.left:
            self.tag = len(self.data)
            self.data.append(0)
            self.left = 8
        self.left -= 1
        self.data[self.tag] |= value << self.left

    def bits(self, values):
        for value in values:
            self.bit(int(value))

    def gamma(self, value):
        assert value >= 2
        digits = bin(value)[3:]
        for i, value in enumerate(digits):
            self.bit(int(value))
            self.bit(int(i + 1 != len(digits)))

    def literal(self, value):
        self.bit(0)
        self.data.append(value)
        self.plain.append(value)
        self.was_match = False

    def copy(self, offset, length):
        assert 0 < offset <= len(self.plain)
        for _ in range(length):
            self.plain.append(self.plain[-offset])

    def short(self, offset, length):
        assert 1 <= offset <= 127 and length in (2, 3)
        self.bits('110')
        self.data.append(offset * 2 + length - 2)
        self.copy(offset, length)
        self.was_match, self.last_offset = True, offset

    def nibble(self, offset):
        assert 0 <= offset < 16
        self.bits('111' + f'{offset:04b}')
        if offset:
            self.copy(offset, 1)
        else:
            self.plain.append(0)
        self.was_match = False

    def long(self, offset, length):
        adjustment = int(offset >= 32000) + int(offset >= 1280) + (2 if offset < 128 else 0)
        self.bits('10')
        self.gamma((offset >> 8) + (2 if self.was_match else 3))
        self.data.append(offset & 255)
        self.gamma(length - adjustment)
        self.copy(offset, length)
        self.was_match, self.last_offset = True, offset

    def reuse(self, length):
        assert not self.was_match and self.last_offset > 0
        self.bits('10')
        self.gamma(2)
        self.gamma(length)
        self.copy(self.last_offset, length)
        self.was_match = True

    def finish(self):
        self.bits('110')
        self.data.append(0)
        return bytes(self.data), bytes(self.plain)


def literals(data):
    assert data
    stream = Stream(data[0])
    for value in data[1:]:
        stream.literal(value)
    return stream.finish()


def container(packed, plain):
    return struct.pack('<4s5I', b'AP32', 24, len(packed), zlib.crc32(packed), len(plain), zlib.crc32(plain)) + packed


def main():
    DEST.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stream = Stream(ord('A'))
    stream.literal(ord('B'))
    stream.short(2, 3)
    stream.nibble(1)
    stream.nibble(0)
    stream.literal(ord('C'))
    stream.reuse(3)
    stream.long(3, 5)
    stream.long(5, 4)
    vectors = [('tokens', *stream.finish())]
    stream = Stream(0)
    for i in range(1, 32000):
        stream.literal(i % 251)
    for offset in [127, 128, 1279, 1280, 31999, 32000]:
        adjustment = int(offset >= 32000) + int(offset >= 1280) + (2 if offset < 128 else 0)
        stream.long(offset, 2 + adjustment)
    vectors.append(('offsets', *stream.finish()))
    for name, file in [('pe32', 'pe32.original'), ('upx', 'pe32.packed')]:
        vectors.append((name, *literals((ROOT / 'testdata/upx' / file).read_bytes())))
    inner = container(vectors[0][1], vectors[0][2])
    vectors.append(('nested', *literals(inner)))
    manifest = {'generator': 'scripts/generate_aplib_fixtures.py', 'encoder': 'test token/literal encoder (not a compression ratio benchmark)', 'samples': []}
    for name, packed, plain in vectors:
        wrapped = container(packed, plain)
        for suffix, data in [('.aplib', packed), ('.ap32', wrapped), ('.unpacked', plain)]:
            (DEST / (name + suffix)).write_bytes(data)
        manifest['samples'].append({'name': name, 'packed': name + '.ap32', 'raw': name + '.aplib', 'unpacked': name + '.unpacked', 'packed_sha256': hashlib.sha256(wrapped).hexdigest(), 'unpacked_sha256': hashlib.sha256(plain).hexdigest()})
    (DEST / 'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2) + '\n')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```
