```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Deterministic SVG charts from checked-in measurements and format/fixture metadata; stdlib only."""
import argparse
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from html import escape
import json
import math
import pathlib
import re
import statistics
import subprocess
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DOCS = ROOT / 'docs'
COLORS = {'before': '#a9bab7', 'after': '#157d70', 'unpack': '#157d70', 'recovery': '#b87930', 'detect_only': '#657b89'}
SVG_NS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'
ET.register_namespace('', SVG_NS)


def log_width(value, maximum, width):
    """Compress large ratios while preserving order; labels retain the measured values."""
    return width * math.log1p(value) / math.log1p(maximum)


def chinese_svg(content, labels):
    root = ET.fromstring(content)
    root.set('lang', 'zh-CN')
    for element in root.iter():
        if 'font-family' in element.attrib:
            element.set('font-family', 'Arial, "PingFang SC", "Microsoft YaHei", "Noto Sans CJK SC", sans-serif')
        if element.tag.rsplit('}', 1)[-1] in ('text', 'title', 'desc') and element.text:
            value = labels['text'].get(element.text, element.text)
            for pattern, replacement in labels['patterns']:
                value = re.sub(pattern, replacement, value)
            element.text = value
    return ET.tostring(root, encoding='unicode') + '\n'


def text(x, y, value, size=14, color='#526966', anchor='start'):
    return f'<text x="{x}" y="{y}" font-size="{size}" fill="{color}" text-anchor="{anchor}">{escape(str(value))}</text>'


def box(x, y, width, height, fill, radius=5):
    return f'<rect x="{x}" y="{y}" width="{max(width, 0):.3f}" height="{height}" rx="{radius}" fill="{fill}"/>'


def svg(title, description, height, parts):
    result = f'<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="900" height="{height}" viewBox="0 0 900 {height}" role="img" aria-labelledby="title desc">\n<title id="title">{escape(title)}</title>\n<desc id="desc">{escape(description)}</desc>\n<g font-family="Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif">\n'
    result += box(0.5, 0.5, 899, height - 1, '#f5f9f7', 16)
    result += '<rect x="0.5" y="0.5" width="899" height="' + str(height - 1) + '" rx="16" fill="none" stroke="#dce6e1"/>'
    result += box(28, 22, 4, 22, COLORS['after'], 2) + text(44, 39, title, 22, '#152e2b') + text(28, 65, description, 12)
    result += '<path d="M28 80H872" stroke="#dce6e1"/>'
    result += '\n'.join(parts) + '</g></svg>\n'
    ET.fromstring(result)
    return result


def support_chart(formats):
    counts = Counter(f['support'] for f in formats)
    parts = []
    total = len(formats)
    x = 28
    for key, label in [('unpack', 'Full decode paths'), ('recovery', 'Static PE recovery'), ('detect_only', 'Detection only')]:
        width = 844 * counts[key] / total
        parts.append(box(x, 100, width, 38, COLORS[key], 0))
        parts.append(text(x + width / 2, 125, counts[key], 18, '#ffffff', 'middle'))
        x += width
    for i, (key, label) in enumerate([('unpack', 'Full decode paths'), ('recovery', 'Static PE recovery'), ('detect_only', 'Detection only')]):
        y = 178 + i * 63
        names = ', '.join(f['name'] for f in formats if f['support'] == key)
        parts += [box(28, y - 13, 14, 14, COLORS[key]), text(52, y, f'{label}: {counts[key]}', 16, '#0f172a'), text(52, y + 22, names, 12)]
    parts.append(text(28, 370, '5 native decoders + 1 external UPX path. LZMA requires explicit selection.', 12))
    return svg(f'Format support: {total} built-in formats', 'Capability categories, not a claim of full commercial protector removal.', 394, parts)


def fixture_chart():
    manifest = json.loads((ROOT / 'testdata/upx/manifest.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    rows = []
    for sample in manifest['samples']:
        directory = ROOT / 'testdata/upx'
        rows.append((sample['name'], (directory / sample['packed']).stat().st_size, (directory / sample['unpacked']).stat().st_size))
    maximum = max(u for _, _, u in rows)
    parts = [text(28, 92, 'Sample', 12), text(700, 92, 'Packed / unpacked bytes', 12), text(872, 92, 'Ratio', 12, anchor='end')]
    for i, (name, packed, unpacked) in enumerate(rows):
        y = 125 + i * 65
        parts += [text(28, y + 10, name), box(155, y - 8, 505 * unpacked / maximum, 19, '#d4e0db'), box(155, y + 14, 505 * packed / maximum, 12, COLORS['after']), text(700, y + 9, f'{packed:,} / {unpacked:,}', 12), text(872, y + 9, f'{packed / unpacked:.1%}', 13, anchor='end')]
    legend = 125 + len(rows) * 65 + 15
    parts += [box(155, legend, 14, 12, '#d4e0db'), text(176, legend + 11, 'Unpacked'), box(310, legend, 14, 12, COLORS['after']), text(332, legend + 11, 'Packed'), text(28, legend + 45, 'Benign tiny C fixtures; sizes are test data, not representative malware or compression benchmarks.', 12)]
    return svg('Real UPX fixture sizes', f'{len(rows)} checked-in variants; golden output is produced by the official decoder.', legend + 69, parts)


def codec_chart():
    before = {m['name']: m for m in json.loads((DOCS / 'benchmarks/codecs-before.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))}
    after = {m['name']: m for m in json.loads((DOCS / 'benchmarks/codecs-after.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))}
    parts = []
    for i, (key, label) in enumerate([('crc32_65kib', 'CRC32: 65 KiB input'), ('ap32_65kib', 'AP32: verify both CRCs and decode 65 KiB')]):
        y = 114 + i * 157
        old, new = before[key]['median_ns'], after[key]['median_ns']
        maximum = max(old, new)
        parts.append(text(28, y, label, 16, '#0f172a'))
        for j, (value, color, name) in enumerate([(old, COLORS['before'], 'Before'), (new, COLORS['after'], 'After')]):
            row = y + 20 + j * 36
            parts += [text(28, row + 16, name, 12), box(100, row, log_width(value / 1000, maximum / 1000, 565), 24, color), text(687, row + 17, f'{value / 1000:,.2f} us', 13)]
        parts.append(text(870, y, f'{old / new:.2f}x ratio', 19, '#152e2b', 'end'))
    parts += [text(28, 419, 'Five batches of 200 calls. Median batch-average latency; lower is better. Excludes I/O and startup.', 12), text(28, 440, 'Logarithmic bar lengths; labels show measured values. Each panel uses its own scale.', 12)]
    return svg('Native AP32: faster checksums and decoding', 'crc32fast replaces the byte-by-byte bit loop; CRC polynomial and results remain unchanged.', 465, parts)


def native_chart():
    before = {m['name']: m for m in json.loads((DOCS / 'benchmarks/baseline.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))}
    after = {m['name']: m for m in json.loads((DOCS / 'benchmarks/optimized.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))}
    parts = []
    for i, (key, label, unit) in enumerate([('detect_plain_pe', 'Plain PE: no packer match', 1000), ('detect_upx_pe', 'UPX PE: section match', 1)]):
        y = 114 + i * 157
        old, new = before[key]['median_ns'], after[key]['median_ns']
        maximum = max(old, new)
        suffix = 'us' if unit == 1000 else 'ns'
        parts += [text(28, y, label, 16, '#0f172a')]
        for j, (value, color, name) in enumerate([(old, COLORS['before'], 'Before'), (new, COLORS['after'], 'After')]):
            row = y + 20 + j * 36
            parts += [text(28, row + 16, name, 12), box(100, row, log_width(value / unit, maximum / unit, 565), 24, color), text(687, row + 17, f'{value / unit:,.2f} {suffix}', 13)]
        change = (new / old - 1) * 100
        label = f'{old / new:.2f}x faster' if new < old else f'+{change:.1f}% latency'
        parts.append(text(870, y, label, 19, '#152e2b', 'end'))
    parts += [text(28, 419, 'Logarithmic bar lengths; labels show measured values. Each panel uses its own scale.', 12), text(28, 440, 'Same two files, five batches of 1,000 calls. Lower is better; excludes I/O and process startup.', 12)]
    return svg('Native detection: before and after search optimization', 'The no-match signature scan improves; the section-match fast path is shown separately.', 465, parts)


def rpc_measurements():
    groups = defaultdict(list)
    pattern = r'BenchmarkRPCDecode/(base64|binary)_(\d+)KiB-\d+\s+\d+\s+([\d.]+) ns/op.*?([\d.]+) B/op\s+(\d+) allocs/op'
    for mode, size, latency, memory, allocations in re.findall(pattern, (DOCS / 'benchmarks/rpc.txt').read_text(encoding='utf-8')):
        groups[(mode, int(size))].append((float(latency), float(memory), int(allocations)))
    assert len(groups) == 4 and all(len(rows) == 5 for rows in groups.values()), 'Expected four RPC benchmark cases, each with five repeats'
    return {key: tuple(statistics.median(row[i] for row in rows) for i in range(3)) for key, rows in groups.items()}


def rpc_chart():
    metrics = rpc_measurements()
    parts = []
    for i, size in enumerate([64, 4096]):
        old = metrics[('base64', size)]
        new = metrics[('binary', size)]
        y = 110 + i * 130
        parts += [text(28, y, f'{size:,} KiB payload: allocations during response decode', 16, '#0f172a')]
        for j, (value, color, name) in enumerate([(old[1], COLORS['before'], 'Base64'), (new[1], COLORS['after'], 'Binary')]):
            row = y + 20 + j * 34
            parts += [text(28, row + 16, name, 12), box(100, row, log_width(value, old[1], 560), 23, color), text(685, row + 16, f'{value:,.0f} B/op', 13)]
    parts += [text(28, 379, 'Logarithmic bar lengths; labels show measured values. The existing response buffer is excluded.', 12), text(28, 400, 'This measures decode allocations only, not total memory, pipe throughput or end-to-end unpack speed.', 12)]
    return svg('Historical RPC SDK: decoding allocations', 'Former subprocess SDK; these measurements do not describe the current statically linked SDK.', 425, parts)


def overview_chart():
    before = {m['name']: m['median_ns'] for m in json.loads((DOCS / 'benchmarks/baseline.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))}
    after = {m['name']: m['median_ns'] for m in json.loads((DOCS / 'benchmarks/optimized.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))}
    codecs_before = {m['name']: m['median_ns'] for m in json.loads((DOCS / 'benchmarks/codecs-before.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))}
    codecs_after = {m['name']: m['median_ns'] for m in json.loads((DOCS / 'benchmarks/codecs-after.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))}
    rpc = rpc_measurements()
    panels = [
        ('01 / DETECTION', 'No-match PE scan', before['detect_plain_pe'] / 1000, after['detect_plain_pe'] / 1000, 'us', f"{before['detect_plain_pe'] / after['detect_plain_pe']:.1f}x", 'baseline / optimized latency'),
        ('02 / NATIVE DECODE', 'AP32 / 65 KiB', codecs_before['ap32_65kib'] / 1000, codecs_after['ap32_65kib'] / 1000, 'us', f"{codecs_before['ap32_65kib'] / codecs_after['ap32_65kib']:.1f}x", 'baseline / optimized latency'),
        ('03 / HISTORICAL RPC', '4 MiB response decode', rpc[('base64', 4096)][1], rpc[('binary', 4096)][1], 'B/op', f"{(1 - rpc[('binary', 4096)][1] / rpc[('base64', 4096)][1]) * 100:.3f}%", 'less allocated'),
    ]
    parts = []
    for i, (eyebrow, label, old, new, unit, ratio, caption) in enumerate(panels):
        x = 28 + i * 286
        parts += [box(x, 100, 272, 265, '#ffffff', 12), text(x + 18, 127, eyebrow, 11), text(x + 18, 157, label, 16, '#152e2b'), text(x + 18, 204, ratio, 34, COLORS['after']), text(x + 18, 226, caption, 12)]
        for j, (value, name, color) in enumerate([(old, 'Before', COLORS['before']), (new, 'After', COLORS['after'])]):
            y = 256 + j * 48
            number = f'{value:,.2f}' if unit == 'us' else f'{value:,.0f}'
            parts += [text(x + 18, y, name, 11), text(x + 254, y, f'{number} {unit}', 12, '#152e2b', 'end'), box(x + 18, y + 9, 236, 6, '#edf3ef', 3), box(x + 18, y + 9, log_width(value, old, 236), 6, color, 3)]
    parts += [text(28, 395, 'Apple M4 Pro / release Rust + Go benchmarks / median of five batches / lower is better.', 12), text(28, 416, 'Logarithmic bar lengths; labels show measured values. Excludes I/O, startup and end-to-end unpacking.', 12), text(28, 437, 'RPC allocation values are historical; the current cgo SDK copies Rust output into Go once.', 12)]
    return svg('Performance at a glance', 'Historical baseline vs recorded optimization results. Sources: docs/benchmarks/.', 459, parts)


def workflow_chart(formats):
    counts = Counter(f['support'] for f in formats)
    parts = [
        '<defs><marker id="arrow" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="9" refY="5" markerWidth="6" markerHeight="6" orient="auto-start-reverse"><path d="M0 0L10 5L0 10" fill="none" stroke="#809891" stroke-width="1.5"/></marker></defs>',

        '<path d="M702 142V394M702 229H726" fill="none" stroke="#809891" stroke-width="1.5" marker-end="url(#arrow)"/>',

    lanes = [

    for y, color, title, detail in lanes:

    parts += [text(28, 468, 'Automatic mode repeats native / UPX layers; recovery stops after repair. Analyzed programs are never run.', 12), text(28, 490, 'LZMA-Alone needs explicit selection. UPX needs an installed executable; recovery does not promise runnable output.', 12)]
    return svg('How Unpack processes each layer', 'Rust API, CLI and statically linked Go SDK share the same core.', 514, parts)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--engine', help='Read live format metadata from this unpack executable')
    parser.add_argument('--check', action='store_true', help='Fail if checked-in artifacts differ')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    formats_path = DOCS / 'formats.json'
    formats = json.loads(subprocess.check_output([args.engine, 'formats'], text=True)) if args.engine else json.loads(formats_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    generated = {formats_path: json.dumps(formats, indent=2) + '\n'}
    generated.update({DOCS / 'charts' / name: content for name, content in {
        'support.svg': support_chart(formats), 'upx-fixtures.svg': fixture_chart(),
        'native-detection.svg': native_chart(), 'sdk-allocations.svg': rpc_chart(),
        'ap32-decode.svg': codec_chart(),
        'overview.svg': overview_chart(), 'workflow.svg': workflow_chart(formats),
    }.items()})
    labels = json.loads((ROOT / 'scripts/chart_labels_zh.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    generated.update({path.with_stem(path.stem + '-zh'): chinese_svg(content, labels)
                      for path, content in list(generated.items()) if path.suffix == '.svg'})
    generated[DOCS / 'assets/hero-zh.svg'] = chinese_svg((DOCS / 'assets/hero.svg').read_text(encoding='utf-8'), labels)
    stale = []
    for path, content in generated.items():
        if args.check:
            if not path.is_file() or path.read_text(encoding='utf-8') != content:
                stale.append(str(path.relative_to(ROOT)))
        else:
            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            path.write_text(content, encoding='utf-8')
    if stale:
        raise SystemExit('Stale generated artifacts: ' + ', '.join(stale))
    print(('Verified' if args.check else 'Generated') + f' {len(generated)} metadata/chart files')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

`scripts/generate_demo.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Capture real CLI output and render bilingual GIFs. Requires Pillow and official UPX."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import pathlib
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
WIDTH, HEIGHT = 1040, 640
MINT, INK, MUTED = '#b6edcb', '#eaf4ed', '#a3bfb7'


def font(size, chinese=False):
    candidates = [
        '/System/Library/Fonts/Hiragino Sans GB.ttc' if chinese else '/System/Library/Fonts/Menlo.ttc',
        'C:/Windows/Fonts/msyh.ttc' if chinese else 'C:/Windows/Fonts/consola.ttf',
        '/usr/share/fonts/opentype/noto/NotoSansCJK-Regular.ttc' if chinese else '/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSansMono.ttf',
    ]
    for path in candidates:
        if pathlib.Path(path).is_file():
            return ImageFont.truetype(path, size)
    raise SystemExit('Install a monospace font and a Chinese font (for example DejaVu Sans Mono + Noto Sans CJK).')


def record(engine):
    manifest = json.loads((ROOT / 'testdata/payloads/manifest.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    sample = next(s for s in manifest['samples'] if s['name'] == 'gzip-xz-upx')
    checksum = shutil.which('shasum') or shutil.which('sha256sum')
    if not checksum:
        raise SystemExit('Install shasum or sha256sum to record the checksum command.')
    checksum_args = ['-a', '256'] if pathlib.Path(checksum).stem == 'shasum' else []
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='unpack-demo-') as temporary:
        work = pathlib.Path(temporary)
        for source, name, digest in [(sample['packed'], 'sample.packed', sample['packed_sha256']),
                                     (sample['unpacked'], 'expected.exe', sample['unpacked_sha256'])]:
            content = (ROOT / 'testdata/payloads' / source).read_bytes()
            assert hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest() == digest, f'Fixture hash mismatch: {source}'
            (work / name).write_bytes(content)
        commands = [
            ('unpack detect sample.packed', [str(engine), 'detect', 'sample.packed']),
            ('unpack unpack -o restored.exe sample.packed', [str(engine), 'unpack', '-o', 'restored.exe', 'sample.packed']),
            (' '.join([pathlib.Path(checksum).name, *checksum_args, 'restored.exe', 'expected.exe']),
             [checksum, *checksum_args, 'restored.exe', 'expected.exe']),
        ]
        stages = []
        for display, command in commands:
            result = subprocess.run(command, cwd=work, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True, timeout=45)
            stages.append((display, result.stdout.rstrip().splitlines()))
        assert (work / 'restored.exe').read_bytes() == (work / 'expected.exe').read_bytes(), 'Decoded bytes differ'
        layers = [line.split(': ', 1)[1].split()[0] for line in stages[1][1] if line.startswith('Layer ')]
        assert layers == sample['history'], f'Unexpected layer history: {layers}'
        assert all(line.split()[0] == sample['unpacked_sha256'] for line in stages[2][1]), 'Checksum output differs'
        version = subprocess.check_output([str(engine), 'version'], text=True).strip()
    return stages, version


def render(stages, version, language):
    chinese = language == 'zh'
    terminal_font, label_font, title_font = font(18), font(16, chinese), font(28, chinese)
    labels = ['识别外层格式', '逐层解包', '校验解包结果'] if chinese else ['Detect the outer format', 'Unpack every layer', 'Verify the result']
    title = 'Unpack 实际演示' if chinese else 'Unpack in action'
    success = '逐字节一致 · SHA-256 校验通过' if chinese else 'Byte-for-byte match · SHA-256 verified'
    footer = '真实命令输出 · 演示节奏经调整' if chinese else 'Real CLI output · Playback timing adjusted'
    frames, durations, transcript = [], [], []

    def frame(stage, lines, typing='', cursor=False, verified=False, duration=100):
        canvas = Image.new('RGB', (WIDTH, HEIGHT), '#f5f9f7')
        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
        draw.text((26, 18), title, font=title_font, fill='#152e2b')
        draw.text((28, 57), f'0{stage + 1} / {labels[stage]}', font=label_font, fill='#157d70')
        draw.text((720, 33), 'GZIP > XZ > UPX > PE', font=terminal_font, fill='#526966')
        draw.rounded_rectangle((16, 92, 1024, 552), radius=16, fill='#0d2428')
        for x, color in [(42, '#b87960'), (64, '#d5b36b'), (86, '#79bd94')]:
            draw.ellipse((x, 113, x + 10, 123), fill=color)
        draw.text((432, 108), version, font=label_font, fill=MUTED)
        draw.line((34, 140, 1006, 140), fill='#28483f')
        visible = lines + ([('$ ' + typing, INK)] if typing or cursor else [])
        for index, (line, color) in enumerate(visible):
            assert terminal_font.getlength(line) < WIDTH - 76, f'Terminal line would be clipped: {line}'
            assert index < 13, 'Transcript no longer fits the terminal'
            draw.text((38, 158 + index * 28), line, font=terminal_font, fill=color)
        if cursor:
            x = 38 + terminal_font.getlength('$ ' + typing)
            y = 162 + (len(visible) - 1) * 28
            draw.rectangle((x + 2, y, x + 10, y + 18), fill=MINT)
        if verified:
            draw.rounded_rectangle((26, 571, 1014, 619), radius=10, fill='#e0f0e5')
            draw.text((44, 585), success, font=label_font, fill='#157d70')
        else:
            draw.text((28, 582), footer, font=label_font, fill='#526966')
        frames.append(canvas)
        durations.append(duration)

    for index, (command, output) in enumerate(stages):
        frame(index, transcript, cursor=True, duration=600)
        for length in range(4, len(command), 4):
            frame(index, transcript, typing=command[:length], cursor=True, duration=85)
        frame(index, transcript, typing=command, cursor=True, duration=300)
        transcript.append(('$ ' + command, MINT))
        for line in output:
            transcript.append((line, MINT if line.startswith('Layer ') else MUTED))
            frame(index, transcript, duration=350)
        frame(index, transcript, duration=1300 if index < 2 else 4200, verified=index == 2)
        transcript.append(('', MUTED))
    palette = frames[-1].quantize(colors=128)
    frames = [image.quantize(palette=palette, dither=Image.Dither.NONE) for image in frames]
    destination = ROOT / 'docs/assets' / ('demo-zh.gif' if chinese else 'demo.gif')
    frames[0].save(destination, save_all=True, append_images=frames[1:], duration=durations,
                   loop=0, optimize=True, disposal=1,
                   comment=b'Real CLI capture; output and fixture SHA-256 verified. Playback timing adjusted.')
    with Image.open(destination) as animation:
        total = 0
        for index in range(animation.n_frames):
            animation.seek(index)
            total += animation.info['duration']
        print(f'{destination.relative_to(ROOT)}: {animation.n_frames} frames, {total / 1000:.1f}s, {destination.stat().st_size / 1024:.0f} KiB')


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--engine', type=pathlib.Path, default=ROOT / 'target/release/unpack')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    stages, version = record(args.engine.resolve())
    for language in ('en', 'zh'):
        render(stages, version, language)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

`scripts/generate_fixtures.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Generate benign real executable fixtures with LLVM and official UPX; never execute them."""
import hashlib
import json
import pathlib
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DEST = ROOT / "testdata" / "upx"


def run(*args):
    subprocess.run([str(a) for a in args], check=True)


def main():
    DEST.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    manifest = {"generator": "scripts/generate_fixtures.py", "upx": subprocess.check_output(["upx", "--version"], text=True).splitlines()[0], "samples": []}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary:
        tmp = pathlib.Path(temporary)
        cases = [("pe32", "i686-pc-windows-msvc", "x86"), ("pe64", "x86_64-pc-windows-msvc", "x64"), ("elf64", "x86_64-linux-gnu", None),
                 ("elf32", "i686-linux-gnu", None), ("elf-arm64", "aarch64-linux-gnu", None), ("macho64", "x86_64-apple-macos10.15", "macho")]
        for name, target, machine in cases:
            obj = tmp / (name + ".o")
            original = DEST / (name + ".original")
            packed = DEST / (name + ".packed")
            run("clang", "--target=" + target, "-O1", "-ffreestanding", "-fno-stack-protector", "-c", ROOT / "testdata/src/tiny.c", "-o", obj)
            if machine == "macho":
                run("ld64.lld", "-arch", "x86_64", "-platform_version", "macos", "10.15", "10.15", "-e", "_entry", "-no_pie", "-no_uuid", "-o", original, obj)
            elif machine:
                run("lld-link", "/entry:entry", "/subsystem:console", "/nodefaultlib", "/fixed", "/dynamicbase:no", "/timestamp:0", "/machine:" + machine, "/out:" + str(original), obj)
            else:
                run("ld.lld", "-e", "entry", "--build-id=none", "-o", original, obj)
            variants = [(name, ["--best"])]
            if machine == "macho":
                variants = [(name, ["--best", "--force-macos"])]
            if name == "pe32":
                variants += [("pe32-lzma", ["--lzma"]), ("pe32-filter", ["--filter=0x16"])]
            for variant, flags in variants:
                packed = DEST / (variant + ".packed")
                if packed.exists():
                    packed.unlink()
                run("upx", *flags, "--no-color", "-o", packed, original)
                info = subprocess.check_output(["upx", "--fileinfo", packed], text=True)
                method, filter_id = re.search(r"method (\d+),.*filter (0x[0-9a-fA-F]+)", info).groups()
                if variant == "pe32-lzma":
                    assert int(method) == 14, "UPX did not select LZMA"
                if variant == "pe32-filter":
                    assert int(filter_id, 16) == 0x16, "UPX silently disabled the requested filter"
                decoded = tmp / (variant + ".decoded")
                run("upx", "-d", "-q", "-o", decoded, packed)
                # UPX may normalize PE headers. The golden output is the official decoder's output.
                expected = DEST / (variant + ".unpacked")
                shutil.copyfile(decoded, expected)
                manifest["samples"].append({"name": variant, "flags": flags, "method": int(method), "filter": filter_id, "packed": packed.name, "original": original.name, "unpacked": expected.name,
                    "packed_sha256": hashlib.sha256(packed.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
                    "unpacked_sha256": hashlib.sha256(expected.read_bytes()).hexdigest()})
    (DEST / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2) + "\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`scripts/generate_payload_fixtures.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Real gzip/zlib/XZ/LZMA payload fixtures generated and round-tripped with Python codecs."""
import gzip
import hashlib
import json
import lzma
import pathlib
import zlib

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DEST = ROOT / 'testdata/payloads'


def gz(data):
    return gzip.compress(data, mtime=0)


def unxz(data):
    output = bytearray()
    while data:
        reader = lzma.LZMADecompressor(format=lzma.FORMAT_XZ)
        output.extend(reader.decompress(data))
        assert reader.eof
        data = reader.unused_data
        padding = len(data) - len(data.lstrip(b'\x00'))
        assert padding % 4 == 0
        data = data[padding:]
    return bytes(output)


def main():
    DEST.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    plain = b'Executable analysis fixture\x00\xff\n' * 64
    pe = (ROOT / 'testdata/upx/pe32.original').read_bytes()
    upx = (ROOT / 'testdata/upx/pe32.packed').read_bytes()
    ap32 = (ROOT / 'testdata/aplib/tokens.ap32').read_bytes()
    xz = lambda data: lzma.compress(data, format=lzma.FORMAT_XZ)
    alone = lambda data: lzma.compress(data, format=lzma.FORMAT_ALONE)
    samples = [
        ('text-gzip', 'GZIP', gz(plain), plain, ['GZIP'], False),
        ('text-zlib', 'ZLIB', zlib.compress(plain), plain, ['ZLIB'], False),
        ('text-xz', 'XZ', xz(plain), plain, ['XZ'], False),
        ('text-lzma', 'LZMA', alone(plain), plain, ['LZMA'], True),
        ('pe32-gzip', 'GZIP', gz(pe), pe, ['GZIP'], False),
        ('pe32-zlib', 'ZLIB', zlib.compress(pe), pe, ['ZLIB'], False),
        ('pe32-xz', 'XZ', xz(pe), pe, ['XZ'], False),
        ('pe32-lzma', 'LZMA', alone(pe), pe, ['LZMA'], True),
        ('gzip-xz-upx', 'GZIP', gz(xz(upx)), (ROOT / 'testdata/upx/pe32.unpacked').read_bytes(), ['GZIP','XZ','UPX'], False),
        ('gzip-zlib-ap32', 'GZIP', gz(zlib.compress(ap32)), (ROOT / 'testdata/aplib/tokens.unpacked').read_bytes(), ['GZIP','ZLIB','AP32'], False),
        ('gzip-members', 'GZIP', gz(b'hello ') + gz(b'world'), b'hello world', ['GZIP'], False),
        ('xz-streams', 'XZ', xz(b'hello ') + b'\x00'*4 + xz(b'world'), b'hello world', ['XZ'], False),
        ('empty-gzip', 'GZIP', gz(b''), b'', ['GZIP'], False),
        ('empty-zlib', 'ZLIB', zlib.compress(b''), b'', ['ZLIB'], False),
        ('empty-xz', 'XZ', xz(b''), b'', ['XZ'], False),
        ('empty-lzma', 'LZMA', alone(b''), b'', ['LZMA'], True),
        ('pe32-xz-x86', 'XZ', lzma.compress(pe, filters=[{'id': lzma.FILTER_X86}, {'id': lzma.FILTER_LZMA2, 'preset': 6}]), pe, ['XZ'], False),
        ('delta-xz', 'XZ', lzma.compress(plain, filters=[{'id': lzma.FILTER_DELTA, 'dist': 3}, {'id': lzma.FILTER_LZMA2, 'preset': 6}]), plain, ['XZ'], False),
        ('sha256-xz', 'XZ', lzma.compress(plain, check=lzma.CHECK_SHA256), plain, ['XZ'], False),
    ]
    known = bytearray(alone(plain));known[5:13] = len(plain).to_bytes(8, 'little')
    samples.append(('known-size-lzma', 'LZMA', bytes(known), plain, ['LZMA'], True))
    manifest = {'generator': 'scripts/generate_payload_fixtures.py', 'zlib': zlib.ZLIB_RUNTIME_VERSION, 'samples': []}
    decoders = {'GZIP': gzip.decompress, 'ZLIB': zlib.decompress, 'XZ': unxz, 'LZMA': lambda b: lzma.decompress(b, format=lzma.FORMAT_ALONE)}
    for name, codec, packed, expected, history, explicit in samples:
        first = decoders[codec](packed)
        if len(history) == 1:
            assert first == expected, name
        (DEST / (name + '.packed')).write_bytes(packed)
        (DEST / (name + '.unpacked')).write_bytes(expected)
        manifest['samples'].append({'name': name, 'format': codec, 'packed': name + '.packed', 'unpacked': name + '.unpacked', 'history': history, 'explicit': explicit, 'packed_sha256': hashlib.sha256(packed).hexdigest(), 'unpacked_sha256': hashlib.sha256(expected).hexdigest()})
    (DEST / 'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2) + '\n')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

```

`scripts/generate_version_fixtures.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Small synthetic PE layout fixtures; never claimed to be genuine ASPack-compressed programs."""
import copy
import hashlib
import json
import pathlib
import struct

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DEST = ROOT / 'testdata/pe-versions'
# Independent fixture specifications. Tests must not derive their oracle from the Rust layout table.
LAYOUTS = [('2.1', 1, 0x4fd, 0x4f4, None), ('2.12', 2, 0x3b9, 0x748, 0x57c),
           ('other-0x41f', 3, 0x41f, 0x7a4, 0x5d8), ('2.42', 4, 0x42b, 0x7b0, 0x5e4)]


def put16(data, offset, value):
    struct.pack_into('<H', data, offset, value)


def put32(data, offset, value):
    struct.pack_into('<I', data, offset, value)


def pe(section='.aspack', prefix=0, reordered=False, is64=False, import_mode='repair', code_size=512, stub_size=4096):
    optional, header_size = 0x98, 512
    optional_size = 240 if is64 else 224
    table = optional + optional_size
    order = ['stub', 'code', 'imports'] if reordered else ['code', 'imports', 'stub']
    specs = {'code': ['.text', 0x1000, code_size, 0xc0000040], 'imports': ['.idata', 0x3000, 512, 0xc0000040], 'stub': [section, 0x7000, stub_size, 0xe0000060]}
    size = header_size + sum(s[2] for s in specs.values())
    data = bytearray(size)
    data[:2] = b'MZ';put32(data, 0x3c, 0x80);data[0x80:0x84] = b'PE\0\0'
    put16(data, 0x84, 0x8664 if is64 else 0x14c);put16(data, 0x86, 3)
    put16(data, 0x94, optional_size);put16(data, 0x96, 0x22 if is64 else 0x102)
    put16(data, optional, 0x20b if is64 else 0x10b);put32(data, optional+16, 0x7000+prefix)
    if is64:struct.pack_into('<Q', data, optional+24, 0x140000000)
    else:put32(data, optional+28, 0x400000)
    put32(data, optional+32, 4096);put32(data, optional+36, 512);put32(data, optional+56, 0xa000)
    put32(data, optional+60, header_size);put32(data, optional+64, 0x12345678);put16(data, optional+68, 3)
    directory = optional + (112 if is64 else 96)
    put32(data, directory-4, 0 if import_mode == 'none' else 16)
    offsets, headers = {}, {}
    cursor = header_size
    for i, key in enumerate(order):
        name, rva, raw_size, flags = specs[key]
        h = table+i*40;headers[key] = h;offsets[key] = cursor
        data[h:h+8] = name.encode().ljust(8, b'\0')
        put32(data, h+8, raw_size+4096);put32(data, h+12, rva);put32(data, h+16, raw_size)
        put32(data, h+20, cursor);put32(data, h+36, flags)
        cursor += raw_size
    data[offsets['code']:offsets['code']+32] = bytes(range(32))
    data[offsets['code']+16:offsets['code']+22] = b'\xb8\0\0\0\0\xc3'
    stub = offsets['stub']+prefix
    data[stub] = 0xe9;put32(data, stub+1, (0x1010-(0x7000+prefix)-5)&0xffffffff)
    if import_mode != 'none':
        p = offsets['imports'];put32(data, p, 0x3080);put32(data, p+12, 0x3040);put32(data, p+16, 0x30a0)
        data[p+64:p+77] = b'KERNEL32.dll\0'
        if is64:
            struct.pack_into('<Q', data, p+128, 0x30c0);struct.pack_into('<Q', data, p+160, 0x30c0)
        else:put32(data, p+128, 0x30c0);put32(data, p+160, 0x30c0)
        data[p+194:p+206] = b'ExitProcess\0'
        if import_mode == 'preserve':put32(data, directory+8, 0x3000);put32(data, directory+12, 40)
    return data, {'optional': optional, 'directory': directory, 'offsets': offsets, 'headers': headers, 'stub': stub, 'prefix': prefix, 'import_mode': import_mode, 'code_size': code_size, 'stub_size': stub_size}


def aspack(layout, **kwargs):
    data, meta = pe(**kwargs)
    name, version, signature, oep, blocks = layout
    data[meta['stub']+signature:meta['stub']+signature+6] = b'\x68\0\0\0\0\xc3'
    put32(data, meta['stub']+oep, 0x1010)
    if blocks is not None:put32(data, meta['stub']+blocks, 0)
    return data, meta


def recovered(data, meta, patches=()):
    expected = bytearray(data)
    put32(expected, meta['optional']+16, 0x1010);put32(expected, meta['optional']+64, 0)
    for key, h in meta['headers'].items():
        flags = struct.unpack_from('<I', expected, h+36)[0]
        put32(expected, h+36, flags | (0x60000020 if key == 'code' else 0x40000000))
    if meta['import_mode'] == 'repair':put32(expected, meta['directory']+8, 0x3000);put32(expected, meta['directory']+12, 40)
    for destination, plain in patches:expected[meta['offsets']['code']+destination:meta['offsets']['code']+destination+len(plain)] = plain
    return expected


def main():
    DEST.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    cases = []
    def add(name, family, version, data, meta, expected=None, error=None, detect_error=None, detected=True, profile=None, tags=()):
        item = {'name': name, 'family': family, 'version': version, 'profile': profile or family,
                'provenance': 'synthetic PE layout, not actual packer output', 'tags': list(tags),
                'input': name+'.bin', 'detected': detected, 'detect_error': detect_error,
                'recovery_error': error, 'expected_oep': 0x1010 if expected is not None else None,
                'pe64': struct.unpack_from('<H', data, meta['optional'])[0] == 0x20b,
                'entry_offset': meta['stub'], 'input_sha256': hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()}
        (DEST/item['input']).write_bytes(data)
        if expected is not None:
            item['output'] = name+'.recovered'
            item['output_sha256'] = hashlib.sha256(expected).hexdigest()
            (DEST/item['output']).write_bytes(expected)
        cases.append(item)
    for layout in LAYOUTS:
        profile, version, sig, oep, table = layout
        for variant, kwargs in [('basic', {}), ('shifted-entry', {'prefix': 17}), ('reordered-sections', {'prefix': 31, 'reordered': True}),
                                ('preserve-imports', {'import_mode': 'preserve'}), ('no-imports', {'import_mode': 'none'})]:
            data, meta = aspack(layout, **kwargs)
            add('aspack-'+profile+'-'+variant, 'ASPack', version, data, meta, recovered(data, meta), profile=profile, tags=[variant])
        for variant, value in [('zero-oep', 0), ('header-oep', 0x100), ('unbacked-oep', 0x1200), ('outside-oep', 0xffff0000)]:
            data, meta = aspack(layout);put32(data, meta['stub']+oep, value)
            add('aspack-'+profile+'-'+variant, 'ASPack', version, data, meta, error='unsupported', profile=profile, tags=[variant])
        data, meta = aspack(layout)
        other = next(x for x in LAYOUTS if x[1] != version)
        data[meta['stub']+other[2]:meta['stub']+other[2]+6] = b'\x68\0\0\0\0\xc3'
        add('aspack-'+profile+'-ambiguous', 'ASPack', 0, data, meta, error='invalid_input', detect_error='invalid_input', profile=profile, tags=['ambiguous-markers'])
        data, meta = aspack(layout)
        cut = sig+5;put32(data, meta['headers']['stub']+16, cut);data = data[:meta['offsets']['stub']+cut]
        add('aspack-'+profile+'-truncated-marker', 'ASPack', 0, data, meta, detected=False, profile=profile, tags=['truncated-marker'])
        if table is None:
            # The PUSH/RET bytes imply a very large DWORD at 0x500; this must never be read as a block count.
            data, meta = aspack(layout)
            assert struct.unpack_from('<I', data, meta['stub']+0x500)[0] > 1000
            add('aspack-'+profile+'-overlapping-ret', 'ASPack', version, data, meta, recovered(data, meta), profile=profile, tags=['ret-is-not-block-count'])
            continue
        for variant, relative, chunks in [('literal-block', False, [(b'\xe0ABC', b'ABC')]),
                                           ('relative-source', True, [(b'\xe0ABC', b'ABC')]),
                                           ('overlap-match', False, [(b'\x80A\xff\xff', b'AAA')]),
                                           ('two-blocks', False, [(b'\xe0ABC', b'ABC'), (b'\x80A\xff\xff', b'AAA')])]:
            data, meta = aspack(layout, prefix=23, reordered=True);put32(data, meta['stub']+table, len(chunks))
            patches=[]
            for i, (packed, plain) in enumerate(chunks):
                source=0x40+i*16;destination=0x40+i*32
                data[meta['offsets']['stub']+source:meta['offsets']['stub']+source+len(packed)] = packed
                struct.pack_into('<4I', data, meta['stub']+table+4+i*16, 0x1000+destination, len(packed), len(plain), source if relative else 0x7000+source)
                patches.append((destination, plain))
            add('aspack-'+profile+'-'+variant, 'ASPack', version, data, meta, recovered(data, meta, patches), profile=profile, tags=[variant, 'legacy-test-codec'])
        for variant, values, error in [
            ('source-outside', (0x1040,4,3,0xffffffff), 'invalid_input'),
            ('destination-headers', (0x100,4,3,0x7040), 'invalid_input'),
            ('destination-crosses-section', (0x11ff,4,3,0x7040), 'invalid_input'),
            ('oversized-output', (0x1040,4,0xffffffff,0x7040), 'limit'),
            ('short-stream', (0x1040,2,3,0x7040), 'invalid_input')]:
            data, meta = aspack(layout);data[meta['offsets']['stub']+0x40:meta['offsets']['stub']+0x44]=b'\xe0ABC'
            put32(data, meta['stub']+table, 1);struct.pack_into('<4I',data,meta['stub']+table+4,*values)
            add('aspack-'+profile+'-'+variant,'ASPack',version,data,meta,error=error,profile=profile,tags=[variant])
        for variant, count, error in [('unsupported-count',1001,'unsupported'),('truncated-table',200,'invalid_input')]:
            data, meta = aspack(layout);put32(data,meta['stub']+table,count)
            add('aspack-'+profile+'-'+variant,'ASPack',version,data,meta,error=error,profile=profile,tags=[variant])
        data, meta = aspack(layout);cut=oep+3;put32(data,meta['headers']['stub']+16,cut);data=data[:meta['offsets']['stub']+cut]
        add('aspack-'+profile+'-truncated-oep','ASPack',version,data,meta,error='unsupported',profile=profile,tags=['truncated-oep'])
    for family, section, marker, version in [('FSG','.packed','FSG!',1),('FSG','.packed','FSG2',2),('WinUpack','.packed','WinUpack',1),('WinUpack','.packed','UPack',2)]:
        for prefix in [0,29]:
            data,meta=pe(section=section,prefix=prefix)
            data[-len(marker):]=marker.encode()
            add(f'{family.lower()}-id{version}-entry{prefix}',family,version,data,meta,recovered(data,meta),tags=['signature-layout-id','relative-jump'])
    for is64 in [False,True]:
        for section in ['.MPRESS1','.MPRESS2']:
            data,meta=pe(section=section,is64=is64,prefix=19)
            add(f'mpress-{64 if is64 else 32}-{section[1:].lower()}','MPRESS',1,data,meta,recovered(data,meta),tags=['section-suffix-is-not-version','pe64' if is64 else 'pe32'])
    for family,section,support in [('Petite','.petite','recovery'),('PESpin','.pespin','recovery'),('Armadillo','.EPACK','recovery'),('Themida','.themida','recovery'),('PECompact','.pec','recovery'),('NSPack','.nsp0','recovery'),('VMProtect','.vmp0','detect_only'),('Enigma','.enigma1','detect_only'),('MEW','MEW','detect_only'),('PELock','.pelock','detect_only'),('PEBundle','.pebundl','detect_only'),('Yoda','.yP','detect_only')]:
        data,meta=pe(section=section,prefix=13)
        add(family.lower()+'-basic',family,1,data,meta,recovered(data,meta) if support=='recovery' else None,error='unsupported' if support=='detect_only' else None,tags=[support])
    manifest={'generator':'scripts/generate_version_fixtures.py','notice':'All generated cases are synthetic layout/boundary fixtures. Version IDs are compatibility/layout IDs, not proof of actual vendor packer output.','layouts':[{'label':x[0],'id':x[1],'signature_offset':x[2],'oep_offset':x[3],'block_table_offset':x[4]} for x in LAYOUTS],'cases':cases}
    (DEST/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest,indent=2)+'\n')
    print(f'Generated {len(cases)} synthetic version/layout cases')


if __name__=='__main__':
    main()

`scripts/test_generate_charts.py`:

```py
"""Checks for readable nonlinear bars and localized measurement labels."""
import json
import unittest
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

import generate_charts as charts


class ChartTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_extreme_ratios_stay_visible(self):
        self.assertGreater(charts.log_width(0.95271, 393.2125, 565), 50)
        self.assertGreater(charts.log_width(336, 4202825, 560), 180)
        self.assertEqual(charts.log_width(0, 1000, 560), 0)
        self.assertEqual(charts.log_width(1000, 1000, 560), 560)
        widths = [charts.log_width(x, 1000, 560) for x in (1, 10, 100, 1000)]
        self.assertEqual(widths, sorted(set(widths)))

    def test_localization_preserves_measurements(self):
        labels = json.loads((charts.ROOT / 'scripts/chart_labels_zh.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        content = charts.chinese_svg(charts.overview_chart(), labels)
        values = {e.text for e in ET.fromstring(content).iter()}
        for label in ('关键性能指标', '84.6 倍', '17.3 倍', '99.992%', '4,202,825 字节/次', '336 字节/次'):
            self.assertIn(label, values)
        self.assertNotIn('Performance at a glance', values)
        self.assertIn('柱长采用对数缩放', content)

    def test_all_benchmark_charts_disclose_scale(self):
        for render in (charts.overview_chart, charts.codec_chart, charts.native_chart, charts.rpc_chart):
            with self.subTest(chart=render.__name__):
                self.assertIn('Logarithmic bar lengths', render())


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

```

`testdata/README.md`:

```md
# Fixture provenance

`src/tiny.c` is a benign executable fixture source under this repository's MIT
license. It contains a small function call and a 64 KiB data section. It does not
open files, access the network or spawn processes. Tests inspect/decompress the
binaries and never execute them.

`upx/` contains checked-in PE32/64, ELF32/x86-64/ARM64 and x86-64 Mach-O originals, real official-UPX packed
files, and official-decoder golden outputs. PE32 also has explicit LZMA and x86
filter `0x16` variants. `manifest.json` records UPX version, compression flags, the
actual method/filter reported by UPX, and
SHA-256 hashes. UPX may normalize a PE header, so golden output comes from the
official decoder rather than assuming whole-file equality with the original.

Regenerate with Python 3, LLVM `clang`, `lld-link`, `ld.lld`, `ld64.lld`, and UPX on PATH:

```sh
python3 scripts/generate_fixtures.py
```

Fixtures are checked in; Rust/Go tests need UPX but do not need LLVM or Python.
The separate CI chart check uses standard-library Python.
Toolchain or UPX version changes may change the generated binary bytes: review
and commit the regenerated manifest and fixtures together. Tests fail rather than
skip when a required fixture or backend is absent.

Synthetic fixtures in `tests/core.rs` rename
a section of the benign PE fixture and add a simple tail jump or ASPack marker.
They validate detection, recovery control flow, preservation of original code and
error handling. They are **not** genuine outputs of FSG, WinUpack, commercial VM
protectors or other packers, and do not establish full unpacking support.

`aspack/aspack_test.exe` predates the Rust migration. Its provenance was
not recorded by the original repository. It remains a regression sample for
recognition and limited PE repair only. Tests do not run it or claim its compressed
program is restored.

## Native aPLib / AP32 vectors

`aplib/` contains five AP32 containers, corresponding raw streams, SHA-256 manifests,
and decoded payloads. Regenerate with standard-library Python only:

```sh
python3 scripts/generate_aplib_fixtures.py
```

The test encoder emits explicit literal/match tokens, including short and long
copies, repeated offsets, zero literals, overlapping copies and distance thresholds
127/128, 1279/1280, 31999/32000. It is not an optimizing compressor: literal-only
PE/UPX wrappers may be larger than their input and are excluded from compression
ratio charts. `nested.ap32` wraps another AP32 container. `upx.ap32` contains the
real UPX PE32 fixture; normal unpacking must record AP32 then UPX and match the
official UPX decoder's golden result. These are container chains, not commercial
protector stubs.

Both raw streams and containers were verified against the independent `aplib 0.8`
decoder from PyPI with strict checking. Verifier wheel SHA-256:
`a015bb3ed6bf95d9d20a7b93f4f55bbdfdeb8cf8ab5fdee05152f114aa49dfd4`.
That verifier is not bundled, linked, imported by this project or required for
tests; no verifier source was copied into the Rust implementation.

## Native compression payloads

`payloads/` has 20 cases produced and round-tripped with independent Python
`gzip`, `zlib` and `lzma` implementations, with packed/output SHA-256 manifests.
They cover all four codecs, empty streams, GZIP members, padded concatenated XZ
streams, XZ BCJ/delta/SHA-256, known-size LZMA and two three-layer chains:
GZIP -> XZ -> UPX and GZIP -> ZLIB -> AP32.

```sh
python3 scripts/generate_payload_fixtures.py
```

LZMA-Alone is tested by explicit format selection; it is never automatically
guessed. XZ/LZMA decoding has a 64 MiB dictionary/model budget. Raw payload
support is separate from full unpacking of PE protectors using these algorithms.

The eight UPX variants include experimental x86-64 Mach-O produced using
`--force-macos`. Tests compare the official decoder's bytes and validate structure;
they do not establish macOS execution compatibility. ARM64 Mach-O was excluded
after upstream UPX rejected decoding the generated candidate. Mach-O universal
and byte-order tests are structural fixtures, not real packed universal programs.

## PE version and boundary matrix

`pe-versions/` contains **105 synthetic inputs**, generated with independent explicit
layout specifications by `scripts/generate_version_fixtures.py`. **81 cases** cover
ASPack 2.1, 2.12, OTHER (`0x41f`) and 2.42. The remaining cases cover FSG and
WinUpack signature IDs, MPRESS PE32/64 section variants, and other PE families.
They vary entry-point offsets, section order, import tables, OEP validity,
truncation, ambiguous markers, block sources/destinations and overlapping matches.
Positive cases have byte-for-byte expected repair outputs and SHA-256 manifests.
Rust verifies PE structure; Go checks SDK values/errors and the same golden bytes.

```sh
python3 scripts/generate_version_fixtures.py
```

These fixtures are **not vendor packer output**. The limited legacy block test
codec is not a complete ASPack decompressor. ASPack 2.1 has no supported block table:
the old guessed offset overlaps the stub's RET byte. ASPack 2.42/OTHER currently
have synthetic layout coverage only, not verified commercial unpacking.

## Real legacy packer versions

`retdec/` imports 16 regression inputs and one unpacked reference from
[Avast RetDec regression tests](https://github.com/avast/retdec-regression-tests),
commit `8d8087f906c3d38c0cce8fcfb15acf3228c5e532`. The upstream MIT notice is preserved
in `retdec/LICENSE`. `retdec/manifest.json` records each source path, URL and SHA-256.
Vendor versions come from upstream test names/expectations, not section suffixes.

- ASPack 2.12: C sample; recognition and rejection of unverified recovery.
- FSG 1.33: recursive factorial sample; recognition and rejection of unverified recovery.
- MPRESS 1.05, 1.27, 2.05, 2.19: Hello World samples with code before/after/between data.
- MPRESS 2.19 MSVC: LZMA and LZMAT variants.

The upstream `mpress_105_code_after_data-unpacked` reference has entry point
`0x3000`. A stub jump into the compressed `.MPRESS1` section targets `0x102f`,
which proves that selecting a plausible branch is insufficient. Recovery must
reject destinations in the stub or known packed storage. Tests require every
imported packed sample to return `unsupported` in recovery mode; they do not
claim native MPRESS/FSG/ASPack decompression. The legacy repository ASPack 2.1
sample remains covered separately as limited PE repair. No imported executable
is run during tests.

```

`testdata/src/tiny.c`:

```c
/* Benign fixture: no system dependencies, file/network access or subprocesses. */
volatile unsigned char fixture_data[65536] = "unpack fixture: reproducible executable sample";
__attribute__((noinline)) int fixture_helper(void) { return fixture_data[0]; }
int entry(void) {
    int result = fixture_helper() == 'u' ? 0 : 1;
#if defined(__linux__) && defined(__x86_64__)
    __asm__ volatile("syscall" : : "a"(60), "D"(result) : "rcx", "r11", "memory");
    __builtin_unreachable();
#elif defined(__linux__) && defined(__i386__)
    __asm__ volatile("int $0x80" : : "a"(1), "b"(result) : "memory");
    __builtin_unreachable();
#elif defined(__linux__) && defined(__aarch64__)
    register long status __asm__("x0") = result;
    register long syscall_number __asm__("x8") = 93;
    __asm__ volatile("svc #0" : : "r"(status), "r"(syscall_number) : "memory");
    __builtin_unreachable();
#else
    return result;
#endif
}

```

`tests/aplib.rs`:

```rs
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::{fs, path::PathBuf};
use unpack::{Options, aplib, detect, unpack, unpack_format};

fn fixture(name: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
    fs::read(
        PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
            .join("testdata/aplib")
            .join(name),
    )
    .unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn independent_aplib_vectors_and_crc_checks() {
    let manifest: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&fixture("manifest.json")).unwrap();
    let samples = manifest["samples"].as_array().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(samples.len(), 5);
    for sample in samples {
        let input = fixture(sample["packed"].as_str().unwrap());
        let expected = fixture(sample["unpacked"].as_str().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(
            format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&input)),
            sample["packed_sha256"].as_str().unwrap()
        );
        assert_eq!(
            format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&expected)),
            sample["unpacked_sha256"].as_str().unwrap()
        );
        let raw = fixture(sample["raw"].as_str().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(aplib::decompress(&raw, expected.len()).unwrap(), expected);
        assert_eq!(aplib::unpack(&input, expected.len()).unwrap(), expected);
        assert_eq!(detect(&input).unwrap().unwrap().name, "AP32");
        let named = unpack_format(&input, &Options::default(), Some("AP32")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(named.data, expected);
        assert_eq!(named.history.len(), 1);
        assert!(named.warnings.is_empty());
        assert!(aplib::unpack(&input, expected.len() - 1).is_err());
    }
    assert_eq!(aplib::crc32(b"123456789"), 0xcbf43926);
}

#[test]
fn genuine_container_chain_reaches_real_upx_output() {
    let input = fixture("upx.ap32");
    let result = unpack(&input, &Options::default()).unwrap();
    let expected =
        fs::read(PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("testdata/upx/pe32.unpacked"))
            .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(result.data, expected);
    assert_eq!(
        result
            .history
            .iter()
            .map(|l| l.packer_name.as_str())
            .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        ["AP32", "UPX"]
    );
    let one = unpack(
        &input,
        &Options {
            max_depth: 1,
            ..Options::default()
        },
    )
    .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(one.data, fixture("upx.unpacked"));
    assert_eq!(one.history.len(), 1);
    let native = unpack(&fixture("nested.ap32"), &Options::default()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(native.data, fixture("tokens.unpacked"));
    assert_eq!(native.history.len(), 2);
}

#[test]
fn malformed_aplib_and_ap32_are_bounded_errors() {
    let valid = fixture("tokens.ap32");
    for end in 0..valid.len() {
        assert!(aplib::unpack(&valid[..end], 1024).is_err());
    }
    for offset in [4, 8, 12, 16, 20, valid.len() - 1] {
        let mut corrupt = valid.clone();
        corrupt[offset] ^= 0x80;
        assert!(aplib::unpack(&corrupt, 1024).is_err(), "offset {offset}");
    }
    for input in [
        vec![],
        vec![b'A'],
        vec![b'A', 0xc0, 0xfe],
        vec![b'A', 0xff, 0xff],
        vec![b'A', 0x80, 0xff],
    ] {
        assert!(aplib::decompress(&input, 64).is_err());
    }
    let mut trailing = fixture("tokens.aplib");
    trailing.push(0);
    assert!(aplib::decompress(&trailing, 1024).is_err());
    assert!(aplib::decompress(&fixture("tokens.aplib"), 0).is_err());
    assert!(aplib::decompress(&fixture("tokens.aplib"), usize::MAX).is_err());
}

#[test]
fn an_ap32_string_inside_a_pe_is_not_a_container() {
    let mut input =
        fs::read(PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("testdata/upx/pe32.original"))
            .unwrap();
    input.extend_from_slice(b"AP32");
    assert!(detect(&input).unwrap().is_none());
}

`tests/cli.rs`:

```rs
use std::{
    fs,
    io::Write,
    path::PathBuf,
    process::{Command, Stdio},
};

fn binary() -> &'static str {
    env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_unpack")
}
fn root() -> PathBuf {
    PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
}
fn rpc(request: serde_json::Value) -> serde_json::Value {
    let mut child = Command::new(binary())
        .arg("rpc")
        .stdin(Stdio::piped())
        .stdout(Stdio::piped())
        .spawn()
        .unwrap();
    child
        .stdin
        .take()
        .unwrap()
        .write_all(request.to_string().as_bytes())
        .unwrap();
    let output = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
    assert!(output.status.success());
    serde_json::from_slice(&output.stdout).unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn protocol_errors_are_structured() {
    for request in [
        serde_json::json!({"protocol":99,"action":"detect"}),
        serde_json::json!({"protocol":1,"action":"unknown","path":"missing"}),
        serde_json::json!({"protocol":1,"action":"detect","path":"missing"}),
        serde_json::json!({"protocol":1,"action":"detect","path":"missing","max_depth":-1}),
    ] {
        let response = rpc(request);
        assert_eq!(response["protocol"], 1);
        assert!(response["error"]["code"].is_string());
    }
}

#[test]
fn cli_and_rpc_unpack_real_samples() {
    let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let input = root().join("testdata/upx/pe64.packed");
    let output = temp.path().join("with spaces.exe");
    let result = Command::new(binary())
        .args(["unpack", "--json", "-o"])
        .arg(&output)
        .arg(&input)
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(
        result.status.success(),
        "{}",
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stderr)
    );
    let response: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&result.stdout).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(response["history"][0]["packer_name"], "UPX");
    assert_eq!(
        fs::read(&output).unwrap(),
        fs::read(root().join("testdata/upx/pe64.unpacked")).unwrap()
    );
    let response = rpc(serde_json::json!({"protocol":1,"action":"detect","path":input}));
    assert_eq!(response["detection"]["name"], "UPX");
    let response =
        rpc(serde_json::json!({"protocol":1,"action":"unpack","path":input,"max_size":4}));
    assert_eq!(response["error"]["code"], "limit");
}

#[test]
fn cli_preserves_existing_files_and_never_overwrites_input() {
    let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let input = temp.path().join("packed.exe");
    let output = temp.path().join("existing.exe");
    fs::copy(root().join("testdata/upx/pe32.packed"), &input).unwrap();
    fs::write(&output, b"keep this").unwrap();
    assert!(
        !Command::new(binary())
            .args(["unpack", "-o"])
            .arg(&output)
            .arg(&input)
            .status()
            .unwrap()
            .success()
    );
    assert_eq!(fs::read(&output).unwrap(), b"keep this");
    let before = fs::read(&input).unwrap();
    assert!(
        !Command::new(binary())
            .args(["unpack", "--force", "-o"])
            .arg(&input)
            .arg(&input)
            .status()
            .unwrap()
            .success()
    );
    assert_eq!(fs::read(&input).unwrap(), before);
}

#[test]
fn missing_backend_is_an_error_with_no_output_file() {
    let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let output = temp.path().join("missing.exe");
    let result = Command::new(binary())
        .env("UNPACK_UPX", temp.path().join("no-upx"))
        .args(["unpack", "-o"])
        .arg(&output)
        .arg(root().join("testdata/upx/pe32.packed"))
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(!result.status.success());
    assert!(!output.exists());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stderr).contains("cannot start UPX"));
}

#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]
fn backend_limits_terminate_and_clean_private_files() {
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    for (body, expected) in [
        ("while :; do :; done\n", "limit"),
        (
            "cp \"$UNPACK_TEST_GOLDEN\" \"$4\"\nwhile :; do :; done\n",
            "limit",
        ),
    ] {
        let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let private = temp.path().join("private");
        fs::create_dir(&private).unwrap();
        let backend = temp.path().join("backend");
        fs::write(&backend, format!("#!/bin/sh\n{body}")).unwrap();
        fs::set_permissions(&backend, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700)).unwrap();
        let mut child = Command::new(binary())
            .arg("rpc")
            .env("UNPACK_UPX", &backend)
            .env(
                "UNPACK_TEST_GOLDEN",
                root().join("testdata/upx/pe32.unpacked"),
            )
            .env("TMPDIR", &private)
            .stdin(Stdio::piped())
            .stdout(Stdio::piped())
            .spawn()
            .unwrap();
        let request = serde_json::json!({"protocol":1,"action":"unpack","path":root().join("testdata/upx/pe32.packed"),"timeout_ms":100,"max_size":4096});
        child
            .stdin
            .take()
            .unwrap()
            .write_all(request.to_string().as_bytes())
            .unwrap();
        let result = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
        assert!(result.status.success());
        let response: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&result.stdout).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(response["error"]["code"], expected);
        assert_eq!(
            fs::read_dir(&private).unwrap().count(),
            0,
            "temporary backend files leaked"
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn protocol_two_streams_exact_binary_data_and_preserves_protocol_one() {
    use base64::Engine;
    let input = root().join("testdata/upx/pe32.packed");
    let expected = fs::read(root().join("testdata/upx/pe32.unpacked")).unwrap();
    let legacy = rpc(serde_json::json!({"protocol":1,"action":"unpack","path":input}));
    assert_eq!(
        base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD
            .decode(legacy["data"].as_str().unwrap())
            .unwrap(),
        expected
    );
    let mut child = Command::new(binary())
        .arg("rpc")
        .stdin(Stdio::piped())
        .stdout(Stdio::piped())
        .spawn()
        .unwrap();
    child
        .stdin
        .take()
        .unwrap()
        .write_all(
            serde_json::json!({"protocol":2,"action":"unpack","path":input})
                .to_string()
                .as_bytes(),
        )
        .unwrap();
    let output = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
    assert!(output.status.success());
    let end = output.stdout.iter().position(|b| *b == b'\n').unwrap();
    let header: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&output.stdout[..end]).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(header["protocol"], 2);
    assert_eq!(header["data_size"], expected.len());
    assert!(header.get("data").is_none());
    assert_eq!(&output.stdout[end + 1..], expected);
    let error = rpc(serde_json::json!({"protocol":2,"action":"unpack","path":"missing"}));
    assert_eq!(error["protocol"], 2);
    assert_eq!(error["error"]["code"], "io");
}

#[test]
fn explicit_lzma_cli_and_native_container_layers() {
    let temp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let output = temp.path().join("decoded.bin");
    let result = Command::new(binary())
        .args(["unpack", "--format", "LZMA", "-o"])
        .arg(&output)
        .arg(root().join("testdata/payloads/text-lzma.packed"))
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(
        result.status.success(),
        "{}",
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stderr)
    );
    assert_eq!(
        fs::read(output).unwrap(),
        fs::read(root().join("testdata/payloads/text-lzma.unpacked")).unwrap()
    );
    let response = rpc(
        serde_json::json!({"protocol":1,"action":"unpack","path":root().join("testdata/payloads/gzip-xz-upx.packed")}),
    );
    assert_eq!(response["history"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 3);
    assert_eq!(response["history"][2]["packer_name"], "UPX");
}

`tests/compression.rs`:

```rs
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::{fs, path::PathBuf};
use unpack::{Options, compression, detect, unpack, unpack_format};

fn directory() -> PathBuf {
    PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("testdata/payloads")
}
fn fixture(name: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
    fs::read(directory().join(name)).unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn independently_encoded_payloads_and_real_chains() {
    let manifest: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&fixture("manifest.json")).unwrap();
    let samples = manifest["samples"].as_array().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(samples.len(), 20);
    for sample in samples {
        let input = fixture(sample["packed"].as_str().unwrap());
        let expected = fixture(sample["unpacked"].as_str().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(
            format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&input)),
            sample["packed_sha256"].as_str().unwrap()
        );
        assert_eq!(
            format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&expected)),
            sample["unpacked_sha256"].as_str().unwrap()
        );
        let format = sample["format"].as_str().unwrap();
        let explicit = sample["explicit"].as_bool().unwrap();
        if explicit {
            assert!(detect(&input).unwrap().is_none());
        } else {
            assert_eq!(detect(&input).unwrap().unwrap().name, format);
        }
        let result = if explicit {
            unpack_format(&input, &Options::default(), Some(format))
        } else {
            unpack(&input, &Options::default())
        };
        let result = result.unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{}: {e}", sample["name"]));
        assert_eq!(result.data, expected, "{}", sample["name"]);
        let history = result
            .history
            .iter()
            .map(|l| l.packer_name.as_str())
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let expected_history = sample["history"]
            .as_array()
            .unwrap()
            .iter()
            .map(|s| s.as_str().unwrap())
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        assert_eq!(history, expected_history, "{}", sample["name"]);
        assert!(result.warnings.is_empty());
    }
}

#[test]
fn corrupt_truncated_trailing_and_bomb_payloads_are_rejected() {
    for (format, name) in [
        ("GZIP", "text-gzip"),
        ("ZLIB", "text-zlib"),
        ("XZ", "text-xz"),
        ("LZMA", "text-lzma"),
    ] {
        let input = fixture(&format!("{name}.packed"));
        for cut in [0, 1, 2, input.len() / 2, input.len() - 1] {
            assert!(
                compression::unpack(&input[..cut], format, 1 << 20).is_err(),
                "{format} cut {cut}"
            );
        }
        let mut trailing = input.clone();
        trailing.extend_from_slice(b"junk");
        assert!(
            compression::unpack(&trailing, format, 1 << 20).is_err(),
            "{format} trailing"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            compression::unpack(&input, format, 32).unwrap_err().code,
            "limit",
            "{format}"
        );
        if format != "LZMA" {
            let mut corrupt = input.clone();
            let end = corrupt.len() - 1;
            corrupt[end] ^= 0x40;
            assert!(
                compression::unpack(&corrupt, format, 1 << 20).is_err(),
                "{format} checksum"
            );
        }
        assert!(compression::unpack(&input, format, 0).is_err());
    }
}

#[test]
fn dictionary_memory_is_bounded_before_allocation() {
    let mut lzma = fixture("text-lzma.packed");
    lzma[1..5].copy_from_slice(&u32::MAX.to_le_bytes());
    assert!(compression::unpack(&lzma, "LZMA", 1 << 20).is_err());
    let mut xz = fixture("text-xz.packed");
    let header_size = (xz[12] as usize + 1) * 4;
    assert_eq!(&xz[14..16], &[0x21, 1]);
    xz[16] = 40;
    let crc = crc32fast::hash(&xz[12..12 + header_size - 4]);
    xz[12 + header_size - 4..12 + header_size].copy_from_slice(&crc.to_le_bytes());
    let error = compression::unpack(&xz, "XZ", 1 << 20).unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(error.code, "limit");
}

#[test]
fn named_layers_depth_and_filters_are_respected() {
    let input = fixture("gzip-xz-upx.packed");
    let options = Options {
        max_depth: 1,
        ..Options::default()
    };
    let first = unpack(&input, &options).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(first.history.len(), 1);
    assert!(first.data.starts_with(b"\xfd7zXZ\0"));
    let named = unpack_format(&input, &Options::default(), Some("GZIP")).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(named.history.len(), 1);
    let no_formats = Options {
        formats: Some(vec![]),
        ..Options::default()
    };
    assert_eq!(unpack(&input, &no_formats).unwrap().data, input);
    let lzma = fixture("text-lzma.packed");
    assert_eq!(unpack(&lzma, &Options::default()).unwrap().data, lzma);
    assert!(
        unpack_format(
            &fixture("text-gzip.packed"),
            &Options::default(),
            Some("LZMA")
        )
        .is_err()
    );
}

#[test]
fn explicit_payload_decoding_does_not_require_a_valid_inner_executable() {
    use std::io::Write;
    let mut encoder = flate2::write::GzEncoder::new(Vec::new(), flate2::Compression::default());
    encoder.write_all(b"MZ").unwrap();
    let compressed = encoder.finish().unwrap();
    let output = unpack_format(&compressed, &Options::default(), Some("GZIP")).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(output.data, b"MZ");
    assert_eq!(output.history.len(), 1);
    assert!(unpack(&compressed, &Options::default()).is_err());
}

`tests/core.rs`:

```rs
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::{fs, path::PathBuf};
use unpack::{
    Options, Support, detect,
    pe::{Pe, put32},
    unpack,
};

fn root() -> PathBuf {
    PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
}
fn fixture(name: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
    fs::read(root().join("testdata/upx").join(name)).unwrap()
}

fn synthetic(section: &str, marker: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
    let mut data = fixture("pe32.original");
    let pe = Pe::parse(&data).unwrap();
    let stub = pe.sections.last().unwrap();
    data[stub.header..stub.header + 8].fill(0);
    data[stub.header..stub.header + section.len()].copy_from_slice(section.as_bytes());
    put32(&mut data, pe.optional + 16, stub.rva).unwrap();
    put32(&mut data, stub.header + 36, 0xe0000060).unwrap();
    let p = stub.offset as usize;
    data[p] = 0xe9;
    put32(
        &mut data,
        p + 1,
        (i64::from(pe.entry) - i64::from(stub.rva) - 5) as u32,
    )
    .unwrap();
    if section == ".aspack" {
        data[p + 0x4fd..p + 0x503].copy_from_slice(b"\x68\0\0\0\0\xc3");
        put32(&mut data, p + 0x4f4, pe.entry).unwrap();
    }
    let end = data.len();
    data[end - marker.len()..].copy_from_slice(marker.as_bytes());
    data
}

#[test]
fn real_upx_samples_match_official_decoder_and_manifest() {
    let manifest: serde_json::Value =
        serde_json::from_slice(&fs::read(root().join("testdata/upx/manifest.json")).unwrap())
            .unwrap();
    let samples = manifest["samples"].as_array().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(samples.len(), 8);
    assert_eq!(
        samples.iter().find(|s| s["name"] == "pe32-lzma").unwrap()["method"],
        14
    );
    assert_eq!(
        samples.iter().find(|s| s["name"] == "pe32-filter").unwrap()["filter"],
        "0x16"
    );
    for sample in samples {
        let input = fixture(sample["packed"].as_str().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(
            format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&input)),
            sample["packed_sha256"].as_str().unwrap()
        );
        let detection = detect(&input).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(detection.name, "UPX");
        assert_eq!(detection.support, Support::Unpack);
        let result = unpack(&input, &Options::default()).unwrap();
        let golden = fixture(sample["unpacked"].as_str().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(result.data, golden, "{}", sample["name"]);
        assert_eq!(
            format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&result.data)),
            sample["unpacked_sha256"].as_str().unwrap()
        );
        assert_eq!(result.history.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(result.history[0].packer_name, "UPX");
        assert!(result.warnings.is_empty());
        assert!(detect(&result.data).unwrap().is_none());
        let original = fixture(sample["original"].as_str().unwrap());
        assert!(detect(&original).unwrap().is_none());
        assert_eq!(
            unpack(&original, &Options::default()).unwrap().data,
            original
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn all_legacy_recovery_paths_are_explicit_and_preserve_code() {
    for (name, section, marker) in [
        ("ASPack", ".aspack", ""),
        ("FSG", ".packed", "FSG!"),
        ("WinUpack", ".upx0", ""),
        ("Petite", ".petite", ""),
        ("PESpin", ".pespin", ""),
        ("Armadillo", ".EPACK", ""),
        ("Themida", ".themida", ""),
        ("PECompact", ".pec", ""),
        ("NSPack", ".nsp0", ""),
        ("MPRESS", ".MPRESS1", ""),
    ] {
        let input = synthetic(section, marker);
        let detection = detect(&input).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(detection.name, name);
        assert_eq!(detection.support, Support::Recovery);
        assert_eq!(
            unpack(&input, &Options::default()).unwrap_err().code,
            "unsupported"
        );
        let result = unpack(
            &input,
            &Options {
                allow_recovery: true,
                ..Options::default()
            },
        )
        .unwrap();
        let output = Pe::parse(&result.data).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            output.entry,
            Pe::parse(&fixture("pe32.original")).unwrap().entry
        );
        assert_eq!(result.history.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(result.warnings.len(), 1);
        let code = &output.sections[0];
        let range = code.offset as usize..(code.offset + code.size) as usize;
        assert_eq!(&input[range.clone()], &result.data[range]);
    }
}

#[test]
fn new_protectors_are_identified_without_claiming_unpacking() {
    for (name, section) in [
        ("VMProtect", ".vmp0"),
        ("Enigma", ".enigma1"),
        ("MEW", "MEW"),
        ("PELock", ".pelock"),
        ("PEBundle", ".pebundl"),
        ("Yoda", ".yP"),
    ] {
        let input = synthetic(section, "");
        let found = detect(&input).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(found.name, name);
        assert_eq!(found.support, Support::DetectOnly);
        for recover in [false, true] {
            let error = unpack(
                &input,
                &Options {
                    allow_recovery: recover,
                    ..Options::default()
                },
            )
            .unwrap_err();
            assert_eq!(error.code, "unsupported");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn existing_aspack_sample_remains_available_as_limited_recovery() {
    let input = fs::read(root().join("testdata/aspack/aspack_test.exe")).unwrap();
    let found = detect(&input).unwrap().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(found.name, "ASPack");
    assert_eq!(found.version, 1);
    let result = unpack(
        &input,
        &Options {
            allow_recovery: true,
            ..Options::default()
        },
    )
    .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(Pe::parse(&result.data).unwrap().entry, 0x1b808);
    assert!(!result.warnings.is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn malformed_executables_fail_without_panics_or_large_allocations() {
    let original = fixture("pe32.original");
    for length in 0..1024 {
        let _ = detect(&original[..length]);
    }
    let pe = Pe::parse(&original).unwrap();
    for offset in [
        0x3c,
        pe.optional + 60,
        pe.sections[0].header + 12,
        pe.sections[0].header + 16,
        pe.sections[0].header + 20,
    ] {
        let mut corrupt = original.clone();
        put32(&mut corrupt, offset, u32::MAX).unwrap();
        assert!(detect(&corrupt).is_err(), "offset {offset:x}");
    }
    assert!(detect(b"UPX!").unwrap().is_none());
    assert!(detect(b"\x7fELFUPX!").is_err());
    assert!(detect(b"MZUPX!").is_err());
    let mut bad_elf = fixture("elf64.packed");
    bad_elf[32..40].fill(0xff);
    assert!(detect(&bad_elf).is_err());
    assert!(
        unpack(
            &original,
            &Options {
                max_size: 32,
                ..Options::default()
            }
        )
        .is_err()
    );
}

#[test]
fn recovery_rejects_missing_or_unbacked_oep() {
    let mut input = synthetic(".packed", "FSG!");
    let pe = Pe::parse(&input).unwrap();
    let stub = pe.offset(pe.entry).unwrap();
    input[stub..stub + 8].fill(0);
    let error = unpack(
        &input,
        &Options {
            allow_recovery: true,
            ..Options::default()
        },
    )
    .unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(error.code, "unsupported");
}

#[test]
fn registered_format_filter_controls_detection_and_unpacking() {
    let input = fixture("pe32.packed");
    let none = Options {
        formats: Some(vec![]),
        ..Options::default()
    };
    let result = unpack(&input, &none).unwrap();
    assert!(result.history.is_empty());
    assert_eq!(result.data, input);
    assert!(
        unpack::detect_filtered(&input, None, Some(&["FSG".into()]))
            .unwrap()
            .is_none()
    );
    assert!(unpack::detect_format(&input, Some("unknown")).is_err());
}

#[test]
fn owned_input_reuses_its_allocation_for_unpacked_files() {
    let input = fixture("pe32.original");
    let address = input.as_ptr();
    let result = unpack::unpack_owned(input, &Options::default()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(result.data.as_ptr(), address);
    assert!(result.history.is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn recovery_rejects_jumps_into_stub_and_known_packed_storage() {
    for packed_storage in [false, true] {
        let mut input = synthetic(".MPRESS2", "");
        let pe = Pe::parse(&input).unwrap();
        if packed_storage {
            let code = &pe.sections[0];
            input[code.header..code.header + 8].copy_from_slice(b".MPRESS1");
        } else {
            let stub = pe.offset(pe.entry).unwrap();
            put32(&mut input, stub + 1, 27).unwrap(); // E9 targets the same stub section.
        }
        assert_eq!(
            unpack(
                &input,
                &Options {
                    allow_recovery: true,
                    ..Options::default()
                }
            )
            .unwrap_err()
            .code,
            "unsupported"
        );
    }
}
