`tests/macho.rs`:

```rs
use unpack::{detect, macho};

fn write32(data: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u32, little: bool) {
    data[offset..offset + 4].copy_from_slice(&if little {
        value.to_le_bytes()
    } else {
        value.to_be_bytes()
    });
}
fn write64(data: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u64, little: bool) {
    data[offset..offset + 8].copy_from_slice(&if little {
        value.to_le_bytes()
    } else {
        value.to_be_bytes()
    });
}

// Structural fixtures only. Real UPX Mach-O round trips are tested from the manifest in core.rs.
fn thin(little: bool, is64: bool) -> Vec<u8> {
    let mut data = vec![0; 256];
    let header = if is64 { 32 } else { 28 };
    let segment = if is64 { 72 } else { 56 };
    write32(
        &mut data,
        0,
        if is64 { 0xfeedfacf } else { 0xfeedface },
        little,
    );
    write32(&mut data, 4, if is64 { 0x01000007 } else { 7 }, little);
    write32(&mut data, 12, 2, little);
    write32(&mut data, 16, 1, little);
    write32(&mut data, 20, segment, little);
    write32(&mut data, header, if is64 { 0x19 } else { 1 }, little);
    write32(&mut data, header + 4, segment, little);
    data[240..244].copy_from_slice(b"UPX!");
    data
}

fn fat(little: bool, is64: bool) -> Vec<u8> {
    let first = thin(true, true);
    let second = thin(false, false);
    let mut data = vec![0; 8192 + second.len()];
    write32(
        &mut data,
        0,
        if is64 { 0xcafebabf } else { 0xcafebabe },
        little,
    );
    write32(&mut data, 4, 2, little);
    let size = if is64 { 32 } else { 20 };
    for (i, offset, cpu, slice) in [(0, 4096, 0x01000007, first), (1, 8192, 7, second)] {
        let entry = 8 + i * size;
        write32(&mut data, entry, cpu, little);
        if is64 {
            write64(&mut data, entry + 8, offset as u64, little);
            write64(&mut data, entry + 16, slice.len() as u64, little);
            write32(&mut data, entry + 24, 12, little);
        } else {
            write32(&mut data, entry + 8, offset as u32, little);
            write32(&mut data, entry + 12, slice.len() as u32, little);
            write32(&mut data, entry + 16, 12, little);
        }
        data[offset..offset + slice.len()].copy_from_slice(&slice);
    }
    data
}

#[test]
fn thin_and_universal_headers_cover_both_bitnesses_and_byte_orders() {
    for little in [false, true] {
        for is64 in [false, true] {
            for data in [thin(little, is64), fat(little, is64)] {
                assert!(macho::has_magic(&data));
                macho::validate(&data).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(detect(&data).unwrap().unwrap().name, "UPX");
            }
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn malformed_macho_bounds_and_universal_slices_are_rejected() {
    let valid = thin(true, true);
    for end in 0..104 {
        assert!(macho::validate(&valid[..end]).is_err());
    }
    for offset in [16, 20, 36, 80] {
        let mut invalid = valid.clone();
        write32(&mut invalid, offset, u32::MAX, true);
        assert!(macho::validate(&invalid).is_err(), "offset {offset}");
    }
    let mut invalid_segment = valid.clone();
    write64(&mut invalid_segment, 72, u64::MAX, true);
    write64(&mut invalid_segment, 80, 1, true);
    assert!(macho::validate(&invalid_segment).is_err());
    for (offset, value) in [(4, 33), (16, 0), (20, 0), (24, 32), (8, 7)] {
        let mut invalid = fat(false, false);
        write32(&mut invalid, offset, value, false);
        assert!(macho::validate(&invalid).is_err(), "fat field {offset}");
    }
    let mut overlapping = fat(false, false);
    write32(&mut overlapping, 36, 4096, false);
    assert!(macho::validate(&overlapping).is_err());
}

#[test]
fn java_class_magic_is_not_misclassified_as_universal_macho() {
    let class = b"\xca\xfe\xba\xbe\x00\x00\x00\x34\x00\x01";
    assert!(!macho::has_magic(class));
    assert!(detect(class).unwrap().is_none());
}
```

`tests/real_versions.rs`:

```rs
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::{collections::BTreeSet, fs, path::PathBuf};
use unpack::{Options, Support, detect, pe::Pe, unpack};

#[test]
fn real_packer_versions_are_recognized_and_unsupported_recovery_is_explicit() {
    let root = PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("testdata/retdec");
    let manifest: serde_json::Value =
        serde_json::from_slice(&fs::read(root.join("manifest.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
    let samples = manifest["samples"].as_array().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(samples.len(), 16);
    let mut versions = BTreeSet::new();
    for sample in samples {
        let name = sample["input"].as_str().unwrap();
        let input = fs::read(root.join(name)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&input)), sample["sha256"]);
        let found = detect(&input).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(found.name, sample["family"], "{name}");
        assert_eq!(
            found.version,
            sample["compatibility_id"].as_u64().unwrap() as u32,
            "{name}"
        );
        assert_eq!(found.support, Support::Recovery);
        for recovery in [false, true] {
            let error = unpack(
                &input,
                &Options {
                    allow_recovery: recovery,
                    ..Options::default()
                },
            )
            .unwrap_err();
            assert_eq!(error.code, "unsupported", "{name}: {error}");
            if recovery {
                assert!(
                    error.message.contains("no verifiable original entry point"),
                    "{name}: {error}"
                );
            }
        }
        versions.insert((
            found.name,
            sample["vendor_version"].as_str().unwrap().to_string(),
        ));
    }
    assert_eq!(versions.len(), 6); // ASPack 2.12, FSG 1.33, MPRESS 1.05/1.27/2.05/2.19.
    let original = &manifest["original"];
    let input = fs::read(root.join(original["path"].as_str().unwrap())).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&input)), original["sha256"]);
    assert_eq!(Pe::parse(&input).unwrap().entry, 0x3000);
}
```

`tests/versions.rs`:

```rs
use serde::Deserialize;
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::{fs, path::PathBuf};
use unpack::{
    Options, Support, detect,
    pe::{Pe, u32le},
    unpack,
};

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct Case {
    name: String,
    family: String,
    version: u32,
    profile: String,
    input: String,
    input_sha256: String,
    detected: bool,
    detect_error: Option<String>,
    recovery_error: Option<String>,
    expected_oep: Option<u32>,
    pe64: bool,
    output: Option<String>,
    output_sha256: Option<String>,
    tags: Vec<String>,
}
#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct Manifest {
    cases: Vec<Case>,
}

fn verify(c: &Case) {
    let root = PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("testdata/pe-versions");
    let input = fs::read(root.join(&c.input)).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&input)),
        c.input_sha256,
        "{}",
        c.name
    );
    let detection = detect(&input);
    if let Some(code) = &c.detect_error {
        assert_eq!(&detection.unwrap_err().code, code, "{} detection", c.name);
    } else {
        let found = detection.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(found.is_some(), c.detected, "{} detection", c.name);
        if let Some(found) = found {
            assert_eq!(found.name, c.family, "{} family", c.name);
            assert_eq!(found.version, c.version, "{} version", c.name);
        }
    }
    let options = Options {
        allow_recovery: true,
        ..Options::default()
    };
    let result = unpack(&input, &options);
    if let Some(code) = &c.recovery_error {
        assert_eq!(&result.unwrap_err().code, code, "{} recovery", c.name);
        return;
    }
    let result = result.unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{}: {e}", c.name));
    if !c.detected {
        assert_eq!(result.data, input, "{} unchanged", c.name);
        assert!(result.history.is_empty());
        assert!(unpack::unpack_format(&input, &options, Some(&c.family)).is_err());
        return;
    }
    assert_eq!(
        unpack(&input, &Options::default()).unwrap_err().code,
        "unsupported"
    );
    assert_eq!(result.history.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(result.history[0].version, c.version);
    assert_eq!(result.history[0].support, Support::Recovery);
    assert_eq!(result.warnings.len(), 1);
    let golden = fs::read(root.join(c.output.as_ref().unwrap())).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(result.data, golden, "{} golden", c.name);
    assert_eq!(
        format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&result.data)),
        *c.output_sha256.as_ref().unwrap()
    );
    let before = Pe::parse(&input).unwrap();
    let after = Pe::parse(&result.data).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(after.entry, c.expected_oep.unwrap());
    assert_eq!(after.is_64, c.pe64);
    assert_eq!(u32le(&result.data, after.optional + 64).unwrap(), 0);
    for section in &after.sections {
        if section.name == ".text" {
            assert_eq!(section.flags & 0x60000020, 0x60000020);
        }
    }
    let imports = u32le(&result.data, after.directories + 8).unwrap();
    if c.tags.iter().any(|tag| tag == "no-imports") {
        assert_eq!(imports, 0);
    } else {
        assert_eq!(imports, 0x3000, "{} imports", c.name);
        assert_eq!(u32le(&result.data, after.directories + 12).unwrap(), 40);
    }
    assert_eq!(before.sections.len(), after.sections.len());
}

fn group(family: &str, profile: Option<&str>, count: usize) {
    let manifest: Manifest =
        serde_json::from_slice(include_bytes!("../testdata/pe-versions/manifest.json")).unwrap();
    let cases: Vec<_> = manifest
        .cases
        .iter()
        .filter(|c| c.family == family && profile.is_none_or(|v| c.profile == v))
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(cases.len(), count);
    for c in cases {
        verify(c);
    }
}

#[test]
fn aspack_21_layout() {
    group("ASPack", Some("2.1"), 12);
}
#[test]
fn aspack_212_layout() {
    group("ASPack", Some("2.12"), 23);
}
#[test]
fn aspack_other_layout() {
    group("ASPack", Some("other-0x41f"), 23);
}
#[test]
fn aspack_242_layout() {
    group("ASPack", Some("2.42"), 23);
}
#[test]
fn fsg_versions() {
    group("FSG", None, 4);
}
#[test]
fn winupack_versions() {
    group("WinUpack", None, 4);
}
#[test]
fn mpress_pe32_and_pe64() {
    group("MPRESS", None, 4);
}
#[test]
fn other_pe_families() {
    let manifest: Manifest =
        serde_json::from_slice(include_bytes!("../testdata/pe-versions/manifest.json")).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(manifest.cases.len(), 105);
    for c in &manifest.cases {
        if !["ASPack", "FSG", "WinUpack", "MPRESS"].contains(&c.family.as_str()) {
            verify(c);
        }
    }
}
```

`unpack.go`:

```go
// Package unpack is a Go SDK for the Rust unpacking engine.
package unpack

//go:generate python3 scripts/build_go_sdk.py

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"sort"
	"sync"
)

type Unpacker interface {
	Name() string
	Detect(path string) int
	Unpack(path string) (io.ReaderAt, error)
}

type UnpackResult struct {
	ReaderAt io.ReaderAt
	History  []LayerInfo
	Warnings []string
}

type LayerInfo struct {
	PackerName string `json:"packer_name"`
	Version    int    `json:"version"`
	Layer      int    `json:"layer"`
	Support    string `json:"support"`
}

type Detection struct {
	Name     string `json:"name"`
	Version  int    `json:"version"`
	Support  string `json:"support"`
	Evidence string `json:"evidence"`
}

// Options is forwarded to Rust. Zero MaxSize uses 256 MiB; zero MaxDepth is unlimited.
type Options struct {
	MaxDepth      int
	MaxSize       int64
	AllowRecovery bool
	Format        string // Explicit built-in format; processes exactly one layer without registration.
}

var ups sync.Map

// RegisterFormat selects a Rust-backed SDK adapter. Go unpacker implementations are unsupported.
// Invalid adapters are reported when calling the SDK, rather than executing their Go methods.
func RegisterFormat(name string, up Unpacker) { ups.Store(name, up) }

func registeredNames() []string {
	names := []string{}
	ups.Range(func(k, v interface{}) bool { names = append(names, k.(string)); return true })
	sort.Strings(names)
	return names
}

func registeredFormats() ([]string, map[string]Unpacker, error) {
	formats := []string{}
	adapters := map[string]Unpacker{}
	for _, name := range registeredNames() {
		value, ok := ups.Load(name)
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		up, ok := value.(Unpacker)
		if !ok {
			return nil, nil, errors.New("invalid SDK adapter")
		}
		rust, ok := up.(interface{ RustFormat() string })
		if !ok {
			return nil, nil, &Error{Code: "unsupported", Message: "Go unpacker implementations were removed; register a Rust-backed SDK adapter"}
		}
		format := rust.RustFormat()
		formats = append(formats, format)
		if _, ok := adapters[format]; !ok {
			adapters[format] = up
		}
	}
	return formats, adapters, nil
}

// Inspect asks Rust to detect all built-in formats, regardless of SDK registration.
func Inspect(path string) (*Detection, error) {
	result, err := callRust(context.Background(), request{Action: "detect", Path: path})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return result.Detection, nil
}

// DetectFormat preserves the legacy no-match-on-error API. All detection runs in Rust.
func DetectFormat(path string) (Unpacker, int) {
	formats, adapters, err := registeredFormats()
	if err != nil || len(formats) == 0 {
		return nil, 0
	}
	result, err := callRust(context.Background(), request{Action: "detect", Path: path, Formats: &formats})
	if err != nil || result.Detection == nil {
		return nil, 0
	}
	return adapters[result.Detection.Name], result.Detection.Version
}

// Unpack keeps legacy recovery opt-in. Rust performs all file reading and recursive unpacking.
func Unpack(path string, maxDepth int) (*UnpackResult, error) {
	return UnpackWithOptions(context.Background(), path, Options{MaxDepth: maxDepth, AllowRecovery: true})
}
func UnpackAll(path string) (*UnpackResult, error) { return Unpack(path, 10) }

func UnpackWithOptions(ctx context.Context, path string, options Options) (*UnpackResult, error) {
	if options.MaxDepth < 0 || options.MaxSize < 0 || options.MaxSize > 1024*1024*1024 {
		return nil, errors.New("invalid depth or size (maximum size is 1 GiB)")
	}
	req := request{Action: "unpack", Path: path, Format: options.Format, MaxDepth: &options.MaxDepth, MaxSize: options.MaxSize, AllowRecovery: options.AllowRecovery}
	if options.Format == "" {
		formats, _, err := registeredFormats()
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		req.Formats = &formats
	}
	result, err := callRust(ctx, req)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &UnpackResult{ReaderAt: bytes.NewReader(result.Data), History: result.History, Warnings: result.Warnings}, nil
}

// RustUnpacker adapts one built-in Rust format to the original SDK driver interface.
type RustUnpacker struct{ Format string }

func (u RustUnpacker) Name() string       { return u.Format }
func (u RustUnpacker) RustFormat() string { return u.Format }
func (u RustUnpacker) Detect(path string) int {
	result, err := callRust(context.Background(), request{Action: "detect", Path: path, Format: u.Format})
	if err != nil || result.Detection == nil {
		return 0
	}
	return result.Detection.Version
}
func (u RustUnpacker) Unpack(path string) (io.ReaderAt, error) {
	result, err := callRust(context.Background(), request{Action: "unpack", Path: path, Format: u.Format, AllowRecovery: true})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return bytes.NewReader(result.Data), nil
}

```

`unpack_test.go`:

```go
package unpack

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"github.com/orca-zhang/unpack/internal/testfixture"
	"io"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"
)

var builtins = []string{"GZIP", "ZLIB", "XZ", "LZMA", "AP32", "UPX", "ASPack", "FSG", "WinUpack", "Petite", "PESpin", "Armadillo", "Themida", "PECompact", "NSPack", "MPRESS", "VMProtect", "Enigma", "MEW", "PELock", "PEBundle", "Yoda"}

func registry(t *testing.T, names []string) {
	t.Helper()
	previous := map[string]Unpacker{}
	ups.Range(func(k, v interface{}) bool { previous[k.(string)] = v.(Unpacker); ups.Delete(k); return true })
	for _, name := range names {
		RegisterFormat(name, RustUnpacker{Format: name})
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		ups.Range(func(k, v interface{}) bool { ups.Delete(k); return true })
		for name, up := range previous {
			RegisterFormat(name, up)
		}
	})
}
func contents(t *testing.T, reader io.ReaderAt) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	data, err := io.ReadAll(io.NewSectionReader(reader, 0, 1<<20))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return data
}
func sample(name string) string { return filepath.Join(testfixture.Root(), "testdata/upx", name) }

func TestGoSDKRealUPX(t *testing.T) {
	registry(t, builtins)
	for _, name := range []string{"pe32", "pe32-lzma", "pe32-filter", "pe64", "elf64", "elf32", "elf-arm64", "macho64"} {
		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
			input := sample(name + ".packed")
			up, version := DetectFormat(input)
			if up == nil || up.Name() != "UPX" || version != 1 {
				t.Fatal("detection failed")
			}
			info, err := Inspect(input)
			if err != nil || info.Support != "unpack" {
				t.Fatalf("Inspect: %v %v", info, err)
			}
			result, err := UnpackAll(input)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			golden, err := os.ReadFile(sample(name + ".unpacked"))
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if !bytes.Equal(contents(t, result.ReaderAt), golden) || len(result.History) != 1 || len(result.Warnings) != 0 {
				t.Fatal("result differs from official decoder")
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestGoSDKRegistrationAndLimits(t *testing.T) {
	registry(t, nil)
	input := sample("pe32.packed")
	result, err := UnpackAll(input)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	before, _ := os.ReadFile(input)
	if len(result.History) != 0 || !bytes.Equal(before, contents(t, result.ReaderAt)) {
		t.Fatal("unregistered driver was used")
	}
	RegisterFormat("UPX", RustUnpacker{Format: "UPX"})
	for _, options := range []Options{{MaxDepth: -1}, {MaxSize: -1}, {MaxSize: 1 << 31}, {MaxSize: 4}} {
		if _, err := UnpackWithOptions(context.Background(), input, options); err == nil {
			t.Fatal("invalid size/depth accepted")
		}
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	cancel()
	if _, err := UnpackWithOptions(ctx, input, Options{}); !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
		t.Fatalf("cancel: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestGoSDKRegistrationAlias(t *testing.T) {
	registry(t, nil)
	RegisterFormat("custom-registration-key", RustUnpacker{Format: "UPX"})
	up, version := DetectFormat(sample("pe32.packed"))
	if up == nil || up.Name() != "UPX" || version != 1 {
		t.Fatal("registration alias lost")
	}
	result, err := UnpackAll(sample("pe32.packed"))
	if err != nil || len(result.History) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("alias unpack: %v %v", result, err)
	}
}

func TestGoSDKWithoutCLI(t *testing.T) {
	registry(t, builtins)
	t.Setenv("UNPACK_RUST_BIN", filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "nonexistent-cli"))
	t.Setenv("PATH", t.TempDir())
	if _, err := UnpackAll("missing.exe"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("missing input accepted")
	}
	path := filepath.Join(testfixture.Root(), "testdata/aplib/tokens.ap32")
	result, err := UnpackAll(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	expected, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(testfixture.Root(), "testdata/aplib/tokens.unpacked"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !bytes.Equal(contents(t, result.ReaderAt), expected) {
		t.Fatal("embedded Rust returned different bytes")
	}
	info, err := Inspect(path)
	if err != nil || info == nil || info.Name != "AP32" {
		t.Fatalf("native detection: %v %v", info, err)
	}
}

type removedPlugin struct{}

func (removedPlugin) Name() string                       { panic("Go plugin executed") }
func (removedPlugin) Detect(string) int                  { panic("Go detector executed") }
func (removedPlugin) Unpack(string) (io.ReaderAt, error) { panic("Go unpacker executed") }

func TestGoImplementationsAreRejected(t *testing.T) {
	registry(t, nil)
	RegisterFormat("removed", removedPlugin{})
	_, err := UnpackAll(sample("pe32.packed"))
	var coreErr *Error
	if !errors.As(err, &coreErr) || coreErr.Code != "unsupported" {
		t.Fatalf("Go plugin accepted: %v", err)
	}
	if up, _ := DetectFormat(sample("pe32.packed")); up != nil {
		t.Fatal("Go detector accepted")
	}
}

func TestGoSDKNativeAP32AndUPXChain(t *testing.T) {
	registry(t, builtins)
	dir := filepath.Join(testfixture.Root(), "testdata/aplib")
	for _, name := range []string{"tokens", "offsets", "pe32", "nested", "upx"} {
		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
			path := filepath.Join(dir, name+".ap32")
			info, err := Inspect(path)
			if err != nil || info.Name != "AP32" || info.Support != "unpack" {
				t.Fatalf("AP32 detection: %v %v", info, err)
			}
			if name != "upx" {
				t.Setenv("UNPACK_UPX", filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "no-backend-needed"))
			}
			result, err := UnpackAll(path)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			goldenPath := filepath.Join(dir, name+".unpacked")
			if name == "nested" {
				goldenPath = filepath.Join(dir, "tokens.unpacked")
			}
			if name == "upx" {
				goldenPath = sample("pe32.unpacked")
			}
			expected, err := os.ReadFile(goldenPath)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if !bytes.Equal(contents(t, result.ReaderAt), expected) {
				t.Fatal("AP32 output differs from independently verified fixture")
			}
			if name == "upx" && (len(result.History) != 2 || result.History[1].PackerName != "UPX") {
				t.Fatal("AP32/UPX history missing")
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestGoSDKPayloads(t *testing.T) {
	registry(t, builtins)
	dir := filepath.Join(testfixture.Root(), "testdata/payloads")
	data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(dir, "manifest.json"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var manifest struct {
		Samples []struct {
			Name, Format, Packed, Unpacked string
			History                        []string
			Explicit                       bool
		}
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &manifest); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(manifest.Samples) != 20 {
		t.Fatal("missing payload fixtures")
	}
	for _, s := range manifest.Samples {
		t.Run(s.Name, func(t *testing.T) {
			input := filepath.Join(dir, s.Packed)
			if s.Name != "gzip-xz-upx" {
				t.Setenv("UNPACK_UPX", filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "no-backend-needed"))
			}
			var reader io.ReaderAt
			if s.Explicit {
				reader, err = (RustUnpacker{Format: s.Format}).Unpack(input)
			} else {
				result, callErr := UnpackAll(input)
				err = callErr
				if err == nil {
					reader = result.ReaderAt
					if len(result.History) != len(s.History) {
						t.Fatal("wrong layer count")
					}
					for i, layer := range result.History {
						if layer.PackerName != s.History[i] {
							t.Fatal("wrong layer history")
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			expected, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(dir, s.Unpacked))
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if !bytes.Equal(contents(t, reader), expected) {
				t.Fatal("payload differs from independent Python codec output")
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestExplicitFormatWithoutRegistration(t *testing.T) {
	registry(t, nil)
	path := filepath.Join(testfixture.Root(), "testdata/payloads/text-lzma.packed")
	result, err := UnpackWithOptions(context.Background(), path, Options{Format: "LZMA"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(result.History) != 1 || result.History[0].PackerName != "LZMA" {
		t.Fatal("explicit format did not record one layer")
	}
	_, err = UnpackWithOptions(context.Background(), path, Options{Format: "unknown"})
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("unknown format accepted")
	}
}

func TestGoSDKVersionMatrix(t *testing.T) {
	registry(t, builtins)
	data, err := os.ReadFile("testdata/pe-versions/manifest.json")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var manifest struct {
		Cases []struct {
			Name, Family, Input string
			Version             int
			Detected            bool
			DetectError         *string `json:"detect_error"`
			RecoveryError       *string `json:"recovery_error"`
			Output              string
		}
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &manifest); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(manifest.Cases) != 105 {
		t.Fatal("version cases missing")
	}
	for _, c := range manifest.Cases {
		t.Run(c.Name, func(t *testing.T) {
			input := filepath.Join("testdata/pe-versions", c.Input)
			info, err := Inspect(input)
			check := func(err error, code string) {
				t.Helper()
				var coreErr *Error
				if !errors.As(err, &coreErr) || coreErr.Code != code {
					t.Fatalf("want %s, got %v", code, err)
				}
			}
			if c.DetectError != nil {
				check(err, *c.DetectError)
			} else {
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
				if (info != nil) != c.Detected {
					t.Fatalf("detection: %v", info)
				}
				if info != nil && (info.Name != c.Family || info.Version != c.Version) {
					t.Fatalf("version: %+v", info)
				}
			}
			result, err := UnpackAll(input)
			if c.RecoveryError != nil {
				check(err, *c.RecoveryError)
				return
			}
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			golden := input
			if c.Output != "" {
				golden = filepath.Join("testdata/pe-versions", c.Output)
			}
			want, err := os.ReadFile(golden)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if !bytes.Equal(contents(t, result.ReaderAt), want) {
				t.Fatal("native SDK differs from golden")
			}
			if c.Detected {
				if len(result.History) != 1 || result.History[0].Version != c.Version || len(result.Warnings) != 1 {
					t.Fatalf("metadata: %+v", result)
				}
				_, err = UnpackWithOptions(context.Background(), input, Options{})
				check(err, "unsupported")
			} else if len(result.History) != 0 {
				t.Fatal("undetected input changed")
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestGoSDKRealPackerVersionErrors(t *testing.T) {
	registry(t, builtins)
	data, err := os.ReadFile("testdata/retdec/manifest.json")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var manifest struct {
		Samples []struct {
			Input, Family string
			ID            int `json:"compatibility_id"`
		}
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &manifest); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(manifest.Samples) != 16 {
		t.Fatal("real version samples missing")
	}
	for _, s := range manifest.Samples {
		t.Run(s.Input, func(t *testing.T) {
			path := filepath.Join("testdata/retdec", s.Input)
			up, version := DetectFormat(path)
			if up == nil || up.Name() != s.Family || version != s.ID {
				t.Fatal("real version not recognized")
			}
			_, err := UnpackAll(path)
			var coreErr *Error
			if !errors.As(err, &coreErr) || coreErr.Code != "unsupported" {
				t.Fatalf("unverified recovery accepted: %v", err)
			}
		})
	}
}
```
